local ThemeNames = { "\067\114\105\109\115\111\110", "\077\105\100\110\105\103\104\116", "\086\105\111\108\101\116", "\069\109\101\114\097\108\100" }
local CurrentThemeName = "\067\114\105\109\115\111\110"
local Theme = THEMES[CurrentThemeName]
local ThemeBindings = {}
local GlowStrokes = {}
local GlowTweens = {}
local ExternalConnections = {}
local UserScale = CONFIG.DEFAULT_SCALE

local FlyToggleKey = Enum.KeyCode.F

local function TrackConnection(Connection)
table.insert(ExternalConnections, Connection)
return Connection
end
local function BindTheme(Object, Property, Role)
ThemeBindings[#ThemeBindings + 1] = {
Object = Object,
Property = Property,
Role = Role,
}
local Value = Theme[Role]
if Value ~= nil then
Object[Property] = Value
end
return Object
end
local function ApplyTheme()
Theme = THEMES[CurrentThemeName]
for _, Binding in ipairs(ThemeBindings) do
local Object = Binding.Object
if Object and Object.Parent then
local Value = Theme[Binding.Role]
if Value ~= nil then
pcall(function()
Object[Binding.Property] = Value
end)
end
end
end
for _, Stroke in ipairs(GlowStrokes) do
if Stroke and Stroke.Parent then
Stroke.Color = Theme.Accent
end
end
end
local function AddCorner(Object, Radius)
local Corner = Instance.new("\085\073\067\111\114\110\101\114")
Corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, Radius or 10)
Corner.Parent = Object
return Corner
end
local function AddStroke(Object, Role, Thickness, Transparency, ShouldGlow)
local Stroke = Instance.new("\085\073\083\116\114\111\107\101")
Stroke.Thickness = Thickness or 1
Stroke.Transparency = Transparency or 0
Stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
Stroke.Parent = Object
BindTheme(Stroke, "\067\111\108\111\114", Role or "\065\099\099\101\110\116")
if ShouldGlow then
local Index = #GlowStrokes + 1
GlowStrokes[Index] = Stroke
if GlowEnabled then
GlowTweens[Index] = TweenService:Create(
Stroke,
TweenInfo.new(1.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true),
{ Transparency = 0.58 }
)
GlowTweens[Index]:Play()
end
end
return Stroke
end
local function CreateLabel(Parent, Name, Text, Position, Size, Role, Font, TextSize)
local Label = Instance.new("\084\101\120\116\076\097\098\101\108")
Label.Name = Name
Label.Text = Text
Label.Position = Position
Label.Size = Size
Label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
Label.BorderSizePixel = 0
Label.Font = Font or Enum.Font.Gotham
Label.TextSize = TextSize or 14
Label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
Label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
Label.TextWrapped = false
Label.Parent = Parent
BindTheme(Label, "\084\101\120\116\067\111\108\111\114\051", Role or "\084\101\120\116")
return Label
end
local function CreateTween(Object, Duration, Properties, Style, Direction, RepeatCount, Reverses)
local TweenInfoObject = TweenInfo.new(
Duration or 0.2,
Style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quart,
Direction or Enum.EasingDirection.Out,
RepeatCount or 0,
Reverses or false
)
local TweenObject = TweenService:Create(Object, TweenInfoObject, Properties)
TweenObject:Play()
return TweenObject
end
local function CreateButton(Parent, Name, Text, Position, Size)
local Button = Instance.new("\084\101\120\116\066\117\116\116\111\110")
Button.Name = Name
Button.Text = Text
Button.Position = Position
Button.Size = Size
Button.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SurfaceAlt
Button.BorderSizePixel = 0
Button.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
Button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
Button.TextSize = 10
Button.AutoButtonColor = false
Button.Parent = Parent
BindTheme(Button, "\066\097\099\107\103\114\111\117\110\100\067\111\108\111\114\051", "\083\117\114\102\097\099\101\065\108\116")
BindTheme(Button, "\084\101\120\116\067\111\108\111\114\051", "\084\101\120\116")
AddCorner(Button, 8)
AddStroke(Button, "\065\099\099\101\110\116", 1, 0.35)
local Scale = Instance.new("\085\073\083\099\097\108\101")
Scale.Scale = 1
Scale.Parent = Button
Button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
CreateTween(Scale, 0.12, { Scale = 1.035 })
CreateTween(Button, 0.12, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Hover })
end)
Button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
CreateTween(Scale, 0.12, { Scale = 1 })
CreateTween(Button, 0.12, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SurfaceAlt })
end)
Button.Activated:Connect(function()
CreateTween(Scale, 0.07, { Scale = 0.965 })
task.delay(0.08, function()
if Scale.Parent then
CreateTween(Scale, 0.12, { Scale = 1 })
end
end)
end)
return Button
end
local function SetSpriteFrame(ImageObject, FrameNumber, FrameSize, Columns)
local FrameIndex = math.max(1, FrameNumber) - 1
local Column = FrameIndex % Columns
local Row = math.floor(FrameIndex / Columns)
ImageObject.ImageRectSize = FrameSize
ImageObject.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.new(
Column * FrameSize.X,
Row * FrameSize.Y
)
end
local function PlaySpriteSheet(ImageObject, ImageAsset, FrameSize, Columns, FirstFrame, LastFrame, FPS)
ImageObject.Image = ImageAsset
ImageObject.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
SetSpriteFrame(ImageObject, FirstFrame, FrameSize, Columns)
local CurrentFrame = FirstFrame
local Elapsed = 0
local FrameDuration = 1 / math.max(FPS, 1)
local Connection
Connection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(DeltaTime)
if not ImageObject or not ImageObject.Parent then
if Connection then
Connection:Disconnect()
end
return
end
Elapsed += DeltaTime
if Elapsed < FrameDuration then
return
end
local FramesToAdvance = math.floor(Elapsed / FrameDuration)
Elapsed -= FramesToAdvance * FrameDuration
local FrameCount = LastFrame - FirstFrame + 1
if FrameCount <= 0 then
return
end
CurrentFrame = FirstFrame + ((CurrentFrame - FirstFrame + FramesToAdvance) % FrameCount)
SetSpriteFrame(ImageObject, CurrentFrame, FrameSize, Columns)
end)
TrackConnection(Connection)
return Connection
end
local function SetGlow(Enabled)
GlowEnabled = Enabled
for Index, Stroke in ipairs(GlowStrokes) do
local PreviousTween = GlowTweens[Index]
if PreviousTween then
PreviousTween:Cancel()
GlowTweens[Index] = nil
end
if Stroke and Stroke.Parent then
Stroke.Color = Theme.Accent
if Enabled then
Stroke.Transparency = 0.12
GlowTweens[Index] = TweenService:Create(
Stroke,
TweenInfo.new(1.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true),
{ Transparency = 0.58 }
)
GlowTweens[Index]:Play()
else
Stroke.Transparency = 0.12
end
end
end
end

local function UpdateScale()
local Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
if not Camera then
return
end
local Viewport = Camera.ViewportSize
local FitX = (Viewport.X - 24) / CONFIG.WIDTH
local FitY = (Viewport.Y - 24) / CONFIG.HEIGHT
local FitScale = math.min(FitX, FitY, UserScale)
RootScale.Scale = math.clamp(FitScale, CONFIG.MIN_SCALE, CONFIG.MAX_SCALE)
end
local function ConnectCamera()
local Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
if Camera then
TrackConnection(Camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("\086\105\101\119\112\111\114\116\083\105\122\101"):Connect(UpdateScale))
UpdateScale()
end
end
TrackConnection(Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("\067\117\114\114\101\110\116\067\097\109\101\114\097"):Connect(ConnectCamera))
ConnectCamera()

local function ConnectWindowIconEffects(Button, ScaleObject)
Button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
CreateTween(ScaleObject, 0.13, { Scale = 1.09 })
Button.ImageTransparency = 0.04
end)
Button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
CreateTween(ScaleObject, 0.13, { Scale = 1 })
Button.ImageTransparency = 0
end)
Button.Activated:Connect(function()
CreateTween(ScaleObject, 0.08, { Scale = 0.92 })
task.delay(0.09, function()
if ScaleObject.Parent then
CreateTween(ScaleObject, 0.12, { Scale = 1 })
end
end)
end)
end
local function ConnectRestoreTextEffects(Button, ScaleObject)
Button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
CreateTween(ScaleObject, 0.13, { Scale = 1.09 })
CreateTween(Button, 0.13, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Hover })
end)
Button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
CreateTween(ScaleObject, 0.13, { Scale = 1 })
CreateTween(Button, 0.13, { BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Sidebar })
end)
Button.Activated:Connect(function()
CreateTween(ScaleObject, 0.08, { Scale = 0.92 })
task.delay(0.09, function()
if ScaleObject.Parent then
CreateTween(ScaleObject, 0.12, { Scale = 1 })
end
end)
end)
end
ConnectWindowIconEffects(MinimizeButton, MinimizeIconScale)
ConnectRestoreTextEffects(MaximizeButton, MaximizeIconScale)

ToastContainer.Name = "\084\111\097\115\116\067\111\110\116\097\105\110\101\114"

ToastContainer.Position = UDim2.new(1, -22, 0, 22)
ToastContainer.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(310, 90)
ToastContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
ToastContainer.BorderSizePixel = 0
ToastContainer.ZIndex = 500
ToastContainer.Parent = ScreenGui
local function Notify(TitleText, Message, IsError)
local Toast = Instance.new("\067\097\110\118\097\115\071\114\111\117\112")
Toast.Name = "\084\111\097\115\116"
Toast.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(25, 0)
Toast.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(300, 72)
Toast.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Surface
Toast.BackgroundTransparency = 0.03
Toast.BorderSizePixel = 0
Toast.GroupTransparency = 1
Toast.ZIndex = 501
Toast.Parent = ToastContainer
BindTheme(Toast, "\066\097\099\107\103\114\111\117\110\100\067\111\108\111\114\051", "\083\117\114\102\097\099\101")
AddCorner(Toast, 10)
AddStroke(Toast, "\065\099\099\101\110\116", 1.2, 0.1, true)
local Indicator = Instance.new("\070\114\097\109\101")
Indicator.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 12)
Indicator.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 48)
Indicator.BorderSizePixel = 0
Indicator.BackgroundColor3 = IsError and Color3.fromRGB(255, 85, 85) or Theme.Accent
Indicator.Parent = Toast
AddCorner(Indicator, 3)
local ToastTitle = CreateLabel(
Toast,
"\084\111\097\115\116\084\105\116\108\101",
TitleText,
UDim2.fromOffset(14, 10),
UDim2.fromOffset(265, 20),
"\084\101\120\116",
Enum.Font.GothamBold,
12
)
local ToastMessage = CreateLabel(
Toast,
"\084\111\097\115\116\077\101\115\115\097\103\101",
Message,
UDim2.fromOffset(14, 32),
UDim2.fromOffset(270, 28),
"\077\117\116\101\100",
Enum.Font.Gotham,
10
)
ToastMessage.TextWrapped = true
ToastMessage.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
CreateTween(Toast, 0.22, {
GroupTransparency = 0,
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
})
task.delay(3, function()
if Toast.Parent then
CreateTween(Toast, 0.2, {
GroupTransparency = 1,
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 0),
})
task.delay(0.22, function()
if Toast.Parent then
Toast:Destroy()
end
end)
end
end)
end
local function Minimize()
if IsMinimized then return end
IsMinimized = true
Root.Visible = false
MaximizeButton.Visible = true
end
local function Restore()
if not IsMinimized then return end
IsMinimized = false
Root.Visible = true
MaximizeButton.Visible = false
end
MinimizeButton.Activated:Connect(Minimize)
MaximizeButton.Activated:Connect(Restore)
local function GetKeybindName(KeyCode)
if not KeyCode or KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
return "\085\078\066\079\085\078\068"
end
return KeyCode.Name
end
local function ResolveKeyCode(Text)
local Normalized = tostring(Text or "")
:gsub("\094\037\115\043", "")
:gsub("\037\115\043\036", "")
:gsub("\091\037\115\095\037\045\093", "")
if Normalized == "" or string.lower(Normalized) == "\110\111\110\101"
or string.lower(Normalized) == "\117\110\098\111\117\110\100" then
return nil, true
end
for _, KeyCode in ipairs(Enum.KeyCode:GetEnumItems()) do
if KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
and string.lower(KeyCode.Name) == string.lower(Normalized) then
return KeyCode, true
end
end
return nil, false
end
local function RestoreFeatureKeybindEditor(Index)
local TextBox = FeatureKeybindTextBoxes[Index]
if TextBox and TextBox.Parent then
TextBox.Text = GetKeybindName(FeatureKeybinds[Index])
end
local CaptureButton = FeatureKeybindButtons[Index]
if CaptureButton and CaptureButton.Parent then
CaptureButton.Text = "\067\065\080\084\085\082\069"
end
if Index == 5 and FlyBindButton then
FlyBindButton.Text = "\070\076\089\032\075\069\089\058\032" .. GetKeybindName(FeatureKeybinds[5])
end
end
local function SetFeatureKeybind(Index, KeyCode, ShouldNotify)
if KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
KeyCode = nil
end
if KeyCode ~= nil then
if typeof(KeyCode) ~= "\069\110\117\109\073\116\101\109" or KeyCode.EnumType ~= Enum.KeyCode then
RestoreFeatureKeybindEditor(Index)
if ShouldNotify ~= false then
Notify("\073\110\118\097\108\105\100\032\107\101\121\098\105\110\100", "\069\110\116\101\114\032\097\032\118\097\108\105\100\032\107\101\121\098\111\097\114\100\032\107\101\121\032\115\117\099\104\032\097\115\032\070\044\032\071\044\032\076\101\102\116\083\104\105\102\116\032\111\114\032\083\112\097\099\101", true)
end
return false
end
if KeyCode == GuiToggleKey then
RestoreFeatureKeybindEditor(Index)
if ShouldNotify ~= false then
Notify("\075\101\121\032\097\108\114\101\097\100\121\032\097\115\115\105\103\110\101\100", "\084\104\097\116\032\107\101\121\032\105\115\032\099\117\114\114\101\110\116\108\121\032\097\115\115\105\103\110\101\100\032\116\111\032\116\104\101\032\071\085\073\032\116\111\103\103\108\101", true)
end
return false
end
for OtherIndex, ExistingKey in pairs(FeatureKeybinds) do
if OtherIndex ~= Index and ExistingKey == KeyCode then
RestoreFeatureKeybindEditor(Index)
if ShouldNotify ~= false then
Notify("\075\101\121\032\097\108\114\101\097\100\121\032\097\115\115\105\103\110\101\100", "\084\104\097\116\032\107\101\121\032\105\115\032\097\108\114\101\097\100\121\032\097\115\115\105\103\110\101\100\032\116\111\032\097\110\111\116\104\101\114\032\102\101\097\116\117\114\101", true)
end
return false
end
end
end
FeatureKeybinds[Index] = KeyCode
if Index == 5 then
FlyToggleKey = KeyCode or Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
end
RestoreFeatureKeybindEditor(Index)
local TextBox = FeatureKeybindTextBoxes[Index]
if TextBox and TextBox.Parent then
TextBox.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
end
if ShouldNotify ~= false then
local FeatureName = Features and Features[Index] and Features[Index].Name or ("\070\101\097\116\117\114\101\032" .. tostring(Index))
Notify("\075\101\121\098\105\110\100\032\117\112\100\097\116\101\100", FeatureName .. "\058\032" .. GetKeybindName(KeyCode))
end
return true
end
local function ApplyFeatureKeybindText(Index)
local TextBox = FeatureKeybindTextBoxes[Index]
if not TextBox then return false end
local KeyCode, Valid = ResolveKeyCode(TextBox.Text)
if not Valid then
Notify("\073\110\118\097\108\105\100\032\107\101\121\098\105\110\100", "\085\115\101\032\097\032\082\111\098\108\111\120\032\107\101\121\032\110\097\109\101\044\032\102\111\114\032\101\120\097\109\112\108\101\032\070\044\032\071\044\032\076\101\102\116\083\104\105\102\116\032\111\114\032\083\112\097\099\101", true)
RestoreFeatureKeybindEditor(Index)
return false
end
return SetFeatureKeybind(Index, KeyCode, true)
end
local function BeginFeatureKeyCapture(Index)
WaitingForKeybind = "\070\069\065\084\085\082\069\095" .. tostring(Index)
local CaptureButton = FeatureKeybindButtons[Index]
if CaptureButton then
CaptureButton.Text = "\080\082\069\083\083\032\075\069\089\046\046\046"
end
local FeatureName = Features and Features[Index] and Features[Index].Name or "\102\101\097\116\117\114\101"
Notify("\075\101\121\032\099\097\112\116\117\114\101", "\080\114\101\115\115\032\097\032\107\101\121\032\116\111\032\098\105\110\100\032" .. FeatureName .. "\044\032\111\114\032\069\083\067\032\116\111\032\099\097\110\099\101\108")
end
TrackConnection(UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(Input, Processed)
if WaitingForKeybind and Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
local CapturedKey = Input.KeyCode
local FeatureIndex = tonumber(string.match(tostring(WaitingForKeybind), "\094\070\069\065\084\085\082\069\095\040\037\100\043\041\036"))
if CapturedKey == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
WaitingForKeybind = nil
if GuiBindButton then GuiBindButton.Text = "\071\085\073\032\075\069\089\058\032" .. GuiToggleKey.Name end
if FlyBindButton then FlyBindButton.Text = "\070\076\089\032\075\069\089\058\032" .. GetKeybindName(FlyToggleKey) end
if FeatureIndex and FeatureKeybindButtons[FeatureIndex] then
FeatureKeybindButtons[FeatureIndex].Text = "\067\065\080\084\085\082\069"
end
Notify("\075\101\121\098\105\110\100", "\075\101\121\032\099\097\112\116\117\114\101\032\099\097\110\099\101\108\108\101\100")
return
end
if CapturedKey ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
if WaitingForKeybind == "\071\085\073" then
local Conflict = false
for _, ExistingKey in pairs(FeatureKeybinds) do
if ExistingKey == CapturedKey then
Conflict = true
break
end
end
if Conflict then
Notify("\075\101\121\032\097\108\114\101\097\100\121\032\097\115\115\105\103\110\101\100", "\067\104\111\111\115\101\032\097\032\107\101\121\032\116\104\097\116\032\105\115\032\110\111\116\032\097\115\115\105\103\110\101\100\032\116\111\032\097\032\102\101\097\116\117\114\101", true)
else
GuiToggleKey = CapturedKey
if GuiBindButton then GuiBindButton.Text = "\071\085\073\032\075\069\089\058\032" .. GuiToggleKey.Name end
Notify("\075\101\121\098\105\110\100\032\117\112\100\097\116\101\100", "\071\085\073\032\116\111\103\103\108\101\058\032" .. GuiToggleKey.Name)
end
elseif WaitingForKeybind == "\070\076\089" then
SetFeatureKeybind(5, CapturedKey, true)
elseif FeatureIndex then
SetFeatureKeybind(FeatureIndex, CapturedKey, true)
end
WaitingForKeybind = nil
return
end
end
if Processed then return end
if Input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
if Input.KeyCode == GuiToggleKey then
if IsMinimized then
Restore()
else
Minimize()
end
return
end
for FeatureIndex, BoundKey in pairs(FeatureKeybinds) do
if BoundKey and BoundKey ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown and Input.KeyCode == BoundKey then
if SetFeature then
SetFeature(FeatureIndex, not FeatureStates[FeatureIndex])
end
return
end
end
end))
local Dragging = false
local DragStart = nil
local StartPosition = nil
Header.InputBegan:Connect(function(Input)
if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
Dragging = true
DragStart = Input.Position
StartPosition = Root.Position
end
end)
TrackConnection(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(Input)
if not Dragging then return end
if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
local Delta = Input.Position - DragStart
Root.Position = UDim2.new(
StartPosition.X.Scale,
StartPosition.X.Offset + Delta.X,
StartPosition.Y.Scale,
StartPosition.Y.Offset + Delta.Y
)
end
end))
TrackConnection(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(Input)
if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
Dragging = false
end
end))

local function CreatePage(Name)

local PageTransitionToken = 0
local function RefreshSelectedPage(Name)
local Refresh = PageRefreshers[Name]
if Refresh then
local Success, ErrorMessage = pcall(Refresh)
if not Success then
warn("\091\083\052\105\110\116\072\117\098\093\032\080\097\103\101\032\114\101\102\114\101\115\104\032\102\097\105\108\101\100\058", Name, ErrorMessage)
end
end
end
local function SelectPage(Name)
local NewPage = Pages[Name]
if not NewPage then return end
if ActivePageName == Name and NewPage.Visible then
RefreshSelectedPage(Name)
return
end
local SelectedData
local SelectedIndex
for Index, Data in ipairs(Navigation) do
if Data.Name == Name then
SelectedData = Data
SelectedIndex = Index
break
end
end
if not SelectedData then return end
PageTransitionToken += 1
local ThisTransition = PageTransitionToken
for PageName, Page in pairs(Pages) do
if PageName ~= Name then
Page.Visible = false
Page.GroupTransparency = 1
Page.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
end
end
ActivePageName = Name
NewPage.Visible = true
NewPage.GroupTransparency = 1
NewPage.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 0)
for _, Child in ipairs(NewPage:GetChildren()) do
if Child:IsA("\083\099\114\111\108\108\105\110\103\070\114\097\109\101") then
Child.Visible = true
Child.CanvasPosition = Vector2.zero
end
end
CreateTween(NewPage, 0.24, {
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
GroupTransparency = 0,
}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
PageTitle.Text = SelectedData.Title
PageSubtitle.Text = SelectedData.Subtitle
for Index, Button in ipairs(NavigationButtons) do
local IsSelected = Index == SelectedIndex
Button:SetAttribute("\083\101\108\101\099\116\101\100", IsSelected)
CreateTween(Button, 0.18, {
ImageColor3 = IsSelected and Theme.Accent or Theme.Muted,
}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
local Scale = Button:FindFirstChildOfClass("\085\073\083\099\097\108\101")
if Scale and IsSelected then
CreateTween(Scale, 0.12, { Scale = 1.12 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
task.delay(0.13, function()
if Scale.Parent then
CreateTween(Scale, 0.14, { Scale = 1 })
end
end)
end
end
CreateTween(Selection, 0.24, {
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(13, SelectedData.Y - 5),
}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
CreateTween(SelectionScale, 0.11, { Scale = 1.08 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
task.delay(0.12, function()
if SelectionScale.Parent then
CreateTween(SelectionScale, 0.16, { Scale = 1 })
end
end)
local SelectionStroke = Selection:FindFirstChildOfClass("\085\073\083\116\114\111\107\101")
if SelectionStroke then
SelectionStroke.Color = Theme.Accent
end
task.defer(function()
if ThisTransition ~= PageTransitionToken or ActivePageName ~= Name then
return
end
RefreshSelectedPage(Name)
end)
end
for Index, Data in ipairs(Navigation) do
local Button = Instance.new("\073\109\097\103\101\066\117\116\116\111\110")
Button.Name = Data.Name .. "\078\097\118\105\103\097\116\105\111\110\066\117\116\116\111\110"

end

local function CreateGrid(Parent, CellHeight)
local Grid = Instance.new("\085\073\071\114\105\100\076\097\121\111\117\116")
Grid.CellSize = UDim2.new(0.5, -8, 0, CellHeight)
Grid.CellPadding = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 9)
Grid.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
Grid.Parent = Parent
local function UpdateCanvas()
if Parent:IsA("\083\099\114\111\108\108\105\110\103\070\114\097\109\101") then
Parent.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, Grid.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 24)
end
end
Grid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("\065\098\115\111\108\117\116\101\067\111\110\116\101\110\116\083\105\122\101"):Connect(UpdateCanvas)
task.defer(UpdateCanvas)
return Grid
end
local function CreateVerticalLayout(Parent, PaddingValue)
local Layout = Instance.new("\085\073\076\105\115\116\076\097\121\111\117\116")
Layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, PaddingValue or 10)
Layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
Layout.Parent = Parent
if Parent:IsA("\083\099\114\111\108\108\105\110\103\070\114\097\109\101") then
Parent.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.None
local function UpdateCanvas()
Parent.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, Layout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 28)
end
Layout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("\065\098\115\111\108\117\116\101\067\111\110\116\101\110\116\083\105\122\101"):Connect(UpdateCanvas)
task.defer(UpdateCanvas)
end
return Layout
end

{ Name = "\078\111\032\070\111\103", Description = "\082\101\100\117\099\101\032\108\111\099\097\108\032\102\111\103\032\097\110\100\032\097\116\109\111\115\112\104\101\114\101\032\104\097\122\101", Category = "\086\073\083\085\065\076\083" },

local ToggleButtons = {}
local ToggleStatusLabels = {}
local AnimationTokens = {}
for Index = 1, #Features do
FeatureStates[Index] = false
end
local function GetCharacter()
return Player.Character
end
local function GetHumanoid()
local Character = GetCharacter()
return Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100")
end
local OriginalWalkSpeed = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "\107" })
local function ApplySpeed(Enabled)
local Humanoid = GetHumanoid()
if not Humanoid then return end
if OriginalWalkSpeed[Humanoid] == nil then
OriginalWalkSpeed[Humanoid] = Humanoid.WalkSpeed
end
Humanoid.WalkSpeed = Enabled and WalkSpeedValue or OriginalWalkSpeed[Humanoid]
end
local OriginalJump = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "\107" })
local function ApplyJumpBoost(Enabled)
local Humanoid = GetHumanoid()
if not Humanoid then return end
if OriginalJump[Humanoid] == nil then
OriginalJump[Humanoid] = {
UseJumpPower = Humanoid.UseJumpPower,
JumpPower = Humanoid.JumpPower,
JumpHeight = Humanoid.JumpHeight,
}
end
local Original = OriginalJump[Humanoid]
if Enabled then
if Humanoid.UseJumpPower then
Humanoid.JumpPower = JumpPowerValue
else
Humanoid.JumpHeight = JumpPowerValue / 7
end
else
Humanoid.UseJumpPower = Original.UseJumpPower
Humanoid.JumpPower = Original.JumpPower
Humanoid.JumpHeight = Original.JumpHeight
end
end
TrackConnection(UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
if not FeatureStates[3] then return end
local Now = os.clock()
if Now - LastInfiniteJumpAt < InfiniteJumpIntervalValue then
return
end
LastInfiniteJumpAt = Now
local Humanoid = GetHumanoid()
if Humanoid and Humanoid.Health > 0 then
Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
end
end))
local OriginalCollision = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "\107" })
local function ApplyNoclip(Enabled)
if Enabled then
local Character = GetCharacter()
if not Character then return end
for _, Part in ipairs(Character:GetDescendants()) do
if Part:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
if OriginalCollision[Part] == nil then
OriginalCollision[Part] = {
CanCollide = Part.CanCollide,
LocalTransparencyModifier = Part.LocalTransparencyModifier,
}
end
Part.CanCollide = false
Part.LocalTransparencyModifier = NoclipTransparencyValue
end
end
else
for Part, OriginalValue in pairs(OriginalCollision) do
if Part and Part.Parent then
Part.CanCollide = OriginalValue.CanCollide
Part.LocalTransparencyModifier = OriginalValue.LocalTransparencyModifier
end
OriginalCollision[Part] = nil
end
end
end
TrackConnection(RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
if not FeatureStates[4] then return end
local Character = GetCharacter()
if not Character then return end
for _, Part in ipairs(Character:GetDescendants()) do
if Part:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
if OriginalCollision[Part] == nil then
OriginalCollision[Part] = {
CanCollide = Part.CanCollide,
LocalTransparencyModifier = Part.LocalTransparencyModifier,
}
end
Part.CanCollide = false
Part.LocalTransparencyModifier = NoclipTransparencyValue
end
end
end))
local OriginalLighting = nil
local function ApplyFullbright(Enabled)
if Enabled then
if not OriginalLighting then
OriginalLighting = {
Brightness = Lighting.Brightness,
ClockTime = Lighting.ClockTime,
GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
Ambient = Lighting.Ambient,
OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
}
end
Lighting.Brightness = FullbrightBrightnessValue
Lighting.ClockTime = 14
Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
elseif OriginalLighting then
Lighting.Brightness = OriginalLighting.Brightness
Lighting.ClockTime = OriginalLighting.ClockTime
Lighting.GlobalShadows = OriginalLighting.GlobalShadows
Lighting.Ambient = OriginalLighting.Ambient
Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = OriginalLighting.OutdoorAmbient
OriginalLighting = nil
end
end
local ESPObjects = {}
local ESPConnections = {}
local ESPPlayerAddedConnection = nil
local ESPPlayerRemovingConnection = nil
local function RemoveESP(TargetPlayer)
local Data = ESPObjects[TargetPlayer]
if not Data then return end
if Data.Highlight then Data.Highlight:Destroy() end
if Data.Billboard then Data.Billboard:Destroy() end
ESPObjects[TargetPlayer] = nil
end
local function CreateESP(TargetPlayer, Character)
if not FeatureStates[7] or TargetPlayer == Player then return end
task.spawn(function()
local Head = Character:WaitForChild("\072\101\097\100", 5)
if not Head then return end
if not FeatureStates[7] or TargetPlayer.Character ~= Character then return end
RemoveESP(TargetPlayer)
local Highlight = Instance.new("\072\105\103\104\108\105\103\104\116")
Highlight.Name = "\083\052\105\110\116\072\117\098\095\069\083\080\072\105\103\104\108\105\103\104\116"
Highlight.Adornee = Character
Highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
Highlight.FillColor = Theme.Accent
Highlight.FillTransparency = 0.86
Highlight.OutlineColor = Theme.Accent
Highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
Highlight.Parent = Character
local Billboard = Instance.new("\066\105\108\108\098\111\097\114\100\071\117\105")
Billboard.Name = "\083\052\105\110\116\072\117\098\095\069\083\080\078\097\109\101"
Billboard.Adornee = Head
Billboard.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(210, 34)
Billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.7, 0)
Billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
Billboard.LightInfluence = 0
Billboard.MaxDistance = PlayerESPMaxDistanceValue
Billboard.Parent = PlayerGui
local NameLabel = Instance.new("\084\101\120\116\076\097\098\101\108")
NameLabel.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
NameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
NameLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
NameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
NameLabel.Text = TargetPlayer.DisplayName .. "\032\032\040\064" .. TargetPlayer.Name .. "\041"
NameLabel.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
NameLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
NameLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
NameLabel.TextScaled = true
NameLabel.Parent = Billboard
ESPObjects[TargetPlayer] = {
Highlight = Highlight,
Billboard = Billboard,
}
end)
end
local function TrackESPPlayer(TargetPlayer)
if TargetPlayer == Player then return end
if ESPConnections[TargetPlayer] then
ESPConnections[TargetPlayer]:Disconnect()
end
ESPConnections[TargetPlayer] = TargetPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(Character)
RemoveESP(TargetPlayer)
task.wait(0.2)
CreateESP(TargetPlayer, Character)
end)
if TargetPlayer.Character then
CreateESP(TargetPlayer, TargetPlayer.Character)
end
end
local function EnableESP()
for _, TargetPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
TrackESPPlayer(TargetPlayer)
end
ESPPlayerAddedConnection = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(TrackESPPlayer)
ESPPlayerRemovingConnection = Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(TargetPlayer)
RemoveESP(TargetPlayer)
if ESPConnections[TargetPlayer] then
ESPConnections[TargetPlayer]:Disconnect()
ESPConnections[TargetPlayer] = nil
end
end)
end
local function DisableESP()
if ESPPlayerAddedConnection then
ESPPlayerAddedConnection:Disconnect()
ESPPlayerAddedConnection = nil
end
if ESPPlayerRemovingConnection then
ESPPlayerRemovingConnection:Disconnect()
ESPPlayerRemovingConnection = nil
end
for TargetPlayer, Connection in pairs(ESPConnections) do
Connection:Disconnect()
ESPConnections[TargetPlayer] = nil
end
for TargetPlayer in pairs(ESPObjects) do
RemoveESP(TargetPlayer)
end
end
TrackConnection(RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not FeatureStates[7] then return end
local LocalCharacter = Player.Character
local LocalRoot = LocalCharacter and LocalCharacter:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
if not LocalRoot then return end
for TargetPlayer, Data in pairs(ESPObjects) do
local TargetCharacter = TargetPlayer.Character
local TargetRoot = TargetCharacter and TargetCharacter:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
local InRange = TargetRoot ~= nil
and (TargetRoot.Position - LocalRoot.Position).Magnitude <= PlayerESPMaxDistanceValue
if Data.Highlight then
Data.Highlight.Enabled = InRange
end
if Data.Billboard then
Data.Billboard.Enabled = InRange
Data.Billboard.MaxDistance = PlayerESPMaxDistanceValue
end
end
end))
local OriginalFOV = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "\107" })
local function ApplyFOV(Enabled)
local Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
if not Camera then return end
if Enabled then
if OriginalFOV[Camera] == nil then
OriginalFOV[Camera] = Camera.FieldOfView
end
Camera.FieldOfView = FieldOfViewValue
else
for SavedCamera, OriginalValue in pairs(OriginalFOV) do
if SavedCamera then
SavedCamera.FieldOfView = OriginalValue
end
OriginalFOV[SavedCamera] = nil
end
end
end
TrackConnection(Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("\067\117\114\114\101\110\116\067\097\109\101\114\097"):Connect(function()
if FeatureStates[8] then
ApplyFOV(true)
end
end))
local OriginalFog = nil
local OriginalAtmosphere = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "\107" })
local function ApplyNoFog(Enabled)
if Enabled then
if not OriginalFog then
OriginalFog = {
FogStart = Lighting.FogStart,
FogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd,
}
end
Lighting.FogStart = 0
Lighting.FogEnd = NoFogDistanceValue
for _, Object in ipairs(Lighting:GetDescendants()) do
if Object:IsA("\065\116\109\111\115\112\104\101\114\101") then
if OriginalAtmosphere[Object] == nil then
OriginalAtmosphere[Object] = {
Density = Object.Density,
Haze = Object.Haze,
}
end
Object.Density = 0
Object.Haze = 0
end
end
else
if OriginalFog then
Lighting.FogStart = OriginalFog.FogStart
Lighting.FogEnd = OriginalFog.FogEnd
OriginalFog = nil
end
for Object, OriginalValue in pairs(OriginalAtmosphere) do
if Object and Object.Parent then
Object.Density = OriginalValue.Density
Object.Haze = OriginalValue.Haze
end
OriginalAtmosphere[Object] = nil
end
end
end
TrackConnection(Lighting.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(Object)
if FeatureStates[9] and Object:IsA("\065\116\109\111\115\112\104\101\114\101") then
OriginalAtmosphere[Object] = {
Density = Object.Density,
Haze = Object.Haze,
}
Object.Density = 0
Object.Haze = 0
end
end))
local FlySpeedValue = FlySpeedValue or 60
local FlyConnection = nil
local FlyBodyVelocity = nil
local FlyBodyGyro = nil
local FlyRootPart = nil
local FlyHumanoid = nil
local FlyOriginalAutoRotate = nil
local FlyOriginalPlatformStand = nil
local function StopFly()
if FlyConnection then
FlyConnection:Disconnect()
FlyConnection = nil
end
if FlyBodyVelocity then
FlyBodyVelocity:Destroy()
FlyBodyVelocity = nil
end
if FlyBodyGyro then
FlyBodyGyro:Destroy()
FlyBodyGyro = nil
end
if FlyRootPart and FlyRootPart.Parent then
FlyRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
FlyRootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
end
if FlyHumanoid and FlyHumanoid.Parent then
if FlyOriginalAutoRotate ~= nil then
FlyHumanoid.AutoRotate = FlyOriginalAutoRotate
end
if FlyOriginalPlatformStand ~= nil then
FlyHumanoid.PlatformStand = FlyOriginalPlatformStand
end
FlyHumanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
end
FlyRootPart = nil
FlyHumanoid = nil
FlyOriginalAutoRotate = nil
FlyOriginalPlatformStand = nil
end
local function ApplyFly(Enabled)
if not Enabled then
StopFly()
return
end
if FlyConnection then
return
end
local Character = GetCharacter()
local Humanoid = GetHumanoid()
local RootPart = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
if not Character or not Humanoid or not RootPart then
error("\067\104\097\114\097\099\116\101\114\032\105\115\032\110\111\116\032\114\101\097\100\121\032\102\111\114\032\070\108\121")
end
FlyRootPart = RootPart
FlyHumanoid = Humanoid
FlyOriginalAutoRotate = Humanoid.AutoRotate
FlyOriginalPlatformStand = Humanoid.PlatformStand
FlyBodyVelocity = Instance.new("\066\111\100\121\086\101\108\111\099\105\116\121")
FlyBodyVelocity.Name = "\083\052\105\110\116\072\117\098\095\070\108\121\086\101\108\111\099\105\116\121"
FlyBodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
FlyBodyVelocity.P = 12500
FlyBodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
FlyBodyVelocity.Parent = RootPart
FlyBodyGyro = Instance.new("\066\111\100\121\071\121\114\111")
FlyBodyGyro.Name = "\083\052\105\110\116\072\117\098\095\070\108\121\071\121\114\111"
FlyBodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
FlyBodyGyro.P = 12000
FlyBodyGyro.D = 800
FlyBodyGyro.CFrame = Workspace.CurrentCamera and Workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame or RootPart.CFrame
FlyBodyGyro.Parent = RootPart
Humanoid.AutoRotate = false
Humanoid.PlatformStand = true
Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
FlyConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if not FlyRootPart or not FlyRootPart.Parent or not FlyHumanoid or not FlyHumanoid.Parent then
StopFly()
return
end
local Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
if not Camera then
FlyBodyVelocity.Velocity = Vector3.zero
return
end
local MoveDirection = Vector3.zero
local Forward = Camera.CFrame.LookVector
local Right = Camera.CFrame.RightVector
if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then
MoveDirection += Forward
end
if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then
MoveDirection -= Forward
end
if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then
MoveDirection += Right
end
if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then
MoveDirection -= Right
end
if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
MoveDirection += Vector3.yAxis
end
if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl)
or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.C) then
MoveDirection -= Vector3.yAxis
end
if MoveDirection.Magnitude > 1 then
MoveDirection = MoveDirection.Unit
end
FlyBodyVelocity.Velocity = MoveDirection * FlySpeedValue
local FlatLook = Vector3.new(Forward.X, 0, Forward.Z)
if FlatLook.Magnitude > 0.001 then
FlyBodyGyro.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(
FlyRootPart.Position,
FlyRootPart.Position + FlatLook.Unit
)
end
end)
end
local FeatureActions = {
[1] = { Enable = function() ApplySpeed(true) end, Disable = function() ApplySpeed(false) end },
[2] = { Enable = function() ApplyJumpBoost(true) end, Disable = function() ApplyJumpBoost(false) end },
[3] = { Enable = function() end, Disable = function() end },
[4] = { Enable = function() ApplyNoclip(true) end, Disable = function() ApplyNoclip(false) end },
[5] = { Enable = function() ApplyFly(true) end, Disable = function() ApplyFly(false) end },
[6] = { Enable = function() ApplyFullbright(true) end, Disable = function() ApplyFullbright(false) end },
[7] = { Enable = function() EnableESP() end, Disable = function() DisableESP() end },
[8] = { Enable = function() ApplyFOV(true) end, Disable = function() ApplyFOV(false) end },
[9] = { Enable = function() ApplyNoFog(true) end, Disable = function() ApplyNoFog(false) end },
}
local function UpdateToggleVisual(Index, Enabled, Animate)
local Button = ToggleButtons[Index]
local Status = ToggleStatusLabels[Index]
FeatureStates[Index] = Enabled
AnimationTokens[Index] = (AnimationTokens[Index] or 0) + 1
local Token = AnimationTokens[Index]
if Button then
Button:SetAttribute("\069\110\097\098\108\101\100", Enabled)
if Animate then
Button.Image = CONFIG.TRANSITION_IMAGE
task.delay(CONFIG.TOGGLE_TRANSITION_TIME, function()
if not Button.Parent or AnimationTokens[Index] ~= Token then return end
Button.Image = Enabled and CONFIG.ON_IMAGE or CONFIG.OFF_IMAGE
end)
else
Button.Image = Enabled and CONFIG.ON_IMAGE or CONFIG.OFF_IMAGE
end
end
if Status then
Status.Text = Enabled and "\079\078" or "\079\070\070"
Status.TextColor3 = Enabled and Theme.Accent or Theme.Muted
end
end
local FeatureValueBoxes = {}
local function FormatFeatureValue(Value)
if math.abs(Value - math.floor(Value)) < 0.0001 then
return tostring(math.floor(Value))
end
return string.format("\037\046\050\102", Value):gsub("\048\043\036", ""):gsub("\037\046\036", "")
end
local FeatureValueSpecs = {
[1] = {
Label = "\087\065\076\075\032\083\080\069\069\068", Min = 16, Max = 200, Step = 1,
Get = function() return WalkSpeedValue end,
Set = function(Value)
WalkSpeedValue = Value
if FeatureStates[1] then ApplySpeed(true) end
end,
},
[2] = {
Label = "\074\085\077\080\032\080\079\087\069\082", Min = 20, Max = 200, Step = 1,
Get = function() return JumpPowerValue end,
Set = function(Value)
JumpPowerValue = Value
if FeatureStates[2] then ApplyJumpBoost(true) end
end,
},
[3] = {
Label = "\074\085\077\080\032\068\069\076\065\089", Min = 0, Max = 1, Step = 0.05,
Get = function() return InfiniteJumpIntervalValue end,
Set = function(Value) InfiniteJumpIntervalValue = Value end,
},
[4] = {
Label = "\066\079\068\089\032\065\076\080\072\065", Min = 0, Max = 1, Step = 0.05,
Get = function() return NoclipTransparencyValue end,
Set = function(Value)
NoclipTransparencyValue = Value
if FeatureStates[4] then ApplyNoclip(true) end
end,
},
[5] = {
Label = "\070\076\089\032\083\080\069\069\068", Min = 20, Max = 250, Step = 1,
Get = function() return FlySpeedValue end,
Set = function(Value) FlySpeedValue = Value end,
},
[6] = {
Label = "\066\082\073\071\072\084\078\069\083\083", Min = 1, Max = 10, Step = 0.5,
Get = function() return FullbrightBrightnessValue end,
Set = function(Value)
FullbrightBrightnessValue = Value
if FeatureStates[6] then ApplyFullbright(true) end
end,
},
[7] = {
Label = "\069\083\080\032\082\065\078\071\069", Min = 100, Max = 5000, Step = 50,
Get = function() return PlayerESPMaxDistanceValue end,
Set = function(Value)
PlayerESPMaxDistanceValue = Value
for _, Data in pairs(ESPObjects) do
if Data.Billboard and Data.Billboard.Parent then
Data.Billboard.MaxDistance = Value
end
end
end,
},
[8] = {
Label = "\067\065\077\069\082\065\032\070\079\086", Min = 50, Max = 120, Step = 1,
Get = function() return FieldOfViewValue end,
Set = function(Value)
FieldOfViewValue = Value
if FeatureStates[8] then ApplyFOV(true) end
end,
},
[9] = {
Label = "\070\079\071\032\076\073\077\073\084", Min = 500, Max = 1000000, Step = 500,
Get = function() return NoFogDistanceValue end,
Set = function(Value)
NoFogDistanceValue = Value
if FeatureStates[9] then ApplyNoFog(true) end
end,
},
}
local function ApplyFeatureCustomValue(Index)
local Spec = FeatureValueSpecs[Index]
local TextBox = FeatureValueBoxes[Index]
if not Spec or not TextBox then return false end
local RawValue = tonumber((TextBox.Text or ""):gsub("\044", ""))
if not RawValue or RawValue ~= RawValue or RawValue == math.huge or RawValue == -math.huge then
Notify("\073\110\118\097\108\105\100\032\118\097\108\117\101", "\069\110\116\101\114\032\097\032\118\097\108\105\100\032\110\117\109\098\101\114\032\102\111\114\032" .. Features[Index].Name, true)
TextBox.Text = FormatFeatureValue(Spec.Get())
return false
end
local Value = math.clamp(RawValue, Spec.Min, Spec.Max)
Value = Spec.Min + math.floor((Value - Spec.Min) / Spec.Step + 0.5) * Spec.Step
Value = math.clamp(Value, Spec.Min, Spec.Max)
Value = math.floor(Value * 100 + 0.5) / 100
local Success, ErrorMessage = pcall(function()
Spec.Set(Value)
end)
if not Success then
Notify("\086\097\108\117\101\032\117\112\100\097\116\101\032\102\097\105\108\101\100", tostring(ErrorMessage), true)
TextBox.Text = FormatFeatureValue(Spec.Get())
return false
end
TextBox.Text = FormatFeatureValue(Value)
Notify("\067\117\115\116\111\109\032\118\097\108\117\101\032\117\112\100\097\116\101\100", Spec.Label .. "\032\115\101\116\032\116\111\032" .. FormatFeatureValue(Value))
return true
end
SetFeature = function(Index, Enabled, Silent)
local Action = FeatureActions[Index]
if not Action then return false end
local PreviousState = FeatureStates[Index]
if PreviousState == Enabled then return true end
FeatureStates[Index] = Enabled
local Success, ErrorMessage = pcall(function()
if Enabled then
Action.Enable()
else
Action.Disable()
end
end)
if not Success then
FeatureStates[Index] = PreviousState
warn("\091\083\052\105\110\116\072\117\098\093\032\067\111\117\108\100\032\110\111\116\032\099\104\097\110\103\101\032\102\101\097\116\117\114\101\058", Features[Index].Name, ErrorMessage)
if not Silent then
Notify("\070\101\097\116\117\114\101\032\101\114\114\111\114", Features[Index].Name .. "\032\099\111\117\108\100\032\110\111\116\032\098\101\032\099\104\097\110\103\101\100", true)
end
return false
end
UpdateToggleVisual(Index, Enabled, true)
ToggleChanged:Fire(Index, Features[Index].Name, Enabled)
return true
end
local MiscScroll = CreateScrollPage(MiscPage, "\077\111\118\101\109\101\110\116\086\105\115\117\097\108\115\083\099\114\111\108\108")

local function CreateValueSlider(TitleText, Minimum, Maximum, Step, InitialValue, Callback, Order)
local Card = Instance.new("\070\114\097\109\101")
Card.Name = "\065\100\118\097\110\099\101\100\067\111\110\116\114\111\108\095" .. TitleText:gsub("\037\087", "")
Card.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Surface
Card.BorderSizePixel = 0
Card.LayoutOrder = Order
Card.Parent = MiscScroll
BindTheme(Card, "\066\097\099\107\103\114\111\117\110\100\067\111\108\111\114\051", "\083\117\114\102\097\099\101")
AddCorner(Card, 11)
AddStroke(Card, "\065\099\099\101\110\116", 1, 0.5)
CreateLabel(
Card,
"\067\111\110\116\114\111\108\084\105\116\108\101",
TitleText,
UDim2.fromOffset(14, 10),
UDim2.new(1, -100, 0, 20),
"\084\101\120\116",
Enum.Font.GothamBold,
12
)
local ValueLabel = CreateLabel(
Card,
"\067\111\110\116\114\111\108\086\097\108\117\101",
tostring(InitialValue),
UDim2.new(1, -77, 0, 10),
UDim2.fromOffset(62, 20),
"\065\099\099\101\110\116",
Enum.Font.GothamBold,
12
)
ValueLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
local Track = Instance.new("\070\114\097\109\101")
Track.Name = "\083\108\105\100\101\114\084\114\097\099\107"
Track.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 55)
Track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 6)
Track.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SurfaceAlt
Track.BorderSizePixel = 0
Track.Parent = Card
BindTheme(Track, "\066\097\099\107\103\114\111\117\110\100\067\111\108\111\114\051", "\083\117\114\102\097\099\101\065\108\116")
AddCorner(Track, 4)
local Fill = Instance.new("\070\114\097\109\101")
Fill.Name = "\083\108\105\100\101\114\070\105\108\108"
Fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale((InitialValue - Minimum) / (Maximum - Minimum), 1)
Fill.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
Fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
Fill.Parent = Track
BindTheme(Fill, "\066\097\099\107\103\114\111\117\110\100\067\111\108\111\114\051", "\065\099\099\101\110\116")
AddCorner(Fill, 4)
local Knob = Instance.new("\070\114\097\109\101")
Knob.Name = "\083\108\105\100\101\114\075\110\111\098"
Knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
Knob.Position = UDim2.fromScale((InitialValue - Minimum) / (Maximum - Minimum), 0.5)
Knob.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14)
Knob.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Text
Knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
Knob.Parent = Track
BindTheme(Knob, "\066\097\099\107\103\114\111\117\110\100\067\111\108\111\114\051", "\084\101\120\116")
AddCorner(Knob, 100)
AddStroke(Knob, "\065\099\099\101\110\116", 1.2)
local InputSurface = Instance.new("\084\101\120\116\066\117\116\116\111\110")
InputSurface.Name = "\083\108\105\100\101\114\073\110\112\117\116"
InputSurface.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 43)
InputSurface.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 28)
InputSurface.BackgroundTransparency = 1
InputSurface.Text = ""
InputSurface.AutoButtonColor = false
InputSurface.Parent = Card
InputSurface.ZIndex = 5
local CurrentValue = InitialValue
local IsDragging = false
local function SetFromScreenX(ScreenX)
local Width = Track.AbsoluteSize.X
if Width <= 0 then return end
local Ratio = math.clamp((ScreenX - Track.AbsolutePosition.X) / Width, 0, 1)
local RawValue = Minimum + (Maximum - Minimum) * Ratio
local Value = math.floor((RawValue - Minimum) / Step + 0.5) * Step + Minimum
Value = math.clamp(Value, Minimum, Maximum)
if Value == CurrentValue then return end
CurrentValue = Value
local NewRatio = (Value - Minimum) / (Maximum - Minimum)
Fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(NewRatio, 1)
Knob.Position = UDim2.fromScale(NewRatio, 0.5)
ValueLabel.Text = tostring(Value)
Callback(Value)
end
InputSurface.InputBegan:Connect(function(Input)
if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
IsDragging = true
SetFromScreenX(Input.Position.X)
end
end)
TrackConnection(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(Input)
if not IsDragging then return end
if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
SetFromScreenX(Input.Position.X)
end
end))
TrackConnection(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(Input)
if Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
or Input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
IsDragging = false
end
end))
return Card
end

CreateValueSlider("\074\085\077\080\032\080\079\087\069\082", 20, 150, 1, JumpPowerValue, function(Value)

end, 23)
local ScriptContext = {
Player = Player,
Players = Players,
RunService = RunService,
Lighting = Lighting,
Workspace = Workspace,
ScreenGui = ScreenGui,
Notify = Notify,
GetCharacter = GetCharacter,
GetHumanoid = GetHumanoid,
FeatureStates = FeatureStates,
Features = Features,
SetFeature = function(NameOrIndex, Enabled)
local Index = NameOrIndex
if type(NameOrIndex) == "\115\116\114\105\110\103" then
Index = nil
for FeatureIndex, Data in ipairs(Features) do
if Data.Name == NameOrIndex then
Index = FeatureIndex
break
end
end
end
if type(Index) == "\110\117\109\098\101\114" then
return SetFeature(Index, Enabled)
end
return false
end,
}
local function RunRemoteLoader(URL)
assert(type(URL) == "\115\116\114\105\110\103" and URL ~= "", "\077\105\115\115\105\110\103\032\114\101\109\111\116\101\032\115\099\114\105\112\116\032\085\082\076")
local Compiler = loadstring
assert(type(Compiler) == "\102\117\110\099\116\105\111\110",
"\108\111\097\100\115\116\114\105\110\103\032\105\115\032\117\110\097\118\097\105\108\097\098\108\101\046\032\082\101\109\111\116\101\032\108\111\097\100\101\114\115\032\114\101\113\117\105\114\101\032\097\110\032\101\120\101\099\117\116\111\114\059\032\116\104\101\121\032\100\111\032\110\111\116\032\114\117\110\032\105\110\032\097\032\115\116\097\110\100\097\114\100\032\083\116\117\100\105\111\032\076\111\099\097\108\083\099\114\105\112\116\046")
local FetchSuccess, Source = pcall(function()
return game:HttpGet(URL)
end)
assert(FetchSuccess, "\072\084\084\080\032\102\101\116\099\104\032\102\097\105\108\101\100\058\032" .. tostring(Source))
assert(type(Source) == "\115\116\114\105\110\103" and #Source > 0, "\082\101\109\111\116\101\032\115\111\117\114\099\101\032\119\097\115\032\101\109\112\116\121")
local Chunk, CompileError = Compiler(Source)
assert(type(Chunk) == "\102\117\110\099\116\105\111\110", "\067\111\109\112\105\108\097\116\105\111\110\032\102\097\105\108\101\100\058\032" .. tostring(CompileError))
return Chunk()
end

local Filter = {
Query = "",
Category = "\065\076\076",
Categories = { "\065\076\076" },
OnChanged = nil,
}
local function RefreshCategoryText()
CategoryButton.Text = Filter.Category == "\065\076\076"
and "\065\076\076\032\067\065\084\069\071\079\082\073\069\083"
or string.upper(Filter.Category)
end
function Filter:SetCategories(CategoryValues)
local Unique = { "\065\076\076" }
local Seen = { ALL = true }
for _, Category in ipairs(CategoryValues) do
local Clean = tostring(Category or "\079\084\072\069\082")
if not Seen[Clean] then
Seen[Clean] = true
Unique[#Unique + 1] = Clean
end
end
table.sort(Unique, function(A, B)
if A == "\065\076\076" then return true end
if B == "\065\076\076" then return false end
return A < B
end)
Filter.Categories = Unique
if not table.find(Unique, Filter.Category) then
Filter.Category = "\065\076\076"
end
RefreshCategoryText()
end
SearchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("\084\101\120\116"):Connect(function()
Filter.Query = string.lower(SearchBox.Text)
if Filter.OnChanged then Filter.OnChanged() end
end)
CategoryButton.Activated:Connect(function()
local CurrentIndex = table.find(Filter.Categories, Filter.Category) or 1
local NextIndex = CurrentIndex % #Filter.Categories + 1
Filter.Category = Filter.Categories[NextIndex]
RefreshCategoryText()
if Filter.OnChanged then Filter.OnChanged() end
end)
function Filter:Matches(Entry)
local Category = tostring(Entry.Category or "\079\084\072\069\082")
if self.Category ~= "\065\076\076" and Category ~= self.Category then
return false
end
if self.Query == "" then
return true
end
local SearchText = string.lower(
tostring(Entry.Name or "") .. "\032" ..
tostring(Entry.Description or "") .. "\032" ..
Category
)
return string.find(SearchText, self.Query, 1, true) ~= nil
end
return Filter
end

RenderHub = function()
for _, Child in ipairs(HubItemsHolder:GetChildren()) do
if not Child:IsA("\085\073\076\105\115\116\076\097\121\111\117\116") then
Child:Destroy()
end
end
local Categories = {}
for _, Entry in ipairs(HubScripts) do
Categories[#Categories + 1] = Entry.Category or "\079\084\072\069\082"
end
HubFilter:SetCategories(Categories)
local VisibleCount = 0
for _, Entry in ipairs(HubScripts) do
if HubFilter:Matches(Entry) then
VisibleCount += 1
CreateCatalogCard(
HubItemsHolder,
Entry.Name,
Entry.Description,
Entry.Category,
function()
if Entry.URL then
RunRemoteLoader(Entry.URL)
else
assert(type(Entry.Run) == "\102\117\110\099\116\105\111\110", "\072\117\098\032\101\110\116\114\121\032\110\101\101\100\115\032\085\082\076\032\111\114\032\082\117\110\032\061\032\102\117\110\099\116\105\111\110\040\067\111\110\116\101\120\116\041")
Entry.Run(ScriptContext)
end
end,
VisibleCount
)
end
end
if VisibleCount == 0 then
local Empty = CreateLabel(
HubItemsHolder,
"\078\111\082\101\115\117\108\116\115",
"\078\111\032\115\099\114\105\112\116\115\032\109\097\116\099\104\032\121\111\117\114\032\115\101\097\114\099\104\032\111\114\032\099\097\116\101\103\111\114\121\046",
UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0),
UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 42),
"\077\117\116\101\100",
Enum.Font.Gotham,
11
)
Empty.LayoutOrder = 1
end
end
HubFilter.OnChanged = RenderHub
RefreshHubButton.Activated:Connect(function()
RenderHub()
Notify("\072\117\098\032\114\101\102\114\101\115\104\101\100", "\076\111\099\097\108\032\115\099\114\105\112\116\032\099\097\116\097\108\111\103\032\117\112\100\097\116\101\100")
end)
RenderHub()
local function RunEmbeddedTWR()
local Players = game:GetService("\080\108\097\121\101\114\115")
local UIS = game:GetService("\085\115\101\114\073\110\112\117\116\083\101\114\118\105\099\101")
local RunService = game:GetService("\082\117\110\083\101\114\118\105\099\101")
local TweenService = game:GetService("\084\119\101\101\110\083\101\114\118\105\099\101")
local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
local PlayerGui = Player:WaitForChild("\080\108\097\121\101\114\071\117\105")
if _G.TWRMenuCleanup then
pcall(_G.TWRMenuCleanup)
end
do
local oldGui = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("\083\052\105\110\116\032\084\104\111\115\101\032\119\104\111\032\114\101\109\097\105\110\115")
or PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("\084\087\082\077\101\110\117")
if oldGui then
oldGui:Destroy()
end
local oldFX = workspace:FindFirstChild("\095\095\084\087\082\077\101\110\117\070\088")
if oldFX then
oldFX:Destroy()
end
end

local function addUICorner(object, radius)
local corner = object:FindFirstChildOfClass("\085\073\067\111\114\110\101\114")
if not corner then
corner = Instance.new("\085\073\067\111\114\110\101\114")
corner.Parent = object
end
if radius and radius >= 100 then
corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
else
corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius or 8)
end
return corner
end
local function addUIStroke(object, color, thickness, transparency)
local stroke = object:FindFirstChildOfClass("\085\073\083\116\114\111\107\101")
if not stroke then
stroke = Instance.new("\085\073\083\116\114\111\107\101")
stroke.Parent = object
end
stroke.Color = color or Theme.Accent
stroke.Thickness = thickness or 1
stroke.Transparency = transparency or 0
stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
return stroke
end
local function addButtonHover(button, normalColor, hoverColor)
button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
TweenService:Create(button, TweenInfo.new(0.14, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart), {
BackgroundColor3 = hoverColor or Theme.Hover,
}):Play()
end)
button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
TweenService:Create(button, TweenInfo.new(0.14, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart), {
BackgroundColor3 = normalColor or Theme.Surface,
}):Play()
end)
end

local State = {
LimbEnabled = false,
ESPEnabled = false,
EntityOutlineEnabled = false,
PlayerESPEnabled = false,
ItemsEnabled = false,
NoclipCameraEnabled = false,
Limb = "\072\101\097\100",
SizeMode = "\077\117\108\116\105\112\108\105\101\114",
Multiplier = {
X = Config.DefaultMultiplier,
Y = Config.DefaultMultiplier,
Z = Config.DefaultMultiplier,
},
Studs = {
X = Config.DefaultStuds,
Y = Config.DefaultStuds,
Z = Config.DefaultStuds,
},
Transparency = Config.DefaultTransparency,
ListeningForKey = false,
Destroyed = false,
}
local Connections = {}
local Applied = {}
local ESPEntries = {}
local ESPCount = 0
local EntityOutlines = {}
local EntityOutlineCount = 0
local PlayerESPEntries = {}
local PlayerESPCount = 0
local ItemHighlights = {}
local ItemFolderCache = nil
local ItemFolderNextSearch = 0
local FX = Instance.new("\070\111\108\100\101\114")
FX.Name = "\095\095\084\087\082\077\101\110\117\070\088"
FX.Parent = workspace
local function getTargetLimbs()
return LimbGroups[State.Limb] or { State.Limb }
end
local function getFolder(...)
local current = workspace
for _, name in ipairs({ ... }) do
current = current:FindFirstChild(name)
if not current then
return nil
end
end
return current
end
local function getInfected()
return getFolder("\069\110\116\105\116\105\101\115", "\073\110\102\101\099\116\101\100")
end
local function getItemFolder()
if ItemFolderCache and ItemFolderCache.Parent then
return ItemFolderCache
end
ItemFolderCache = nil
local now = os.clock()
if now < ItemFolderNextSearch then
return nil
end
ItemFolderNextSearch = now + Config.ItemFolderRetry
local found = workspace:FindFirstChild("\073\116\101\109\115", true)
if found and found:IsA("\070\111\108\100\101\114") then
ItemFolderCache = found
return found
end
for _, object in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
if object:IsA("\070\111\108\100\101\114") and object.Name == "\073\116\101\109\115" then
ItemFolderCache = object
return object
end
end
return nil
end
local function getPlayerRoot()
local character = Player.Character
return character and character:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
end
local function collectModels(parent, list)
for _, child in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") then
table.insert(list, child)
elseif child:IsA("\070\111\108\100\101\114") then
collectModels(child, list)
end
end
end
local function isAliveModel(model)
if not model or not model.Parent then
return false
end
local humanoid = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100")
if humanoid then
return humanoid.Health > 0
end
return true
end
local function getModelVisualPart(model)
if not model then
return nil
end
local root = model:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
if root and root:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
return root
end
return model.PrimaryPart
or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116", true)
end
local function getLimbPart(model, limb)
local names = LimbParts[limb]
if not names then
return nil
end
for _, name in ipairs(names) do
local part = model:FindFirstChild(name)
if part and part:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
return part
end
end
return nil
end
local function restorePart(part, original)
if part and part.Parent then
part.Size = original.Size
part.CanCollide = original.CanCollide
part.Transparency = original.Transparency
part.Massless = original.Massless
end
end
local function restoreAllLimbs()
for part, original in pairs(Applied) do
restorePart(part, original)
Applied[part] = nil
end
end
local function applyLimbs()
local desired = {}
local folder = getInfected()
if folder then
local models = {}
collectModels(folder, models)
for _, model in ipairs(models) do
if isAliveModel(model) then
for _, limb in ipairs(getTargetLimbs()) do
local part = getLimbPart(model, limb)
if part then
desired[part] = true
local original = Applied[part]
if not original then
original = {
Size = part.Size,
CanCollide = part.CanCollide,
Transparency = part.Transparency,
Massless = part.Massless,
}
Applied[part] = original
end
local targetSize
if State.SizeMode == "\077\117\108\116\105\112\108\105\101\114" then
targetSize = Vector3.new(
original.Size.X * State.Multiplier.X,
original.Size.Y * State.Multiplier.Y,
original.Size.Z * State.Multiplier.Z
)
else
targetSize = Vector3.new(
State.Studs.X,
State.Studs.Y,
State.Studs.Z
)
end
if (part.Size - targetSize).Magnitude > 0.001 then
part.Size = targetSize
end
part.CanCollide = false
part.Massless = true
part.Transparency = State.Transparency
end
end
end
end
end
for part, original in pairs(Applied) do
if not desired[part] then
restorePart(part, original)
Applied[part] = nil
end
end
end
local function escapeRich(text)
return tostring(text)
:gsub("\038", "\038\097\109\112\059")
:gsub("\060", "\038\108\116\059")
:gsub("\062", "\038\103\116\059")
end
local function destroyESPEntry(entry)
if entry.Highlight then
entry.Highlight:Destroy()
end
if entry.Billboard then
entry.Billboard:Destroy()
end
end
local function clearESP()
for part, entry in pairs(ESPEntries) do
destroyESPEntry(entry)
ESPEntries[part] = nil
end
ESPCount = 0
end
local function createESP(model, part)
local highlight = Instance.new("\072\105\103\104\108\105\103\104\116")
highlight.Name = "\084\087\082\076\105\109\098\079\117\116\108\105\110\101"
highlight.Adornee = part
highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
highlight.FillTransparency = 1
highlight.OutlineColor = Config.ESPColor
highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
highlight.Parent = FX
local billboard = Instance.new("\066\105\108\108\098\111\097\114\100\071\117\105")
billboard.Name = "\084\087\082\076\105\109\098\076\097\098\101\108"
billboard.Adornee = part
billboard.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 66)
billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, part.Size.Y / 2 + 2.5, 0)
billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
billboard.MaxDistance = 5000
billboard.Parent = PlayerGui
local label = Instance.new("\084\101\120\116\076\097\098\101\108")
label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
label.RichText = true
label.Text = ""
label.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
label.TextSize = 11
label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
label.Parent = billboard
return {
Model = model,
Part = part,
Highlight = highlight,
Billboard = billboard,
Label = label,
}
end
local function updateESP()
if not State.ESPEnabled then
return
end
local folder = getInfected()
local root = getPlayerRoot()
if not folder or not root then
clearESP()
return
end
local models = {}
collectModels(folder, models)
local candidates = {}
for _, model in ipairs(models) do
if getModelVisualPart(model) and isAliveModel(model) then
for _, limb in ipairs(getTargetLimbs()) do
local part = getLimbPart(model, limb)
if part then
table.insert(candidates, {
Model = model,
Part = part,
Limb = limb,
Distance = (part.Position - root.Position).Magnitude,
})
end
end
end
end
table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
return a.Distance < b.Distance
end)
local desired = {}
local count = 0
for index, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
if index > Config.MaxESP then
break
end
local part = candidate.Part
desired[part] = true
local entry = ESPEntries[part]
if not entry then
entry = createESP(candidate.Model, part)
ESPEntries[part] = entry
end
count += 1
local size = part.Size
entry.Billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(
0,
size.Y / 2 + 2.5,
0
)
entry.Label.Text = string.format(
'<font color="rgb(255,90,100)">%s</font>\n%s\n%.2f x %.2f x %.2f studs\n%.1f studs away',
escapeRich(candidate.Model.Name),
candidate.Limb,
size.X,
size.Y,
size.Z,
candidate.Distance
)
end
for part, entry in pairs(ESPEntries) do
if not desired[part] then
destroyESPEntry(entry)
ESPEntries[part] = nil
end
end
ESPCount = count
end
local function createEntityOutline(model)
local highlight = Instance.new("\072\105\103\104\108\105\103\104\116")
highlight.Name = "\084\087\082\069\110\116\105\116\121\079\117\116\108\105\110\101"
highlight.Adornee = model
highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
highlight.FillColor = Config.EntityColor
highlight.FillTransparency = 0.85
highlight.OutlineColor = Config.EntityColor
highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
highlight.Parent = FX
return highlight
end
local function clearEntityOutlines()
for model, highlight in pairs(EntityOutlines) do
highlight:Destroy()
EntityOutlines[model] = nil
end
EntityOutlineCount = 0
end
local function updateEntityOutlines()
if not State.EntityOutlineEnabled then
return
end
local folder = getInfected()
local root = getPlayerRoot()
if not folder or not root then
clearEntityOutlines()
return
end
local models = {}
collectModels(folder, models)
local candidates = {}
for _, model in ipairs(models) do
local part = getModelVisualPart(model)
if part and isAliveModel(model) then
table.insert(candidates, {
Model = model,
Distance = (part.Position - root.Position).Magnitude,
})
end
end
table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
return a.Distance < b.Distance
end)
local desired = {}
local count = 0
for index, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
if index > Config.MaxESP then
break
end
desired[candidate.Model] = true
count += 1
if not EntityOutlines[candidate.Model] then
EntityOutlines[candidate.Model] =
createEntityOutline(candidate.Model)
end
end
for model, highlight in pairs(EntityOutlines) do
if not desired[model] then
highlight:Destroy()
EntityOutlines[model] = nil
end
end
EntityOutlineCount = count
end
local function createPlayerESP(target, character)
local highlight = Instance.new("\072\105\103\104\108\105\103\104\116")
highlight.Name = "\084\087\082\080\108\097\121\101\114\079\117\116\108\105\110\101"
highlight.Adornee = character
highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
highlight.FillColor = Config.PlayerESPColor
highlight.FillTransparency = 0.85
highlight.OutlineColor = Config.PlayerESPColor
highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
highlight.Parent = FX
local billboard = Instance.new("\066\105\108\108\098\111\097\114\100\071\117\105")
billboard.Name = "\084\087\082\080\108\097\121\101\114\076\097\098\101\108"
billboard.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(200, 65)
billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
billboard.MaxDistance = 5000
billboard.Adornee =
character:FindFirstChild("\072\101\097\100")
or character:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
billboard.Parent = PlayerGui
local label = Instance.new("\084\101\120\116\076\097\098\101\108")
label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
label.RichText = true
label.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
label.TextSize = 11
label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
label.Parent = billboard
return {
Player = target,
Character = character,
Highlight = highlight,
Billboard = billboard,
Label = label,
}
end
local function destroyPlayerESP(entry)
if entry.Highlight then
entry.Highlight:Destroy()
end
if entry.Billboard then
entry.Billboard:Destroy()
end
end
local function clearPlayerESP()
for target, entry in pairs(PlayerESPEntries) do
destroyPlayerESP(entry)
PlayerESPEntries[target] = nil
end
PlayerESPCount = 0
end
local function updatePlayerESP()
if not State.PlayerESPEnabled then
return
end
local root = getPlayerRoot()
if not root then
clearPlayerESP()
return
end
local candidates = {}
for _, target in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if target ~= Player then
local character = target.Character
local humanoid = character
and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100")
local targetRoot = character
and character:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
local head = character
and character:FindFirstChild("\072\101\097\100")
if character and humanoid and targetRoot
and humanoid.Health > 0 then
table.insert(candidates, {
Player = target,
Character = character,
Humanoid = humanoid,
Root = targetRoot,
Head = head,
Distance = (
targetRoot.Position - root.Position
).Magnitude,
})
end
end
end
table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
return a.Distance < b.Distance
end)
local desired = {}
local count = 0
for index, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
if index > Config.PlayerESPMax then
break
end
local target = candidate.Player
local character = candidate.Character
desired[target] = true
local entry = PlayerESPEntries[target]
if not entry or entry.Character ~= character then
if entry then
destroyPlayerESP(entry)
end
entry = createPlayerESP(target, character)
PlayerESPEntries[target] = entry
end
local distance = candidate.Distance
local health = math.max(0, candidate.Humanoid.Health)
local maxHealth = math.max(
1,
candidate.Humanoid.MaxHealth
)
entry.Billboard.Adornee =
candidate.Head or candidate.Root
entry.Label.Text = string.format(
'<font color="rgb(70,170,255)">%s</font>\n' ..
'HP: %d / %d\n' ..
"\068\105\115\116\097\110\099\101\058\032\037\046\049\102\032\115\116\117\100\115",
escapeRich(target.DisplayName),
health,
maxHealth,
distance
)
count += 1
end
for target, entry in pairs(PlayerESPEntries) do
if not desired[target] or not target.Parent then
destroyPlayerESP(entry)
PlayerESPEntries[target] = nil
end
end
PlayerESPCount = count
end
local function getVisualPart(object)
if object:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
return object
end
if object:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") then
return object.PrimaryPart
or object:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116", true)
end
return nil
end
local function hasModelAncestor(object, stopAt)
local ancestor = object.Parent
while ancestor and ancestor ~= stopAt do
if ancestor:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") then
return true
end
ancestor = ancestor.Parent
end
return false
end
local function getItemObjects()
local result = {}
local folder = getItemFolder()
if not folder then
return result
end
for _, object in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do
if object:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") then
if not hasModelAncestor(object, folder)
and getVisualPart(object) then
table.insert(result, object)
end
elseif object:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
if not hasModelAncestor(object, folder) then
table.insert(result, object)
end
end
end
return result
end
local function createItemHighlight(object)
local highlight = Instance.new("\072\105\103\104\108\105\103\104\116")
highlight.Name = "\084\087\082\073\116\101\109\072\105\103\104\108\105\103\104\116"
highlight.Adornee = object
highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
highlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(65, 180, 255)
highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
highlight.FillTransparency = 0.7
highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0.05
highlight.Parent = FX
return highlight
end
local function clearItems()
for object, highlight in pairs(ItemHighlights) do
highlight:Destroy()
ItemHighlights[object] = nil
end
end
local function updateItems()
if not State.ItemsEnabled then
return
end
local root = getPlayerRoot()
local list = {}
for _, object in ipairs(getItemObjects()) do
local part = getVisualPart(object)
if part then
table.insert(list, {
Object = object,
Distance = root
and (part.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
or 0,
})
end
end
table.sort(list, function(a, b)
return a.Distance < b.Distance
end)
local budget = math.max(
Config.HighlightBudget
- ESPCount
- EntityOutlineCount
- PlayerESPCount,
0
)
local desired = {}
for index, entry in ipairs(list) do
if index > budget then
break
end
desired[entry.Object] = true
if not ItemHighlights[entry.Object] then
ItemHighlights[entry.Object] =
createItemHighlight(entry.Object)
end
end
for object, highlight in pairs(ItemHighlights) do
if not desired[object] or not object.Parent then
highlight:Destroy()
ItemHighlights[object] = nil
end
end
end
local CameraOriginal = nil
local function enableNoclipCamera()
if not CameraOriginal then
CameraOriginal = {
Occlusion = Player.DevCameraOcclusionMode,
MaxZoom = Player.CameraMaxZoomDistance,
}
end
Player.DevCameraOcclusionMode = Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Invisicam
Player.CameraMaxZoomDistance = math.max(
Player.CameraMaxZoomDistance,
Config.NoclipCameraMaxZoom
)
end
local function disableNoclipCamera()
if CameraOriginal then
Player.DevCameraOcclusionMode = CameraOriginal.Occlusion
Player.CameraMaxZoomDistance = CameraOriginal.MaxZoom
CameraOriginal = nil
end
end
local function updateNoclipCamera()
if not State.NoclipCameraEnabled then
return
end
if Player.DevCameraOcclusionMode ~= Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Invisicam then
enableNoclipCamera()
end
end

local ActiveThemeName = "\067\114\105\109\115\111\110"
local ThemeBindings = {}
local ToggleControls = {}
local DropdownControls = {}
local CurrentTab = "\072\105\116\098\111\120"
local ManualScale = 1
local RootScale
local StatusText
local Footer
local KeybindControl
local InfoLabels = {}
local MainGradient
local refreshNavigation
local refreshThemeCards
local configureSizeSliders
local formatKeyName
local function bindTheme(object, property, themeKey)
table.insert(ThemeBindings, {
Object = object,
Property = property,
Key = themeKey,
})
if object and object.Parent then
object[property] = Theme[themeKey]
end
end
local function applyTheme(themeName)
local preset = ThemePresets[themeName]
if not preset then
return
end
ActiveThemeName = themeName
for key, value in pairs(preset) do
Theme[key] = value
end
for index = #ThemeBindings, 1, -1 do
local binding = ThemeBindings[index]
local object = binding.Object
if object and object.Parent then
pcall(function()
if binding.Key == "\083\117\114\102\097\099\101\071\114\097\100\105\101\110\116" and binding.Property == "\067\111\108\111\114" then
object.Color = ColorSequence.new({
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Theme.SurfaceAlt),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Theme.Surface),
})
else
object[binding.Property] = Theme[binding.Key]
end
end)
else
table.remove(ThemeBindings, index)
end
end
if MainGradient then
MainGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Theme.Panel),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Theme.Background),
})
end
if refreshNavigation then
refreshNavigation()
end
for _, control in ipairs(ToggleControls) do
control.RefreshTheme()
end
for _, control in ipairs(DropdownControls) do
control.RefreshTheme()
end
if refreshThemeCards then
refreshThemeCards()
end
if Footer then
Footer.TextColor3 = Theme.Muted
end
end
local function uiCorner(object, radius)
local corner = Instance.new("\085\073\067\111\114\110\101\114")
corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius or 8)
corner.Parent = object
return corner
end
local function uiStroke(object, color, thickness, transparency, themeKey)
local stroke = Instance.new("\085\073\083\116\114\111\107\101")
stroke.Color = color or Theme.Divider
stroke.Thickness = thickness or 1
stroke.Transparency = transparency or 0
stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
stroke.Parent = object
if themeKey then
bindTheme(stroke, "\067\111\108\111\114", themeKey)
end
return stroke
end
local function uiTween(object, duration, style, direction, properties)
local tween = TweenService:Create(
object,
TweenInfo.new(duration or 0.2, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, direction or Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
properties
)
tween:Play()
return tween
end

return label
end

local function updateScale()
local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
if not camera then
return
end
local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
local fitX = (viewport.X - 28) / 660
local fitY = (viewport.Y - 28) / 500
RootScale.Scale = math.clamp(math.min(fitX, fitY, ManualScale), 0.55, 1.08)
end
updateScale()
local CameraScaleConnection
local function connectCameraScale()
if CameraScaleConnection then
CameraScaleConnection:Disconnect()
end
local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
if camera then
CameraScaleConnection = camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("\086\105\101\119\112\111\114\116\083\105\122\101"):Connect(updateScale)
end
updateScale()
end
connectCameraScale()
table.insert(Connections, workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("\067\117\114\114\101\110\116\067\097\109\101\114\097"):Connect(connectCameraScale))

local TabButtons = {}

local ActiveTabSubtitle
refreshNavigation = function()
for _, tabData in ipairs(TabDefinitions) do
local entry = TabButtons[tabData.Name]
if entry then
local selected = CurrentTab == tabData.Name
entry.Button.BackgroundColor3 = selected and Theme.Hover or Theme.Sidebar
entry.Icon.TextColor3 = selected and Theme.Accent or Theme.Muted
entry.Label.TextColor3 = selected and Theme.Text or Theme.Muted
entry.Stroke.Color = selected and Theme.Accent or Theme.Divider
entry.Stroke.Transparency = selected and 0.15 or 0.65
end
end
if ActiveTabSubtitle then
for _, tabData in ipairs(TabDefinitions) do
if tabData.Name == CurrentTab then
ActiveTabSubtitle.Text = tabData.Subtitle
break
end
end
end
end

local TabSubtitle = makeLabel(Main, "\065\099\116\105\118\101\084\097\098\083\117\098\116\105\116\108\101", "\072\073\084\066\079\088\032\067\079\078\070\073\071\085\082\065\084\073\079\078", UDim2.fromOffset(139, 64), UDim2.fromOffset(400, 12), 8, "\077\117\116\101\100")
