local function refreshCurrentPage()
local refresh = PageRefreshers[CurrentTab]
if refresh then
pcall(refresh)
end
end
local PageTransitionToken = 0
local function selectTab(name, instant)
if not Pages[name] or CurrentTab == name then
refreshCurrentPage()
return
end
local previous = Pages[CurrentTab]
local target = Pages[name]
CurrentTab = name
PageTransitionToken += 1
local transitionToken = PageTransitionToken
if previous then
uiTween(previous, 0.13, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.In, {
GroupTransparency = 1,
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-9, 0),
}).Completed:Connect(function()
if transitionToken == PageTransitionToken and previous and previous.Parent then
previous.Visible = false
end
end)
end
target.Visible = true
target.Position = instant and UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) or UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0)
target.GroupTransparency = instant and 0 or 1
if not instant then
uiTween(target, 0.24, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, {
GroupTransparency = 0,
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
})
end
local data
for _, tabData in ipairs(TabDefinitions) do
if tabData.Name == name then
data = tabData
break
end
end
if data then
uiTween(SelectionIndicator, 0.23, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, {
Position = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 37 + ((table.find(TabDefinitions, data) - 1) * 53)),
})
end
refreshNavigation()
refreshCurrentPage()
end
for _, data in ipairs(TabDefinitions) do
TabButtons[data.Name].Button.Activated:Connect(function()
selectTab(data.Name)
end)
end
local function pageCard(parent, name, x, y, width, height)
return makeCard(parent, name, UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), UDim2.fromOffset(width, height))
end
local function smallSectionTitle(parent, text, x, y)
local label = makeLabel(parent, "\083\101\099\116\105\111\110\084\105\116\108\101", text, UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), UDim2.fromOffset(450, 16), 9, "\077\117\116\101\100")
label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
return label
end
local ToggleRefreshers = {}
local function createFeatureToggle(parent, id, title, description, x, y, width, height, initialValue, callback)
local card = pageCard(parent, id .. "\067\097\114\100", x, y, width, height or 66)
local accentBar = Instance.new("\070\114\097\109\101")
accentBar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 12)
accentBar.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, (height or 66) - 24)
accentBar.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
accentBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
accentBar.Parent = card
uiCorner(accentBar, 2)
bindTheme(accentBar, "\066\097\099\107\103\114\111\117\110\100\067\111\108\111\114\051", "\065\099\099\101\110\116")
local titleLabel = makeLabel(card, "\084\105\116\108\101", title, UDim2.fromOffset(12, 9), UDim2.new(1, -82, 0, 19), 11, "\084\101\120\116")
titleLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
local descLabel = makeLabel(card, "\068\101\115\099\114\105\112\116\105\111\110", description, UDim2.fromOffset(12, 30), UDim2.new(1, -82, 0, 18), 8, "\077\117\116\101\100")
local stateLabel = makeLabel(card, "\083\116\097\116\101", "\079\070\070", UDim2.new(1, -65, 0, 9), UDim2.fromOffset(52, 14), 8, "\077\117\116\101\100")
stateLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
stateLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
local track = Instance.new("\084\101\120\116\066\117\116\116\111\110")
track.Name = "\084\111\103\103\108\101"
track.Position = UDim2.new(1, -57, 0.5, -11)
track.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(43, 22)
track.BackgroundColor3 = initialValue and Theme.Accent or Theme.Off
track.BorderSizePixel = 0
track.Text = ""
track.AutoButtonColor = false
track.Parent = card
uiCorner(track, 100)
local trackStroke = uiStroke(track, initialValue and Theme.Accent or Theme.Divider, 1, 0.18, initialValue and "\065\099\099\101\110\116" or "\068\105\118\105\100\101\114")
local knob = Instance.new("\070\114\097\109\101")
knob.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16)
knob.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(initialValue and 24 or 3, 3)
knob.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Text
knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
knob.Parent = track
uiCorner(knob, 100)
bindTheme(knob, "\066\097\099\107\103\114\111\117\110\100\067\111\108\111\114\051", "\084\101\120\116")
uiStroke(knob, Theme.Background, 1, 0.3, "\066\097\099\107\103\114\111\117\110\100")
local enabled = initialValue
local busy = false
local function refreshVisual(animated)
local position = UDim2.fromOffset(enabled and 24 or 3, 3)
local trackColor = enabled and Theme.Accent or Theme.Off
local stateColor = enabled and Theme.Accent or Theme.Muted
if animated then
uiTween(track, 0.19, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, { BackgroundColor3 = trackColor })
uiTween(knob, 0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, { Position = position })
else
track.BackgroundColor3 = trackColor
knob.Position = position
end
trackStroke.Color = enabled and Theme.Accent or Theme.Divider
stateLabel.Text = enabled and "\079\078" or "\079\070\070"
stateLabel.TextColor3 = stateColor
accentBar.BackgroundTransparency = enabled and 0 or 0.55
end
local function setEnabled(value, shouldCall)
local nextEnabled = value == true
if enabled == nextEnabled then
refreshVisual(false)
return
end
enabled = nextEnabled
refreshVisual(shouldCall ~= false)
if shouldCall ~= false then
callback(enabled)
end
end
track.Activated:Connect(function()
if busy then return end
busy = true
setEnabled(not enabled, true)
task.delay(0.24, function()
busy = false
end)
end)
card.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
uiTween(card, 0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, {
BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Hover,
})
end)
card.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
uiTween(card, 0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, {
BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Surface,
})
end)
local control = {
Set = function(value, shouldCall) setEnabled(value, shouldCall) end,
Get = function() return enabled end,
RefreshTheme = function() refreshVisual(false) end,
}
table.insert(ToggleControls, control)
ToggleRefreshers[id] = control
return control
end
local function createDropdown(parent, id, title, options, initialValue, x, y, width, callback)
local card = pageCard(parent, id .. "\068\114\111\112\100\111\119\110\067\097\114\100", x, y, width, 51)
local titleLabel = makeLabel(card, "\084\105\116\108\101", title, UDim2.fromOffset(10, 6), UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 13), 8, "\077\117\116\101\100")
titleLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
local button = Instance.new("\084\101\120\116\066\117\116\116\111\110")
button.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 22)
button.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 22)
button.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.SurfaceAlt
button.BorderSizePixel = 0
button.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
button.TextSize = 9
button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
button.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
button.AutoButtonColor = false
button.Parent = card
uiCorner(button, 6)
bindTheme(button, "\066\097\099\107\103\114\111\117\110\100\067\111\108\111\114\051", "\083\117\114\102\097\099\101\065\108\116")
bindTheme(button, "\084\101\120\116\067\111\108\111\114\051", "\084\101\120\116")
uiStroke(button, Theme.Divider, 1, 0.35, "\068\105\118\105\100\101\114")
local padding = Instance.new("\085\073\080\097\100\100\105\110\103")
padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
padding.Parent = button
local popup = Instance.new("\083\099\114\111\108\108\105\110\103\070\114\097\109\101")
popup.Name = id .. "\079\112\116\105\111\110\115"
popup.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 1, 24)
popup.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, math.min(#options * 24, 144))
popup.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Surface
popup.BorderSizePixel = 0
popup.ScrollBarThickness = 3
popup.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Accent
popup.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, #options * 24)
popup.Visible = false
popup.ZIndex = 50
popup.Parent = card
uiCorner(popup, 7)
bindTheme(popup, "\066\097\099\107\103\114\111\117\110\100\067\111\108\111\114\051", "\083\117\114\102\097\099\101")
uiStroke(popup, Theme.Divider, 1, 0.1, "\068\105\118\105\100\101\114")
local listLayout = Instance.new("\085\073\076\105\115\116\076\097\121\111\117\116")
listLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
listLayout.Parent = popup
local selected = initialValue
local optionButtons = {}
local function refresh()
button.Text = selected .. "\032\032\032\032\032" .. (popup.Visible and "\063" or "\063")
for optionName, optionButton in pairs(optionButtons) do
optionButton.TextColor3 = optionName == selected and Theme.Accent or Theme.Text
optionButton.BackgroundColor3 = optionName == selected and Theme.Hover or Theme.Surface
end
end
for index, optionName in ipairs(options) do
local optionButton = Instance.new("\084\101\120\116\066\117\116\116\111\110")
optionButton.Name = "\079\112\116\105\111\110\095" .. index
optionButton.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 24)
optionButton.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Surface
optionButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0
optionButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
optionButton.Text = optionName
optionButton.TextColor3 = Theme.Text
optionButton.TextSize = 9
optionButton.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
optionButton.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
optionButton.LayoutOrder = index
optionButton.ZIndex = 51
optionButton.Parent = popup
local optionPadding = Instance.new("\085\073\080\097\100\100\105\110\103")
optionPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
optionPadding.Parent = optionButton
optionButtons[optionName] = optionButton
optionButton.Activated:Connect(function()
selected = optionName
popup.Visible = false
refresh()
callback(optionName)
end)
end
button.Activated:Connect(function()
popup.Visible = not popup.Visible
refresh()
end)
refresh()
local control = {
Refresh = refresh,
RefreshTheme = refresh,
Set = function(value, callCallback)
selected = value
refresh()
if callCallback then callback(value) end
end,
}
table.insert(DropdownControls, control)
return control
end
local function createSlider(parent, id, title, x, y, width, minValue, maxValue, defaultValue, step, formatter, callback)
local card = pageCard(parent, id .. "\083\108\105\100\101\114\067\097\114\100", x, y, width, 54)
local titleLabel = makeLabel(card, "\084\105\116\108\101", title, UDim2.fromOffset(10, 6), UDim2.new(1, -80, 0, 14), 9, "\077\117\116\101\100")
titleLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
local valueLabel = makeLabel(card, "\086\097\108\117\101", "", UDim2.new(1, -78, 0, 5), UDim2.fromOffset(68, 15), 9, "\065\099\099\101\110\116")
valueLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
valueLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
local track = Instance.new("\070\114\097\109\101")
track.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 34)
track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 5)
track.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Off
track.BorderSizePixel = 0
track.Parent = card
uiCorner(track, 100)
bindTheme(track, "\066\097\099\107\103\114\111\117\110\100\067\111\108\111\114\051", "\079\102\102")
local fill = Instance.new("\070\114\097\109\101")
fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1)
fill.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Accent
fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
fill.Parent = track
uiCorner(fill, 100)
bindTheme(fill, "\066\097\099\107\103\114\111\117\110\100\067\111\108\111\114\051", "\065\099\099\101\110\116")
local knob = Instance.new("\070\114\097\109\101")
knob.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12)
knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
knob.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Text
knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
knob.Parent = track
uiCorner(knob, 100)
bindTheme(knob, "\066\097\099\107\103\114\111\117\110\100\067\111\108\111\114\051", "\084\101\120\116")
uiStroke(knob, Theme.Accent, 1, 0.08, "\065\099\099\101\110\116")
local hit = Instance.new("\084\101\120\116\066\117\116\116\111\110")
hit.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 25)
hit.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 22)
hit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
hit.Text = ""
hit.Parent = card
local currentMin = minValue
local currentMax = maxValue
local currentStep = step
local currentFormatter = formatter
local currentValue = defaultValue
local dragging = false
local function setValue(value, invoke)
value = math.clamp(math.floor(value / currentStep + 0.5) * currentStep, currentMin, currentMax)
value = math.floor(value * 100 + 0.5) / 100
currentValue = value
local alpha = (value - currentMin) / math.max(currentMax - currentMin, 0.0001)
fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(alpha, 1)
knob.Position = UDim2.new(alpha, 0, 0.5, 0)
valueLabel.Text = currentFormatter(value)
if invoke ~= false then callback(value) end
end
local function updateFromX(xPosition)
local widthNow = hit.AbsoluteSize.X
if widthNow <= 0 then return end
local alpha = math.clamp((xPosition - hit.AbsolutePosition.X) / widthNow, 0, 1)
setValue(currentMin + alpha * (currentMax - currentMin), true)
end
hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging = true
updateFromX(input.Position.X)
end
end)
table.insert(Connections, UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
updateFromX(input.Position.X)
end
end))
table.insert(Connections, UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging = false
end
end))
setValue(defaultValue, false)
return {
Configure = function(newMin, newMax, newValue, newStep, newFormatter)
currentMin = newMin
currentMax = newMax
currentStep = newStep
currentFormatter = newFormatter
setValue(newValue, false)
end,
Get = function() return currentValue end,
}
end

for _, axis in ipairs(Axes) do
if State.SizeMode == "\077\117\108\116\105\112\108\105\101\114" then
SizeSliders[axis].Configure(
Config.MinMultiplier,

State.Multiplier[axis],

formatMultiplier
)
else
SizeSliders[axis].Configure(
Config.MinStuds,
Config.MaxStuds,
State.Studs[axis],
Config.StudsStep,
formatStuds
)
end
end
end

local ThemeTiles = {}

refreshThemeCards = function()
for themeName, tile in pairs(ThemeTiles) do

tile.Stroke.Color = selected and Theme.Accent or Theme.Divider

tile.Card.BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Surface
end
end

local function countActive()
local count = 0
if State.LimbEnabled then count += 1 end
if State.ESPEnabled then count += 1 end
if State.EntityOutlineEnabled then count += 1 end
if State.ItemsEnabled then count += 1 end
if State.PlayerESPEnabled then count += 1 end
if State.NoclipCameraEnabled then count += 1 end
return count
end
local function refreshAllState()
local active = countActive()
local status = tostring(active) .. "\032\047\032\054\032\065\067\084\073\086\069"
SidebarStatus.Text = status
InfoStatus.Text = "\070\069\065\084\085\082\069\083\032\065\067\084\073\086\069\032\032" .. status
if StatusText then
StatusText.Text = status
end
VisualsStatus.Text = string.format(
"\076\073\077\066\032\069\083\080\032\037\100\032\032\226\128\162\032\032\069\078\084\073\084\073\069\083\032\037\100\032\032\226\128\162\032\032\080\076\065\089\069\082\083\032\037\100",
ESPCount,
EntityOutlineCount,
PlayerESPCount
)
if KeybindButton then
KeybindButton.Text = State.ListeningForKey and "\080\082\069\083\083\032\065\032\075\069\089\046\046\046" or formatKeyName(Config.ToggleKey)
KeybindButton.TextColor3 = State.ListeningForKey and Theme.Accent or Theme.Text
end
end

local Dragging = false
local DragStart = Vector2.zero
local FrameStart = Vector2.zero
local function clampToScreen(position)
local viewport = Gui.AbsoluteSize
local size = Main.AbsoluteSize
return Vector2.new(
math.clamp(position.X, 0, math.max(viewport.X - size.X, 0)),
math.clamp(position.Y, 0, math.max(viewport.Y - size.Y, 0))
)
end
Header.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
Dragging = true
DragStart = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
FrameStart = Main.AbsolutePosition
end
end)
table.insert(Connections, UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
if not Dragging then return end
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
local delta = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y) - DragStart
local target = clampToScreen(FrameStart + delta)
local size = Main.AbsoluteSize
Main.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
target.X + (size.X / 2),
target.Y + (size.Y / 2)
)
end
end))
table.insert(Connections, UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
Dragging = false
end
end))

local function setMenuVisible(visible)
if visible then
MaximizeButton.Visible = false
Main.Visible = true
local finalSize = UDim2.fromOffset(660, 500)
Main.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(638, 484)
uiTween(Main, 0.24, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, {
Size = finalSize,
})
else
Main.Visible = false
MaximizeButton.Visible = true
end
end

end)

end
end)
formatKeyName = function(keyCode)
local names = {
[Enum.KeyCode.Down] = "\068\079\087\078",
[Enum.KeyCode.Up] = "\085\080",
[Enum.KeyCode.Left] = "\076\069\070\084",
[Enum.KeyCode.Right] = "\082\073\071\072\084",
[Enum.KeyCode.Space] = "\083\080\065\067\069",
[Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl] = "\076\032\067\084\082\076",
[Enum.KeyCode.RightControl] = "\082\032\067\084\082\076",
[Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift] = "\076\032\083\072\073\070\084",
[Enum.KeyCode.RightShift] = "\082\032\083\072\073\070\084",
}
return names[keyCode] or keyCode.Name:upper()
end
local function updateFooter()
Footer.Text = "\072\073\068\069\032\071\085\073\032\032\047\032\032" .. formatKeyName(Config.ToggleKey)
end
KeybindButton.Text = formatKeyName(Config.ToggleKey)
updateFooter()
local function refreshCurrentTab()
refreshCurrentPage()
refreshAllState()
refreshNavigation()
end
for _, data in ipairs(TabDefinitions) do
TabButtons[data.Name].Button.Activated:Connect(function()
selectTab(data.Name)
end)
end
table.insert(Connections, UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if State.ListeningForKey then
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
if input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Escape and input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
Config.ToggleKey = input.KeyCode
end
State.ListeningForKey = false
refreshAllState()
updateFooter()
end
return
end
if input.KeyCode == Config.ToggleKey and not UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then
setMenuVisible(not Main.Visible)
end
end))
applyTheme("\067\114\105\109\115\111\110")
CurrentTab = "\073\110\102\111"
selectTab("\072\105\116\098\111\120", true)
refreshAllState()
local targetInitialSize = UDim2.fromOffset(660, 500)
Main.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(635, 480)
uiTween(Main, 0.34, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, {
Size = targetInitialSize,
})
task.spawn(function()
while Gui.Parent and not State.Destroyed do
if Main.Visible then
refreshCurrentTab()
end
task.wait(0.75)
end
end)
local LimbTimer = 0
local ESPTimer = 0
local ItemTimer = 0
table.insert(Connections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
if State.Destroyed then
return
end
LimbTimer += dt
if LimbTimer >= Config.ApplyInterval then
LimbTimer = 0
if State.LimbEnabled then
applyLimbs()
end
end
ESPTimer += dt
if ESPTimer >= Config.ESPInterval then
ESPTimer = 0
updateESP()
updateEntityOutlines()
updatePlayerESP()
updateNoclipCamera()
end
ItemTimer += dt
if ItemTimer >= Config.ItemInterval then
ItemTimer = 0
updateItems()
end
end))
local function destroyAll()
if State.Destroyed then
return
end
State.Destroyed = true
Dragging = false
for _, connection in ipairs(Connections) do
pcall(function()
connection:Disconnect()
end)
end
table.clear(Connections)
if CameraScaleConnection then
CameraScaleConnection:Disconnect()
CameraScaleConnection = nil
end
if MainGlowTween then
MainGlowTween:Cancel()
end
disableNoclipCamera()
restoreAllLimbs()
clearESP()
clearEntityOutlines()
clearPlayerESP()
clearItems()
if FX and FX.Parent then
FX:Destroy()
end
if Gui and Gui.Parent then
Gui:Destroy()
end
if _G.TWRMenuCleanup == destroyAll then
_G.TWRMenuCleanup = nil
end
end
_G.TWRMenuCleanup = destroyAll
end

local Players = game:GetService("\080\108\097\121\101\114\115")
local UIS = game:GetService("\085\115\101\114\073\110\112\117\116\083\101\114\118\105\099\101")
local RunService = game:GetService("\082\117\110\083\101\114\118\105\099\101")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local PlayerGui = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("\080\108\097\121\101\114\071\117\105")
local BIND_NAME = "\083\052\105\110\116\095\080\108\097\121\101\114\065\105\109\065\115\115\105\115\116\095\086\051"
pcall(function()
RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(BIND_NAME)
end)
local ExistingGui = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("\083\052\105\110\116\080\108\097\121\101\114\065\105\109\065\115\115\105\115\116")
if ExistingGui then
ExistingGui:Destroy()
end

local Connections = {}

local function Connect(signal, callback)
local connection = signal:Connect(callback)
table.insert(Connections, connection)
return connection
end
local function Create(className, properties, parent)
local object = Instance.new(className)
for property, value in pairs(properties or {}) do
object[property] = value
end
object.Parent = parent
return object
end
local function AddCorner(object, radius)
Create("\085\073\067\111\114\110\101\114", {
CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius or 7),
}, object)
end
local function AddStroke(object, color, thickness)
return Create("\085\073\083\116\114\111\107\101", {
Color = color or Theme.Border,
Thickness = thickness or 1,
Transparency = 0,
}, object)
end
local function CreateLabel(parent, text, position, size, fontSize)
return Create("\084\101\120\116\076\097\098\101\108", {
BackgroundTransparency = 1,
Position = position,
Size = size,
Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
Text = text,
TextColor3 = Theme.White,
TextSize = fontSize or 12,
TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
BorderSizePixel = 0,
TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
}, parent)
end

local function IsTeammate(targetPlayer)
if not targetPlayer or targetPlayer == LocalPlayer then
return false
end
if not LocalPlayer.Team or not targetPlayer.Team then
return false
end
return LocalPlayer.Team == targetPlayer.Team
end
local function CanTargetPlayer(targetPlayer)
if not targetPlayer or targetPlayer == LocalPlayer then
return false
end
if Config.TeamCheck and IsTeammate(targetPlayer) then
return false
end
return true
end
local function RemoveHighlight(targetPlayer)
local highlight = Highlights[targetPlayer]
if highlight then
highlight:Destroy()
Highlights[targetPlayer] = nil
end
end
local function UpdateHighlight(targetPlayer)
if Destroyed then
return
end
if targetPlayer == LocalPlayer then
return
end
if not Config.PlayerESP then
RemoveHighlight(targetPlayer)
return
end
if Config.TeamCheck and IsTeammate(targetPlayer) then
RemoveHighlight(targetPlayer)
return
end
local character = targetPlayer.Character
if not character then
RemoveHighlight(targetPlayer)
return
end
local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100")
if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
RemoveHighlight(targetPlayer)
return
end
local highlight = Highlights[targetPlayer]
if highlight and highlight.Adornee ~= character then
RemoveHighlight(targetPlayer)
highlight = nil
end
if not highlight then
highlight = Instance.new("\072\105\103\104\108\105\103\104\116")
highlight.Name = "\083\052\105\110\116\095\080\108\097\121\101\114\069\083\080"
highlight.Adornee = character
highlight.Parent = character
Highlights[targetPlayer] = highlight
end
highlight.Adornee = character
highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
highlight.FillTransparency = 1
highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
highlight.OutlineColor = Theme.White
highlight.Enabled = true
end
local function UpdateAllHighlights()
for _, targetPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
UpdateHighlight(targetPlayer)
end
end
Connect(ESPButton.MouseButton1Click, function()
Config.PlayerESP = not Config.PlayerESP
ESPButton.Text = Config.PlayerESP
and "\080\076\065\089\069\082\032\069\083\080\058\032\079\078"
or "\080\076\065\089\069\082\032\069\083\080\058\032\079\070\070"
UpdateAllHighlights()
end)
Connect(TeamButton.MouseButton1Click, function()
Config.TeamCheck = not Config.TeamCheck
TeamButton.Text = Config.TeamCheck
and "\084\069\065\077\032\067\072\069\067\075\058\032\079\078"
or "\084\069\065\077\032\067\072\069\067\075\058\032\079\070\070"
CurrentTarget = nil
CurrentTargetPart = nil
UpdateAllHighlights()
end)
local function SetupPlayer(targetPlayer)
if targetPlayer == LocalPlayer then
return
end
Connect(targetPlayer.CharacterAdded, function()
task.defer(function()
if not Destroyed then
UpdateHighlight(targetPlayer)
end
end)
end)
Connect(targetPlayer.CharacterRemoving, function()
RemoveHighlight(targetPlayer)
end)
Connect(
targetPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("\084\101\097\109"),
function()
UpdateHighlight(targetPlayer)
end
)
UpdateHighlight(targetPlayer)
end
for _, targetPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
SetupPlayer(targetPlayer)
end
Connect(Players.PlayerAdded, function(targetPlayer)
SetupPlayer(targetPlayer)
end)
Connect(Players.PlayerRemoving, function(targetPlayer)
RemoveHighlight(targetPlayer)
if CurrentTarget == targetPlayer then
CurrentTarget = nil
CurrentTargetPart = nil
end
end)
Connect(
LocalPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("\084\101\097\109"),
function()
CurrentTarget = nil
CurrentTargetPart = nil
UpdateAllHighlights()
end
)

local DragStart
local StartPosition
Connect(DragArea.InputBegan, function(input)
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
Dragging = true
DragStart = input.Position
StartPosition = Main.Position
end
end)

if not Dragging then
return
end
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
local delta = input.Position - DragStart
Main.Position = UDim2.new(
StartPosition.X.Scale,
StartPosition.X.Offset + delta.X,
StartPosition.Y.Scale,
StartPosition.Y.Offset + delta.Y
)
end
end)
Connect(UIS.InputEnded, function(input)
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
Dragging = false
end
end)
local function IsPointInside(guiObject, point)
if not guiObject.Visible then
return false
end
local position = guiObject.AbsolutePosition
local size = guiObject.AbsoluteSize
return point.X >= position.X
and point.X <= position.X + size.X
and point.Y >= position.Y
and point.Y <= position.Y + size.Y
end
local function IsMouseOverControls()
local mousePosition = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
if IsPointInside(Main, mousePosition) then
return true
end
if IsPointInside(Dock, mousePosition) then
return true
end
return false
end
local function DestroyUI()
if Destroyed then
return
end
Destroyed = true
HoldingAim = false
CurrentTarget = nil
CurrentTargetPart = nil
RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(BIND_NAME)
for _, connection in ipairs(Connections) do
if connection.Connected then
connection:Disconnect()
end
end
for targetPlayer in pairs(Highlights) do
RemoveHighlight(targetPlayer)
end
ScreenGui:Destroy()
end
Connect(DestroyButton.MouseButton1Click, DestroyUI)
Connect(UIS.InputBegan, function(input, gameProcessed)
if Destroyed then
return
end
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
if gameProcessed then
return
end
if UIS:GetFocusedTextBox() then
return
end
if IsMouseOverControls() then
return
end
HoldingAim = true
end
end)
Connect(UIS.InputEnded, function(input)
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
HoldingAim = false
CurrentTarget = nil
CurrentTargetPart = nil
if not Destroyed then
SetStatus("\082\069\065\068\089\032\032\124\032\032\072\079\076\068\032\076\069\070\084\032\077\079\085\083\069", false)
end
end
end)

}
local function GetTargetParts(character)
local names
if Config.TargetMode == "\072\101\097\100" then
names = HeadParts
elseif Config.TargetMode == "\084\111\114\115\111" then
names = TorsoParts
else
names = AllBodyParts
end
local parts = {}
for _, name in ipairs(names) do
local part = character:FindFirstChild(name)
if part and part:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
table.insert(parts, part)
end
end
if Config.TargetMode == "\084\111\114\115\111" and #parts == 0 then
local root = character:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
if root and root:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
table.insert(parts, root)
end
end
return parts
end
local function IsValidPlayer(targetPlayer)
if not targetPlayer or targetPlayer == LocalPlayer then
return false
end
if not CanTargetPlayer(targetPlayer) then
return false
end
local character = targetPlayer.Character
if not character then
return false
end
local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100")
if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
return false
end
if not character:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116") then
return false
end
return true
end
local RaycastParams = RaycastParams.new()
RaycastParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
RaycastParams.IgnoreWater = true
local function HasLineOfSight(camera, character, part)
if not Config.WallCheck then
return true
end
local excluded = {}
if LocalPlayer.Character then
table.insert(excluded, LocalPlayer.Character)
end
RaycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = excluded
local origin = camera.CFrame.Position
local direction = part.Position - origin
local result = workspace:Raycast(
origin,
direction,
RaycastParams
)
if not result then
return true
end
return result.Instance:IsDescendantOf(character)
end
local function GetBestPart(camera, character, center)
local parts = GetTargetParts(character)
local bestPart = nil
local bestDistance = math.huge
for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
local screenPosition, onScreen =
camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
if onScreen and screenPosition.Z > 0 then
local screenPoint = Vector2.new(
screenPosition.X,
screenPosition.Y
)
local distance = (screenPoint - center).Magnitude
if distance <= Config.FOVRadius
and distance < bestDistance then
if HasLineOfSight(camera, character, part) then
bestPart = part
bestDistance = distance
end
end
end
end
if not bestPart then
return nil
end
return bestPart, bestDistance
end
local function GetBestTarget(camera, center)
local candidates = {}
local existingCandidate = nil
for _, targetPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if IsValidPlayer(targetPlayer) then
local character = targetPlayer.Character
local part, distance = GetBestPart(
camera,
character,
center
)
if part then
local candidate = {
Player = targetPlayer,
Character = character,
Part = part,
Distance = distance,
}
table.insert(candidates, candidate)
if targetPlayer == CurrentTarget then
existingCandidate = candidate
end
end
end
end
table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
return a.Distance < b.Distance
end)
local best = candidates[1]
if not best then
return nil
end
if existingCandidate
and best.Player ~= existingCandidate.Player then
local switchDistance =
existingCandidate.Distance * Config.SwitchThreshold
if best.Distance >= switchDistance then
return existingCandidate
end
end
return best
end
local function UpdateCircle(camera)
local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
Circle.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
viewport.X / 2,
viewport.Y / 2
)
Circle.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(
Config.FOVRadius * 2,
Config.FOVRadius * 2
)
Circle.Visible = Config.ShowCircle
end
RunService:BindToRenderStep(
BIND_NAME,
Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1,
function(deltaTime)
if Destroyed then
return
end
local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
if not camera then
return
end
UpdateCircle(camera)
if not HoldingAim then
CircleStroke.Color = Theme.Circle
return
end
local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
local center = Vector2.new(
viewport.X / 2,
viewport.Y / 2
)
local candidate = GetBestTarget(
camera,
center
)
if not candidate then
CurrentTarget = nil
CurrentTargetPart = nil
CircleStroke.Color = Theme.Circle
SetStatus("\078\079\032\069\078\069\077\089\032\073\078\083\073\068\069\032\070\079\086", false)
return
end
CurrentTarget = candidate.Player
CurrentTargetPart = candidate.Part
CircleStroke.Color = Theme.Active
SetStatus(
"\084\065\082\071\069\084\032\032\124\032\032" .. candidate.Player.Name,
true
)
local desiredCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(
camera.CFrame.Position,
candidate.Part.Position
)
local speed = math.clamp(
Config.AimSpeed,
Config.MinSpeed,
Config.MaxSpeed
)
local alpha = 1 - math.exp(-speed * deltaTime)
camera.CFrame = camera.CFrame:Lerp(
desiredCFrame,
alpha
)
end
)
Circle.Visible = Config.ShowCircle
SetStatus("\082\069\065\068\089\032\032\124\032\032\072\079\076\068\032\076\069\070\084\032\077\079\085\083\069", false)
print("\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061")
print("\083\052\105\110\116\032\080\108\097\121\101\114\032\065\105\109\032\065\115\115\105\115\116\032\086\051\032\076\111\097\100\101\100")
print("\080\108\097\121\101\114\032\069\083\080\058", Config.PlayerESP)
print("\084\101\097\109\032\067\104\101\099\107\058", Config.TeamCheck)
print("\087\097\108\108\032\067\104\101\099\107\058", Config.WallCheck)
print("\084\097\114\103\101\116\032\077\111\100\101\058", Config.TargetMode)
print("\070\079\086\032\082\097\100\105\117\115\058", Config.FOVRadius)
print("\065\105\109\032\083\112\101\101\100\058", Config.AimSpeed)
print("\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061\061")
end
local function RunEmbeddedPligrammed()
local Players = game:GetService("\080\108\097\121\101\114\115")

local function splitCatalog(raw)
local result = {}
for line in string.gmatch(raw, "[^\r\n]+") do
local value = string.gsub(line, "\094\037\115\042\040\046\045\041\037\115\042\036", "\037\049")
if value ~= "" then
table.insert(result, value)
end
end
return result
end

local function parseStaticDestinations(raw, pathPrefix)
local entries = {}
local nameCounts = {}
for line in string.gmatch(raw, "[^\r\n]+") do
local name, info, rawNumbers = string.match(line, "\094\040\046\045\041\124\040\046\045\041\124\040\046\042\041\036")
if name and rawNumbers then
local values = {}
for token in string.gmatch(rawNumbers, "\091\094\044\093\043") do
local value = tonumber((string.gsub(token, "\094\037\115\042\040\046\045\041\037\115\042\036", "\037\049")))
if value ~= nil then
table.insert(values, value)
end
end
if #values == 12 then
nameCounts[name] = (nameCounts[name] or 0) + 1
local index = nameCounts[name]
local detail = info ~= "" and info or "\087\079\082\076\068\032\068\069\083\084\073\078\065\084\073\079\078"
table.insert(entries, {
Name = name,
Info = detail,
CFrame = CFrame.new(table.unpack(values)),
Path = string.upper(pathPrefix) .. "\032\047\032" .. name .. "\032\047\032" .. detail
.. "\032\047\032\080\079\073\078\084\032" .. string.format("\037\048\050\100", index),
})
else
warn("\091\080\105\108\103\114\097\109\109\101\100\115\099\114\105\112\116\093\032\083\107\105\112\112\101\100\032\105\110\118\097\108\105\100\032\115\116\097\116\105\099\032\100\101\115\116\105\110\097\116\105\111\110\058\032" .. tostring(name))
end
end
end
return entries
end

local GUI_NAME = "\080\105\108\103\114\097\109\109\101\100\115\099\114\105\112\116"
local GUI_CHILD_PREFIX = "\080\105\108\103\114\097\109\109\101\100\115\099\114\105\112\116\095"
for _, oldName in ipairs({"\077\111\098\115\083\101\097\114\099\104\071\117\105", GUI_NAME}) do
local oldGui = playerGui:FindFirstChild(oldName)
if oldGui then
oldGui:Destroy()
end
end
local function create(className, properties, parent)
local object = Instance.new(className)
for property, value in pairs(properties) do
if property == "\078\097\109\101" and typeof(value) == "\115\116\114\105\110\103"
and value ~= GUI_NAME
and string.sub(value, 1, #GUI_CHILD_PREFIX) ~= GUI_CHILD_PREFIX then
object[property] = GUI_CHILD_PREFIX .. value
else
object[property] = value
end
end
object.Parent = parent
return object
end
local function connectGlobal(signal, callback)
local connection = signal:Connect(callback)
table.insert(globalConnections, connection)
return connection
end
local function addCorner(parent, radius)
create("\085\073\067\111\114\110\101\114", {
CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius)
}, parent)
end
local function addStroke(parent, transparency, thickness)
create("\085\073\083\116\114\111\107\101", {
Color = COLORS.Accent,
Transparency = transparency or 0.35,
Thickness = thickness or 1,
ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
}, parent)
end
local function setToggleVisual(button, enabled)
if not button then
return
end
button.BackgroundColor3 = enabled and COLORS.AccentSoft or COLORS.Element
button.TextColor3 = enabled and COLORS.Accent or COLORS.Gray
end
local function makeLabel(parent, name, text, size, position, textSize, muted)
return create("\084\101\120\116\076\097\098\101\108", {
Name = name,
Size = size,
Position = position,
BackgroundTransparency = 1,
Text = text,
TextColor3 = muted and COLORS.Gray or COLORS.White,
TextSize = textSize or 11,
Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium,
TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
}, parent)
end
local function makeButton(parent, name, text, size, position)
local button = create("\084\101\120\116\066\117\116\116\111\110", {
Name = name,
Size = size,
Position = position,
BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.Element,
BorderSizePixel = 0,
Text = text,
TextColor3 = COLORS.White,
TextSize = 10,
Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
AutoButtonColor = false,
Active = true,
Selectable = true,
}, parent)
addCorner(button, 8)
addStroke(button, 0.35, 1)
local buttonScale = create("\085\073\083\099\097\108\101", {
Name = "\084\104\101\109\101\066\117\116\116\111\110\083\099\097\108\101",
Scale = 1,
}, button)
local function tweenButtonScale(scale)
TweenService:Create(
buttonScale,
TweenInfo.new(0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
{Scale = scale}
):Play()
end
button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
tweenButtonScale(1.035)
end)
button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
tweenButtonScale(1)
end)
button.Activated:Connect(function()
TweenService:Create(
buttonScale,
TweenInfo.new(0.07, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
{Scale = 0.965}
):Play()
task.delay(0.08, function()
if buttonScale.Parent then
TweenService:Create(
buttonScale,
TweenInfo.new(0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
{Scale = 1}
):Play()
end
end)
end)
return button
end

BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.Panel,
BorderSizePixel = 0,
}, parent)
addCorner(panel, 11)

return panel
end

local responsiveScale = create("\085\073\083\099\097\108\101", {
Name = "\082\101\115\112\111\110\115\105\118\101\083\099\097\108\101",
Scale = 0.82,
}, main)
local function getViewportSize()
local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
if camera then
return camera.ViewportSize
end
return Vector2.new(1280, 720)
end
local function clampMainToViewport()
if not main.Parent then
return
end
local viewport = getViewportSize()
local size = main.AbsoluteSize
local halfWidth = size.X * 0.5
local halfHeight = size.Y * 0.5
local center = main.AbsolutePosition + size * 0.5
local margin = 10
local minX = math.min(halfWidth + margin, viewport.X * 0.5)
local maxX = math.max(viewport.X - halfWidth - margin, minX)
local minY = math.min(halfHeight + margin, viewport.Y * 0.5)
local maxY = math.max(viewport.Y - halfHeight - margin, minY)
local x = math.clamp(center.X, minX, maxX)
local y = math.clamp(center.Y, minY, maxY)
main.Position = UDim2.new(
0.5, x - viewport.X * 0.5,
0.5, y - viewport.Y * 0.5
)
end
local function updateResponsiveScale()
local viewport = getViewportSize()
local availableWidth = math.max(1, viewport.X - 20)
local availableHeight = math.max(1, viewport.Y - 20)
local fitScale = math.min(
0.82,
availableWidth / SETTINGS.GUIWidth,
availableHeight / SETTINGS.GUIHeight
)
responsiveScale.Scale = math.max(0.1, fitScale)
task.defer(clampMainToViewport)
end
local cameraViewportConnection
local function watchCurrentCamera()
if cameraViewportConnection then
cameraViewportConnection:Disconnect()
cameraViewportConnection = nil
end
local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
if camera then
cameraViewportConnection = camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("\086\105\101\119\112\111\114\116\083\105\122\101"):Connect(updateResponsiveScale)
table.insert(globalConnections, cameraViewportConnection)
end
updateResponsiveScale()
end

local dragHandle = create("\084\101\120\116\066\117\116\116\111\110", {
Name = "\068\114\097\103\072\097\110\100\108\101",
Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0),
BackgroundTransparency = 1,
BorderSizePixel = 0,
Text = "",
AutoButtonColor = false,
Active = true,
Selectable = false,
ZIndex = 7,
}, header)
local dragging = false
local dragInput = nil
local dragStartPointer = nil
local dragStartTopLeft = nil
local function beginDrag(input)
if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
return
end
if dragging then
return
end
dragging = true
dragInput = input
dragStartPointer = input.Position
dragStartTopLeft = main.AbsolutePosition
end
local function updateDrag(input)
if not dragging or not dragStartPointer or not dragStartTopLeft then
return
end
local isMouseMove = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
local isActiveTouch = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
and input == dragInput
if not isMouseMove and not isActiveTouch then
return
end
local delta = input.Position - dragStartPointer
local viewport = getViewportSize()
local size = main.AbsoluteSize
local margin = 10
local minX = margin
local minY = margin
local maxX = math.max(margin, viewport.X - size.X - margin)
local maxY = math.max(margin, viewport.Y - size.Y - margin)
local targetX = math.clamp(dragStartTopLeft.X + delta.X, minX, maxX)
local targetY = math.clamp(dragStartTopLeft.Y + delta.Y, minY, maxY)
local anchor = main.AnchorPoint
main.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
targetX + size.X * anchor.X,
targetY + size.Y * anchor.Y
)
end
local function endDrag(input)
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
or (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input == dragInput) then
dragging = false
dragInput = nil
dragStartPointer = nil
dragStartTopLeft = nil
end
end
connectGlobal(dragHandle.InputBegan, beginDrag)
connectGlobal(UserInputService.InputChanged, updateDrag)
connectGlobal(UserInputService.InputEnded, endDrag)
local left = create("\070\114\097\109\101", {
Name = "\076\101\102\116\080\097\110\101\108",

}, main)

"\077\079\068\069\076\032\070\073\078\068\069\082",
UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 17),
UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
10,
true
)

local expandedMainSize = main.Size
local minimizedMainSize = UDim2.new(
expandedMainSize.X.Scale,
expandedMainSize.X.Offset,
0,
46
)
local isMinimized = false
local minimizeTween = nil
local function setMinimized(minimized)
minimized = minimized == true
if isMinimized == minimized then
return
end
isMinimized = minimized
if minimizeTween then
minimizeTween:Cancel()
minimizeTween = nil
end
if isMinimized then
left.Visible = false
right.Visible = false
if catalogNavBar then catalogNavBar.Visible = false end
if catalogPanel then catalogPanel.Visible = false end
minimizeButton.Text = "\043"
else
if catalogNavBar then catalogNavBar.Visible = true end
if catalogCurrentSection == "\065\117\116\111\109\097\116\105\111\110" then
left.Visible = true
right.Visible = true
if catalogPanel then catalogPanel.Visible = false end
else
left.Visible = false
right.Visible = false
if catalogPanel then catalogPanel.Visible = true end
end
minimizeButton.Text = "\226\128\148"
end
local targetSize = isMinimized and minimizedMainSize or expandedMainSize
minimizeTween = TweenService:Create(
main,
TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
{Size = targetSize}
)
minimizeTween:Play()
end
minimizeButton.Activated:Connect(function()
setMinimized(not isMinimized)
end)
makeLabel(
right,
"\083\101\116\116\105\110\103\115\084\105\116\108\101",
"\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\032\047\032\069\083\080\032\083\069\084\084\073\078\071\083",
UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 17),
UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
10,
true
)

local function applyZektonToTextObject(object)
if not object then return end
if object:IsA("\084\101\120\116\076\097\098\101\108") or object:IsA("\084\101\120\116\066\117\116\116\111\110") or object:IsA("\084\101\120\116\066\111\120") then
pcall(function() object.Font = Enum.Font.SciFi end)
if zektonFontFace then
pcall(function() object.FontFace = zektonFontFace end)
end
end
end
local function applyZektonToTree(root)
if not root then return end
applyZektonToTextObject(root)
for _, descendant in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
applyZektonToTextObject(descendant)
end
end
local function getLeaderboardNameButton(row)
if not row or not row:IsA("\070\114\097\109\101") then return nil end
local button = row:FindFirstChild("\084\101\120\116\066\117\116\116\111\110")
if button and button:IsA("\084\101\120\116\066\117\116\116\111\110") then
return button
end
return nil
end
local function setLeaderboardRgbEnabled(enabled)
leaderboardRgbEnabled = enabled == true
leaderboardRgbButton.Text = leaderboardRgbEnabled and "\082\071\066\058\032\079\078" or "\082\071\066\058\032\079\070\070"
setToggleVisual(leaderboardRgbButton, leaderboardRgbEnabled)
end
local function applyLeaderboardRgbNames()
if not leaderboardStyleReady or not leaderboardScrollingFrame then return end
local rowIndex = 0
for _, row in ipairs(leaderboardScrollingFrame:GetChildren()) do
if row:IsA("\070\114\097\109\101") and row.Visible then
local nameButton = getLeaderboardNameButton(row)
if nameButton then
rowIndex += 1
if leaderboardRgbEnabled then
local hue = (os.clock() * 0.12 + rowIndex * 0.075) % 1
nameButton.TextColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 0.88, 1)
else
local savedColor = row:GetAttribute("\067\111\108\111\114")
if typeof(savedColor) == "\067\111\108\111\114\051" then
nameButton.TextColor3 = savedColor
end
end
end
end
end
end
local function attachLeaderboardAppearance()
task.spawn(function()
local deadline = os.clock() + 45
local screenGui = nil
local board = nil
while not destroyed and gui.Parent and os.clock() < deadline do
screenGui = playerGui:FindFirstChild("\083\099\114\101\101\110\071\117\105")
board = screenGui and screenGui:FindFirstChild("\076\101\097\100\101\114\098\111\097\114\100")
if board then break end
task.wait(0.5)
end
if destroyed or not gui.Parent then return end
if not board then
leaderboardStyleDescription.Text = "\076\069\065\068\069\082\066\079\065\082\068\032\078\079\084\032\070\079\085\078\068\032\047\032\070\079\078\084\032\083\069\084\084\073\078\071\032\082\069\065\068\089"
return
end
local frame = board:FindFirstChild("\070\114\097\109\101")
local scrolling = frame and frame:FindFirstChild("\083\099\114\111\108\108\105\110\103\070\114\097\109\101")
local example = board:FindFirstChild("\069\120\097\109\112\108\101")
if not frame or not scrolling or not example then
leaderboardStyleDescription.Text = "\076\069\065\068\069\082\066\079\065\082\068\032\085\073\032\072\073\069\082\065\082\067\072\089\032\078\079\084\032\070\079\085\078\068"
warn("\091\080\105\108\103\114\097\109\109\101\100\115\099\114\105\112\116\093\032\076\101\097\100\101\114\098\111\097\114\100\032\115\116\121\108\105\110\103\032\115\107\105\112\112\101\100\058\032\101\120\112\101\099\116\101\100\032\083\099\114\101\101\110\071\117\105\046\076\101\097\100\101\114\098\111\097\114\100\046\070\114\097\109\101\046\083\099\114\111\108\108\105\110\103\070\114\097\109\101\032\097\110\100\032\069\120\097\109\112\108\101\046")
return
end
leaderboardGui = board
leaderboardScrollingFrame = scrolling
leaderboardExample = example
leaderboardStyleReady = true
applyZektonToTree(leaderboardGui)
connectGlobal(scrolling.DescendantAdded, function(descendant)
applyZektonToTextObject(descendant)
end)
connectGlobal(scrolling.ChildAdded, function(child)
applyZektonToTree(child)
end)
connectGlobal(scrolling.ChildRemoved, function()
task.defer(applyLeaderboardRgbNames)
end)
leaderboardStyleDescription.Text = "\070\079\078\084\058\032\090\069\075\084\079\078\032\047\032\082\071\066\032\085\083\069\082\078\065\077\069\083"
applyLeaderboardRgbNames()
task.spawn(function()
while not destroyed and gui.Parent and leaderboardStyleReady do
if leaderboardRgbEnabled then
applyLeaderboardRgbNames()
end
task.wait(0.05)
end
end)
end)
end
leaderboardRgbButton.Activated:Connect(function()
setLeaderboardRgbEnabled(not leaderboardRgbEnabled)
applyLeaderboardRgbNames()
end)
setLeaderboardRgbEnabled(false)
attachLeaderboardAppearance()

local function clearAdminCommandRows()
for _, child in ipairs(adminCommandList:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("\084\101\120\116\066\117\116\116\111\110") then
child:Destroy()
end
end
end
local function renderAdminCommandRows()
clearAdminCommandRows()
local query = string.lower(adminCommandSearch.Text or "")
local sortedCommands = table.clone(adminCommandDefinitions)
table.sort(sortedCommands, function(a, b)
return string.lower(a.Name) < string.lower(b.Name)
end)
local shown = 0
for _, command in ipairs(sortedCommands) do
if query == "" or string.find(string.lower(command.Name), query, 1, true) then
shown += 1
local row = makeButton(
adminCommandList,
"\065\100\109\105\110\067\111\109\109\097\110\100\082\111\119",
command.Name .. "\032\032\032\047\032\032\032\067\076\069\065\082\065\078\067\069\032" .. tostring(command.Clearance),
UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 27),
UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
)
row.LayoutOrder = shown
row.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
row.TextSize = 9
create("\085\073\080\097\100\100\105\110\103", {
PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8),
}, row)
row.Activated:Connect(function()
local arguments = #command.Arguments > 0
and table.concat(command.Arguments, "\044\032")
or "\078\079\078\069"
adminCommandDetails.Text = "\067\079\077\077\065\078\068\058\032" .. command.Name
.. "\nCLEARANCE: " .. tostring(command.Clearance)
.. "\032\032\032\047\032\032\032\065\082\071\085\077\069\078\084\083\058\032" .. arguments
end)
end
end
if shown == 0 then
local empty = makeLabel(
adminCommandList,
"\078\111\065\100\109\105\110\067\111\109\109\097\110\100\115",
"\078\079\032\077\065\084\067\072\073\078\071\032\067\079\077\077\065\078\068\083",
UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 25),
UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4),
9,
true
)
empty.LayoutOrder = 1
end
end
connectGlobal(adminCommandSearch:GetPropertyChangedSignal("\084\101\120\116"), renderAdminCommandRows)
renderAdminCommandRows()
adminSettingsDescription.Text = tostring(#adminCommandDefinitions)
.. "\032\067\079\077\077\065\078\068\083\032\047\032\082\069\070\069\082\069\078\067\069\032\079\078\076\089\032\047\032\083\069\082\086\069\082\032\080\069\082\077\073\083\083\073\079\078\032\067\072\069\067\075\032\082\069\081\085\073\082\069\068"
local function startCatalogBrowser()
local TextService = game:GetService("\084\101\120\116\083\101\114\118\105\099\101")
local browserDestroyed = false
local sectionByKey = {}
local sectionStates = {}
local categoryCache = {}
local scanning = {}
local rescanQueued = {}
local watchedRoots = {}
local visitedSections = {}
local refreshTimers = {}
local scheduleCategoryRefresh
local refreshCategory
local currentCategory = "\067\104\101\115\116\115"
local currentName = nil
local currentEntry = nil
local pendingOreTeleportName = nil
local teleportEntry
local getNearestEntry
local namePageLimit = 250
local instancePageLimit = 35
local namePage = 1
local instancePage = 1
local maxNodesPerScan = 10000
local refreshDebounce = 0.22
local farmStates = {
Ores = {Enabled = false, Token = 0, Name = nil, Target = nil, LastTargetObject = nil},
Bosses = {Enabled = false, Token = 0, Name = nil, Target = nil, LastTargetObject = nil},
Mobs = {Enabled = false, Token = 0, Name = nil, Target = nil, LastTargetObject = nil},
}
local function isFarmCategory(category)
return farmStates[category] ~= nil
end
local function getFarmNoun(category)
if category == "\079\114\101\115" then return "\079\082\069" end
if category == "\077\111\098\115" then return "\069\078\069\077\089" end
return "\066\079\083\083"
end
local enemyCatalogLongestFirst = table.clone(CATALOGS.Enemies)
table.sort(enemyCatalogLongestFirst, function(a, b)
local na = string.gsub(string.lower(a), "\091\094\037\119\093", "")
local nb = string.gsub(string.lower(b), "\091\094\037\119\093", "")
return #na > #nb
end)
local enemyAttributeNames = {"\069\110\101\109\121\078\097\109\101", "\077\111\098\078\097\109\101", "\068\105\115\112\108\097\121\078\097\109\101", "\069\110\116\105\116\121\078\097\109\101",
"\078\080\067\078\097\109\101", "\084\097\114\103\101\116\078\097\109\101", "\069\110\101\109\121\084\121\112\101", "\077\111\098\084\121\112\101", "\084\121\112\101"}
local function makeSet(values)
local set = {}
for _, value in ipairs(values or {}) do
set[value] = true
end
return set
end
local function resolveOreCatalogName(rawName)
if typeof(rawName) ~= "\115\116\114\105\110\103" then
return nil
end
local candidate = string.gsub(rawName, "\094\037\115\042\040\046\045\041\037\115\042\036", "\037\049")
if candidate == "" then
return nil
end
local lowered = string.lower(candidate)
local compact = string.gsub(lowered, "\091\037\115\095\037\045\093\043", "")
for _, catalogName in ipairs(CATALOGS.Ores) do
local base = string.lower(catalogName)
local normalized = string.gsub(base, "\091\037\115\095\037\045\093\043", "")
if lowered == base or compact == normalized
or compact == normalized .. "\111\114\101"
or compact == normalized .. "\111\114\101\115"
or compact == normalized .. "\111\114\101\110\111\100\101"
or compact == normalized .. "\111\114\101\100\101\112\111\115\105\116" then
return catalogName
end
end
return nil
end
local function resolveBossCatalogName(rawName)
if typeof(rawName) ~= "\115\116\114\105\110\103" then
return nil
end
local compact = string.lower(rawName)
compact = string.gsub(compact, "\091\094\037\119\093", "")
if compact == "" then
return nil
end
local prefixes = {"\098\111\115\115", "\101\110\101\109\121", "\109\111\098", "\110\112\099", "\109\111\100\101\108"}
for _, prefix in ipairs(prefixes) do
if string.sub(compact, 1, #prefix) == prefix then
compact = string.sub(compact, #prefix + 1)
break
end
end
for _, catalogName in ipairs(CATALOGS.Bosses) do
local target = string.lower(catalogName)
target = string.gsub(target, "\091\094\037\119\093", "")
if compact == target or string.sub(compact, 1, #target) == target then
local suffix = string.sub(compact, #target + 1)
if suffix == "" or string.sub(suffix, 1, 4) == "\098\111\115\115"
or string.sub(suffix, 1, 5) == "\108\101\118\101\108"
or string.sub(suffix, 1, 2) == "\108\118"
or string.sub(suffix, 1, 5) == "\112\104\097\115\101"
or string.match(suffix, "\094\037\100") then
return catalogName
end
end
end
return nil
end
local function resolveEnemyCatalogName(rawName)
if typeof(rawName) ~= "\115\116\114\105\110\103" then return nil end
local compact = string.lower(rawName)
compact = string.gsub(compact, "\091\094\037\119\093", "")
if compact == "" then return nil end
for _, prefix in ipairs({"\101\110\101\109\121", "\109\111\098", "\110\112\099", "\109\111\100\101\108", "\098\111\115\115"}) do
if string.sub(compact, 1, #prefix) == prefix then
compact = string.sub(compact, #prefix + 1)
break
end
end
for _, catalogName in ipairs(enemyCatalogLongestFirst) do
local target = string.gsub(string.lower(catalogName), "\091\094\037\119\093", "")
if compact == target then return catalogName end
if #target > 1 and string.sub(compact, 1, #target) == target then
local suffix = string.sub(compact, #target + 1)
if suffix == "\098\111\115\115" or suffix == "\101\110\101\109\121" or suffix == "\109\111\098"
or suffix == "\110\112\099" or suffix == "\109\111\100\101\108" or suffix == "\099\108\111\110\101"
or suffix == "\115\112\097\119\110" or suffix == "\099\111\112\121"
or string.sub(suffix, 1, 5) == "\108\101\118\101\108"
or string.sub(suffix, 1, 2) == "\108\118"
or string.sub(suffix, 1, 5) == "\112\104\097\115\101"
or string.match(suffix, "\094\037\100") then
return catalogName
end
end
end
return nil
end
local function isPotentialEnemyObject(instance)
if not instance then return false end
if instance:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") then
if resolveEnemyCatalogName(instance.Name) then return true end
for _, attributeName in ipairs(enemyAttributeNames) do
if resolveEnemyCatalogName(instance:GetAttribute(attributeName)) then return true end
end
for _, child in ipairs(instance:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("\083\116\114\105\110\103\086\097\108\117\101") and table.find(enemyAttributeNames, child.Name)
and resolveEnemyCatalogName(child.Value) then return true end
end
elseif instance:IsA("\083\116\114\105\110\103\086\097\108\117\101") and resolveEnemyCatalogName(instance.Value) then
return true
end
return false
end
local function isPotentialOreObject(instance)
if not instance then return false end
if instance:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") or instance:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
if resolveOreCatalogName(instance.Name) then return true end
for _, attributeName in ipairs({"\079\114\101\084\121\112\101", "\079\114\101\078\097\109\101", "\082\101\115\111\117\114\099\101\084\121\112\101", "\079\114\101", "\084\121\112\101"}) do
if resolveOreCatalogName(instance:GetAttribute(attributeName)) then return true end
end
for _, child in ipairs(instance:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("\083\116\114\105\110\103\086\097\108\117\101")
and (child.Name == "\079\114\101\084\121\112\101" or child.Name == "\079\114\101\078\097\109\101"
or child.Name == "\082\101\115\111\117\114\099\101\084\121\112\101" or child.Name == "\084\121\112\101")
and resolveOreCatalogName(child.Value) then
return true
end
end
elseif instance:IsA("\083\116\114\105\110\103\086\097\108\117\101") and resolveOreCatalogName(instance.Value) then
return true
end
return false
end
local function isPotentialBossObject(instance)
if not instance then return false end
if instance:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") then
if resolveBossCatalogName(instance.Name) then
return true
end
for _, attributeName in ipairs({"\066\111\115\115\078\097\109\101", "\069\110\101\109\121\078\097\109\101", "\068\105\115\112\108\097\121\078\097\109\101", "\069\110\116\105\116\121\078\097\109\101", "\078\080\067\078\097\109\101", "\084\097\114\103\101\116\078\097\109\101", "\084\121\112\101"}) do
if resolveBossCatalogName(instance:GetAttribute(attributeName)) then
return true
end
end
for _, child in ipairs(instance:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("\083\116\114\105\110\103\086\097\108\117\101")
and (child.Name == "\066\111\115\115\078\097\109\101" or child.Name == "\069\110\101\109\121\078\097\109\101"
or child.Name == "\068\105\115\112\108\097\121\078\097\109\101" or child.Name == "\069\110\116\105\116\121\078\097\109\101"
or child.Name == "\078\080\067\078\097\109\101" or child.Name == "\078\097\109\101" or child.Name == "\084\121\112\101")
and resolveBossCatalogName(child.Value) then
return true
end
end
elseif instance:IsA("\083\116\114\105\110\103\086\097\108\117\101") and resolveBossCatalogName(instance.Value) then
return true
end
return false
end

local teleportButton = makeButton(panel, "\067\097\116\097\108\111\103\084\101\108\101\112\111\114\116\066\117\116\116\111\110", "\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\032\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068", UDim2.fromOffset(168, 34), UDim2.fromOffset(10, 470))
teleportButton.Visible = false
