farmButton.Visible = false
local detailLabel = makeLabel(panel, "\067\097\116\097\108\111\103\068\101\116\097\105\108\076\097\098\101\108", "\067\076\073\067\075\032\065\032\078\065\077\069\032\079\082\032\073\078\083\084\065\078\067\069\032\084\079\032\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\046", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 70), UDim2.fromOffset(10, 510), 9, true)
detailLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
detailLabel.TextWrapped = true
local lightingButton = makeButton(panel, "\065\112\112\108\121\068\097\121\078\111\070\111\103\066\117\116\116\111\110", "\065\080\080\076\089\032\068\065\089\032\047\032\078\079\032\070\079\071",
UDim2.fromOffset(220, 38), UDim2.fromOffset(10, 96))
lightingButton.Visible = false
lightingButton.TextSize = 10
local lightingDescription = makeLabel(panel, "\076\105\103\104\116\105\110\103\068\101\115\099\114\105\112\116\105\111\110",
"\067\076\073\069\078\084\045\083\073\068\069\032\079\078\076\089\032\032\047\032\032\067\076\069\065\082\083\032\076\073\071\072\084\073\078\071\032\069\070\070\069\067\084\032\073\078\083\084\065\078\067\069\083\044\032\083\069\084\083\032\067\076\079\067\075\032\084\073\077\069\032\084\079\032\049\050\058\048\048\044\032\065\078\068\032\082\069\077\079\086\069\083\032\070\079\071",
UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 54), UDim2.fromOffset(10, 144), 10, true)
lightingDescription.TextWrapped = true
lightingDescription.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
lightingDescription.Visible = false
lightingButton.Activated:Connect(function()
if browserDestroyed or not gui.Parent then return end
local removedCount = 0
for _, child in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
child:Destroy()
removedCount += 1
end
local ok, err = pcall(function()
Lighting.ClockTime = 12
Lighting.TimeOfDay = "\049\050\058\048\048\058\048\048"
Lighting.FogStart = 0
Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
Lighting.Brightness = 2
Lighting.ExposureCompensation = 0
end)
if ok then
detailLabel.Text = "DAY / NO FOG APPLIED LOCALLY\n" .. tostring(removedCount)
.. "\032\076\073\071\072\084\073\078\071\032\073\078\083\084\065\078\067\069\040\083\041\032\067\076\069\065\082\069\068"
rootStatus.Text = "\067\076\073\069\078\084\032\076\073\071\072\084\073\078\071\032\085\080\068\065\084\069\068"
else
detailLabel.Text = "LIGHTING UPDATE FAILED\n" .. tostring(err)
end
end)
catalogNavBar = nav
catalogPanel = panel
left.Visible = false
right.Visible = false
panel.Visible = true
local function clearHorizontalList(container)
for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("\071\117\105\066\117\116\116\111\110") or child:IsA("\084\101\120\116\076\097\098\101\108") then
child:Destroy()
end
end
end
local function safeNameMatch(value, query)
return query == "" or string.find(string.lower(tostring(value or "")), query, 1, true) ~= nil
end
local function getRelativePath(root, node)
local names = {}
local current = node
while current and current ~= root do
table.insert(names, 1, current.Name)
current = current.Parent
end
return table.concat(names, "\047")
end
local function getSectionRoots(definition)
local roots = {}
local names = definition.Roots or {}
for _, rootName in ipairs(names) do
local root = workspace:FindFirstChild(rootName)
if root and (root:IsA("\070\111\108\100\101\114") or root:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108")) then
table.insert(roots, root)
end
end
if #roots == 0 and definition.FallbackRoots then
for _, rootName in ipairs(definition.FallbackRoots) do
local root = workspace:FindFirstChild(rootName)
if root and (root:IsA("\070\111\108\100\101\114") or root:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108")) then
table.insert(roots, root)
end
end
end
if #roots == 0 and (definition.Mode == "\111\114\101" or definition.Mode == "\098\111\115\115" or definition.Mode == "\099\104\097\114\097\099\116\101\114") then
table.insert(roots, workspace)
end
return roots
end
local function getEntryTransform(entry)
if entry and entry.StaticCFrame then
return entry.StaticCFrame
end
local object = entry and entry.Object
if not object or not object.Parent or not object:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
return nil
end
if object:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
return object.CFrame
elseif object:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") then
local ok, pivot = pcall(function() return object:GetPivot() end)
if ok then return pivot end
end
return nil
end
local function entryIsAvailable(entry)
if entry and entry.StaticCFrame then
return true
end
local object = entry and entry.Object
if not object or not object.Parent or not object:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
return false
end
if object:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") then
local humanoid = object:FindFirstChildOfClass("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100")
if not humanoid then
humanoid = object:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100", true)
end
if humanoid and humanoid.Health <= 0 then
return false
end
end
for _, attributeName in ipairs({"\072\101\097\108\116\104", "\072\080", "\068\117\114\097\098\105\108\105\116\121"}) do
local value = object:GetAttribute(attributeName)
if typeof(value) == "\110\117\109\098\101\114" and value <= 0 then
return false
end
end
return getEntryTransform(entry) ~= nil
end
local function addEntry(byName, seen, name, object, root, path, canTeleport)
if not name or name == "" then return end
local list = byName[name]
if not list then
list = {}
byName[name] = list
end
local key = object or (root and (name .. "\124" .. root:GetFullName()))
if key and seen[key] then return end
if key then seen[key] = true end
table.insert(list, {
Name = name,
Object = object,
Root = root,
Path = path or (object and object:GetFullName()) or (root and root:GetFullName()) or name,
CanTeleport = canTeleport == true,
})
end
local function appendStaticEntries(byName, seen, staticEntries)
for _, record in ipairs(staticEntries or {}) do
addEntry(byName, seen, record.Name, nil, nil, record.Path, true)
local list = byName[record.Name]
local entry = list and list[#list]
if entry then
entry.StaticCFrame = record.CFrame
entry.StaticInfo = record.Info
end
end
end
local function catalogPathMatch(definition, relativePath)
if not definition.Catalog or relativePath == "" then return nil end
for _, catalogName in ipairs(definition.Catalog) do
if relativePath == catalogName then
return catalogName
end
local startIndex = #relativePath - #catalogName + 1
if startIndex > 1
and string.sub(relativePath, startIndex, -1) == catalogName
and string.sub(relativePath, startIndex - 1, startIndex - 1) == "\047" then
return catalogName
end
end
return nil
end
local function scanWorkspaceCategory()
local byName = {}
local seen = {}
for _, child in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
local target = nil
if child:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") or child:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
target = child
end
addEntry(byName, seen, child.Name, target or child, child,
target and target:GetFullName() or child:GetFullName(), target ~= nil)
end
return {ByName = byName, Roots = {workspace}, RootSummary = "\087\079\082\075\083\080\065\067\069\032\067\072\073\076\068\082\069\078\032\047\032\068\073\082\069\067\084\032\079\078\076\089"}
end
local function scanDefinition(definition)
if definition.Mode == "\119\111\114\107\115\112\097\099\101" then
return scanWorkspaceCategory()
end
if definition.Mode == "\108\105\103\104\116\105\110\103" then
return {ByName = {}, Roots = {}, RootSummary = "\076\079\067\065\076\032\067\076\073\069\078\084\032\076\073\071\072\084\073\078\071\032\067\079\078\084\082\079\076"}
end
if definition.Mode == "\115\116\097\116\105\099" then
local byName = {}
appendStaticEntries(byName, {}, definition.StaticEntries)
return {
ByName = byName,
Roots = {},
RootSummary = "\083\084\065\084\073\067\032\068\069\083\084\073\078\065\084\073\079\078\083\032\047\032" .. tostring(#(definition.StaticEntries or {})) .. "\032\080\079\073\078\084\083",
}
end
if definition.Mode == "\109\105\115\099" then
local byName = {
AprilMachine = {{Name = "\065\112\114\105\108\077\097\099\104\105\110\101", Object = nil, Root = nil,
Path = "\083\084\065\084\073\067\032\068\069\083\084\073\078\065\084\073\079\078\032\047\032\069\088\065\067\084\032\065\080\082\073\076\077\065\067\072\073\078\069\032\067\070\082\065\077\069", CanTeleport = true, StaticAprilMachine = true}},
}
appendStaticEntries(byName, {}, definition.StaticEntries)
return {
ByName = byName,
Roots = {},
RootSummary = "\083\084\065\084\073\067\032\068\069\083\084\073\078\065\084\073\079\078\083\032\047\032\077\073\083\067\032\065\082\069\065\083",
}
end
local roots = getSectionRoots(definition)
local byName = {}
local seen = {}
local visited = 0
local catalogSet = definition.CatalogSet or {}
for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
local stack = {{Node = root, Label = nil, RelativePath = ""}}
while #stack > 0 and visited < maxNodesPerScan do
local frame = table.remove(stack)
local node = frame.Node
local relativePath = frame.RelativePath
local inheritedLabel = frame.Label
visited += 1
local ownCatalogName = catalogSet[node.Name] and node.Name or nil
local attributeLabel = nil
if definition.Mode == "\099\104\097\114\097\099\116\101\114" then
if node:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") then
ownCatalogName = ownCatalogName or resolveEnemyCatalogName(node.Name)
for _, attributeName in ipairs(enemyAttributeNames) do
local resolved = resolveEnemyCatalogName(node:GetAttribute(attributeName))
if resolved then attributeLabel = resolved; break end
end
if not attributeLabel then
for _, child in ipairs(node:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("\083\116\114\105\110\103\086\097\108\117\101") and table.find(enemyAttributeNames, child.Name) then
local resolved = resolveEnemyCatalogName(child.Value)
if resolved then attributeLabel = resolved; break end
end
end
end
end
elseif definition.Mode == "\098\111\115\115" then
ownCatalogName = ownCatalogName or resolveBossCatalogName(node.Name)
for _, attributeName in ipairs({"\066\111\115\115\078\097\109\101", "\069\110\101\109\121\078\097\109\101", "\068\105\115\112\108\097\121\078\097\109\101", "\069\110\116\105\116\121\078\097\109\101", "\078\080\067\078\097\109\101", "\084\097\114\103\101\116\078\097\109\101", "\084\121\112\101"}) do
local value = node:GetAttribute(attributeName)
local resolved = resolveBossCatalogName(value)
if resolved then
attributeLabel = resolved
break
end
end
if not attributeLabel then
for _, child in ipairs(node:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("\083\116\114\105\110\103\086\097\108\117\101")
and (child.Name == "\066\111\115\115\078\097\109\101" or child.Name == "\069\110\101\109\121\078\097\109\101"
or child.Name == "\068\105\115\112\108\097\121\078\097\109\101" or child.Name == "\069\110\116\105\116\121\078\097\109\101"
or child.Name == "\078\080\067\078\097\109\101" or child.Name == "\078\097\109\101" or child.Name == "\084\121\112\101") then
local resolved = resolveBossCatalogName(child.Value)
if resolved then
attributeLabel = resolved
break
end
end
end
end
elseif definition.Mode == "\111\114\101" then
ownCatalogName = ownCatalogName or resolveOreCatalogName(node.Name)
for _, attributeName in ipairs({"\079\114\101\084\121\112\101", "\079\114\101\078\097\109\101", "\082\101\115\111\117\114\099\101\084\121\112\101", "\079\114\101", "\084\121\112\101"}) do
local value = node:GetAttribute(attributeName)
local resolved = resolveOreCatalogName(value)
if resolved then
attributeLabel = resolved
break
end
end
if not attributeLabel then
for _, child in ipairs(node:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("\083\116\114\105\110\103\086\097\108\117\101")
and (child.Name == "\079\114\101\084\121\112\101" or child.Name == "\079\114\101\078\097\109\101"
or child.Name == "\082\101\115\111\117\114\099\101\084\121\112\101" or child.Name == "\084\121\112\101") then
local resolved = resolveOreCatalogName(child.Value)
if resolved then
attributeLabel = resolved
break
end
end
end
end
end
local pathCatalogName = definition.Mode == "\108\111\099\097\116\105\111\110"
and catalogPathMatch(definition, relativePath) or nil
local effectiveLabel = attributeLabel or ownCatalogName or pathCatalogName or inheritedLabel
local isModel = node:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108")
local isPart = node:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116")
local isFolder = node:IsA("\070\111\108\100\101\114")
local isTarget = isModel or isPart
local candidateName = nil
if definition.Catalog then
if definition.Mode == "\099\104\097\114\097\099\116\101\114" then
local enemyLabel = attributeLabel or ownCatalogName or inheritedLabel
if isModel and enemyLabel and definition.CatalogSet[enemyLabel] then
candidateName = enemyLabel
end
elseif definition.Mode == "\098\111\115\115" then
local bossLabel = attributeLabel or ownCatalogName or inheritedLabel
if isModel and bossLabel and definition.CatalogSet[bossLabel] then
candidateName = bossLabel
end
elseif definition.Mode == "\111\114\101" then
if isTarget and effectiveLabel then
candidateName = effectiveLabel
end
elseif definition.Mode == "\108\111\099\097\116\105\111\110" then
if isTarget and effectiveLabel then
candidateName = effectiveLabel
end
else
if isTarget and effectiveLabel then
candidateName = effectiveLabel
end
end
else
if node ~= root then
if definition.Mode == "\110\112\099" then
if isModel then candidateName = node.Name end
elseif isModel or isPart then
candidateName = node.Name
end
end
end
if candidateName then
addEntry(byName, seen, candidateName, node, root,
node:GetFullName(), true)
end
local stopAtModel = isModel and candidateName ~= nil
local shouldDescend = not stopAtModel and not isPart
if shouldDescend then
local childLabel = effectiveLabel
for _, child in ipairs(node:GetChildren()) do
local childPath = relativePath == "" and child.Name
or (relativePath .. "\047" .. child.Name)
table.insert(stack, {
Node = child,
Label = childLabel,
RelativePath = childPath,
})
end
end
if visited % 350 == 0 then
task.wait()
if browserDestroyed or not gui.Parent then
return {ByName = {}, Roots = roots, RootSummary = "\067\065\078\067\069\076\076\069\068"}
end
end
end
end
appendStaticEntries(byName, seen, definition.StaticEntries)
local rootNames = {}
for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
table.insert(rootNames, root.Name)
end
local summary
if #rootNames == 0 then
summary = #(definition.StaticEntries or {}) > 0
and ("\083\084\065\084\073\067\032\068\069\083\084\073\078\065\084\073\079\078\083\032\047\032" .. tostring(#definition.StaticEntries) .. "\032\080\079\073\078\084\083\032\047\032\076\073\086\069\032\082\079\079\084\032\078\079\084\032\070\079\085\078\068")
or "\082\079\079\084\032\077\073\083\083\073\078\071\032\047\032\067\072\069\067\075\032\069\088\080\069\067\084\069\068\032\070\079\076\068\069\082\032\078\065\077\069"
else
summary = "\082\079\079\084\083\058\032" .. table.concat(rootNames, "\044\032") .. "\032\047\032\078\079\068\069\083\032\067\072\069\067\075\069\068\058\032" .. tostring(visited)
if visited >= maxNodesPerScan then
summary = summary .. "\032\047\032\083\067\065\078\032\067\065\080\080\069\068"
end
end
return {ByName = byName, Roots = roots, RootSummary = summary}
end
local function countAvailable(category, name)
local state = categoryCache[category]
local list = state and state.ByName and state.ByName[name]
local count = 0
for _, entry in ipairs(list or {}) do
if entryIsAvailable(entry) then count += 1 end
end
return count
end
local function updateSelectedText()
if currentEntry then
selectedLabel.Text = "\084\065\082\071\069\084\058\032" .. tostring(currentEntry.Name)
.. "\n" .. tostring(currentEntry.Path)
teleportButton.Active = currentEntry.StaticAprilMachine == true
or entryIsAvailable(currentEntry)
or (isFarmCategory(currentCategory) and currentName ~= nil)
else
local noun = isFarmCategory(currentCategory) and getFarmNoun(currentCategory) or "\084\065\082\071\069\084"
selectedLabel.Text = currentName and ("\084\065\082\071\069\084\032" .. noun .. "\058\032" .. tostring(currentName)
.. "\nWAITING FOR LIVE INSTANCE") or "\084\065\082\071\069\084\058\032\078\079\078\069"
teleportButton.Active = isFarmCategory(currentCategory) and currentName ~= nil
end
teleportButton.BackgroundTransparency = teleportButton.Active and 0 or 0.35
end
local function renderInstances()
clearHorizontalList(instanceList)
local state = categoryCache[currentCategory]
local list = state and state.ByName and state.ByName[currentName] or {}
local available = {}
for _, entry in ipairs(list) do
if entryIsAvailable(entry) or entry.StaticAprilMachine then
table.insert(available, entry)
end
end
instancesTitle.Text = "\079\066\074\069\067\084\032\073\078\083\084\065\078\067\069\083\032\047\032" .. tostring(#available) .. "\032\065\086\065\073\076\065\066\076\069"
instancePage = math.max(1, instancePage)
if #available == 0 then
local empty = makeLabel(instanceList, "\078\111\073\110\115\116\097\110\099\101\115", "\078\079\032\065\086\065\073\076\065\066\076\069\032\073\078\083\084\065\078\067\069", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 38), UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), 10, true)
empty.ZIndex = 3
currentEntry = nil
updateSelectedText()
return
end
local firstIndex = (instancePage - 1) * instancePageLimit + 1
local lastIndex = math.min(#available, firstIndex + instancePageLimit - 1)
for index = firstIndex, lastIndex do
local entry = available[index]
local displayPath = entry.Path
local button = makeButton(instanceList, "\073\110\115\116\097\110\099\101\067\104\111\105\099\101", "\035" .. tostring(index) .. "\032\032" .. displayPath,
UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 44), UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0))
button.TextSize = 9
button.TextWrapped = true
button.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
button.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
button.LayoutOrder = index
create("\085\073\080\097\100\100\105\110\103", {
PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10),
PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8),
}, button)
button.Activated:Connect(function()
if browserDestroyed or teleporting then return end
currentEntry = entry
updateSelectedText()
if isFarmCategory(currentCategory) and currentCategory ~= "\079\114\101\115" then
detailLabel.Text = string.upper(getFarmNoun(currentCategory))
.. " INSTANCE READY\n" .. tostring(entry.Path)
else
detailLabel.Text = "\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\073\078\071\032\084\079\032" .. string.upper(tostring(entry.Name))
.. "\n" .. tostring(entry.Path)
teleportEntry(entry)
end
end)
end
if #available > lastIndex then
local more = makeButton(instanceList, "\077\111\114\101\073\110\115\116\097\110\099\101\115", "\076\079\065\068\032\077\079\082\069\032\073\078\083\084\065\078\067\069\083",
UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 34), UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0))
more.Activated:Connect(function()
instancePage += 1
renderInstances()
end)
end
if currentEntry and (not currentEntry.StaticAprilMachine) and not entryIsAvailable(currentEntry) then
currentEntry = nil
end
updateSelectedText()
end
local function renderNames()
clearHorizontalList(nameList)
local state = categoryCache[currentCategory]
local definition = sectionByKey[currentCategory]
local names = {}
local query = string.lower(searchBox.Text or "")
if definition and definition.Catalog then
for _, name in ipairs(definition.Catalog) do
if safeNameMatch(name, query) then table.insert(names, name) end
end
elseif state and state.ByName then
for name in pairs(state.ByName) do
if safeNameMatch(name, query) then table.insert(names, name) end
end
table.sort(names, function(a, b) return string.lower(a) < string.lower(b) end)
end
local availableCatalogCount = #names
local maxToShow = math.min(availableCatalogCount, namePage * namePageLimit)
local firstToShow = 1
for index = firstToShow, maxToShow do
local name = names[index]
local text = name
if currentCategory == "\079\114\101\082\111\117\116\101\115" and ORE_ROUTE_INFO[name] then
text = name .. "\032\032\047\032\032" .. ORE_ROUTE_INFO[name]
end
local button = makeButton(nameList, "\067\097\116\097\108\111\103\078\097\109\101\067\104\111\105\099\101", text,
UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 32), UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0))
button.TextSize = 9
button.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
button.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
button.LayoutOrder = index
create("\085\073\080\097\100\100\105\110\103", {
PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10),
PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8),
}, button)
if name == currentName then
button.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.AccentSoft
button.TextColor3 = COLORS.Accent
end
button.Activated:Connect(function()
currentName = name
currentEntry = nil
instancePage = 1
local activeFarm = farmStates[currentCategory]
if activeFarm and activeFarm.Enabled then
activeFarm.Name = name
activeFarm.Target = nil
activeFarm.LastTargetObject = nil
end
detailLabel.Text = "\078\065\077\069\032\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068\058\032" .. name
renderNames()
renderInstances()
if currentCategory == "\079\114\101\115" then
local nearestOre = getNearestEntry("\079\114\101\115", name)
if nearestOre then
pendingOreTeleportName = nil
currentEntry = nearestOre
updateSelectedText()
detailLabel.Text = "\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\073\078\071\032\084\079\032\078\069\065\082\069\083\084\032" .. string.upper(name) .. " ORE\n" .. tostring(nearestOre.Path)
teleportEntry(nearestOre)
else
pendingOreTeleportName = name
currentEntry = nil
updateSelectedText()
detailLabel.Text = "\083\069\065\082\067\072\073\078\071\032\070\079\082\032" .. string.upper(name)
.. "\032\079\082\069\032\073\078\083\084\065\078\067\069\083\046\046\046"
refreshCategory("\079\114\101\115", true)
end
elseif currentCategory == "\066\111\115\115\101\115" or currentCategory == "\077\111\098\115" then
local nearestTarget = getNearestEntry(currentCategory, name)
if nearestTarget then
currentEntry = nearestTarget
updateSelectedText()
detailLabel.Text = string.upper(getFarmNoun(currentCategory))
.. " READY\n" .. tostring(nearestTarget.Path)
else
currentEntry = nil
updateSelectedText()
detailLabel.Text = "\065\085\084\079\045\083\069\065\082\067\072\073\078\071\032\070\079\082\032" .. string.upper(name)
.. "\032\047\032\087\065\073\084\073\078\071\032\070\079\082\032\076\073\086\069\032\073\078\083\084\065\078\067\069"
refreshCategory(currentCategory, true)
end
elseif not isFarmCategory(currentCategory) then
local nearestTarget = getNearestEntry(currentCategory, name)
if not nearestTarget then
local cached = categoryCache[currentCategory]
local candidates = cached and cached.ByName and cached.ByName[name] or {}
for _, candidate in ipairs(candidates) do
if candidate.StaticAprilMachine or entryIsAvailable(candidate) then
nearestTarget = candidate
break
end
end
end
if nearestTarget then
currentEntry = nearestTarget
updateSelectedText()
detailLabel.Text = "\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\073\078\071\032\084\079\032" .. string.upper(name)
.. "\n" .. tostring(nearestTarget.Path)
teleportEntry(nearestTarget)
else
currentEntry = nil
updateSelectedText()
detailLabel.Text = "\078\079\032\065\086\065\073\076\065\066\076\069\032\073\078\083\084\065\078\067\069\032\070\079\082\032" .. string.upper(name)
refreshCategory(currentCategory, true)
end
end
end)
end
if availableCatalogCount > maxToShow then
local more = makeButton(nameList, "\077\111\114\101\078\097\109\101\115", "\076\079\065\068\032\077\079\082\069\032\032\043" .. tostring(availableCatalogCount - maxToShow),
UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 32), UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0))
more.Activated:Connect(function()
namePage += 1
renderNames()
end)
end
namesTitle.Text = (isFarmCategory(currentCategory) and "\070\065\082\077\032\084\065\082\071\069\084\083\032\047\032" or "\065\086\065\073\076\065\066\076\069\032\078\065\077\069\083\032\047\032")
.. tostring(availableCatalogCount)
.. (definition and definition.Catalog and "\032\032\040\067\065\084\065\076\079\071\041" or "\032\032\040\076\073\086\069\032\079\066\074\069\067\084\083\041")
if availableCatalogCount == 0 then
makeLabel(nameList, "\078\111\078\097\109\101\115", "\078\079\032\067\065\084\065\076\079\071\032\069\078\084\082\073\069\083\032\077\065\084\067\072\032\084\072\073\083\032\083\069\065\082\067\072", UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 34), UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), 10, true)
end
if currentName then
renderInstances()
else
clearHorizontalList(instanceList)
instancesTitle.Text = "\079\066\074\069\067\084\032\073\078\083\084\065\078\067\069\083\032\047\032\067\076\073\067\075\032\084\079\032\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084"
end
end
scheduleCategoryRefresh = function(category)
if browserDestroyed or refreshTimers[category] then return end
refreshTimers[category] = true
task.delay(refreshDebounce, function()
refreshTimers[category] = nil
if not browserDestroyed then
refreshCategory(category, true)
end
end)
end
refreshCategory = function(category, force)
if browserDestroyed or not gui.Parent then return end
if scanning[category] then
rescanQueued[category] = true
return
end
if not force and categoryCache[category] then
return
end
scanning[category] = true
task.spawn(function()
local definition = sectionByKey[category]
local ok, result = pcall(scanDefinition, definition)
if ok and result and not browserDestroyed then
categoryCache[category] = result
visitedSections[category] = true
if isFarmCategory(category) and currentName and currentName ~= "" then
local selectedEntries = result.ByName and result.ByName[currentName] or {}
local selectedAvailable = false
for _, entry in ipairs(selectedEntries) do
if entryIsAvailable(entry) then
selectedAvailable = true
break
end
end
if not selectedAvailable then
local worldFallbackDefinition = {
Key = category .. "\087\111\114\108\100\083\101\108\101\099\116\101\100\070\097\108\108\098\097\099\107",
Label = definition.Label,
Catalog = definition.Catalog,
Roots = {},
Mode = definition.Mode,
CatalogSet = definition.CatalogSet,
}
local fallbackOk, fallbackResult = pcall(scanDefinition, worldFallbackDefinition)
local fallbackEntries = fallbackOk and fallbackResult
and fallbackResult.ByName and fallbackResult.ByName[currentName] or {}
local fallbackAvailable = false
for _, entry in ipairs(fallbackEntries) do
if entryIsAvailable(entry) then
fallbackAvailable = true
break
end
end
if fallbackAvailable then
result = fallbackResult
categoryCache[category] = result
end
end
end
for _, root in ipairs(result.Roots or {}) do
local key = category .. "\124" .. tostring(root)
if not watchedRoots[key] then
watchedRoots[key] = true
if root == workspace and isFarmCategory(category) then
connectGlobal(workspace.DescendantAdded, function(instance)
local relevant = (category == "\066\111\115\115\101\115" and isPotentialBossObject(instance))
or (category == "\077\111\098\115" and isPotentialEnemyObject(instance))
or (category == "\079\114\101\115" and isPotentialOreObject(instance))
if relevant and visitedSections[category] then
scheduleCategoryRefresh(category)
end
end)
connectGlobal(workspace.DescendantRemoving, function(instance)
local relevant = (category == "\066\111\115\115\101\115" and isPotentialBossObject(instance))
or (category == "\077\111\098\115" and isPotentialEnemyObject(instance))
or (category == "\079\114\101\115" and isPotentialOreObject(instance))
if relevant and visitedSections[category] then
scheduleCategoryRefresh(category)
end
end)
elseif root ~= workspace then
connectGlobal(root.DescendantAdded, function(instance)
if instance:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") or instance:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") or instance:IsA("\070\111\108\100\101\114")
or instance:IsA("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100") then
if visitedSections[category] then
scheduleCategoryRefresh(category)
end
end
end)
connectGlobal(root.DescendantRemoving, function(instance)
if instance:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") or instance:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") or instance:IsA("\070\111\108\100\101\114")
or instance:IsA("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100") then
if visitedSections[category] then
scheduleCategoryRefresh(category)
end
end
end)
end
end
end
if currentCategory == category then
if isFarmCategory(category) then
rootStatus.Text = "\065\085\084\079\032\068\069\084\069\067\084\073\079\078\032\065\067\084\073\086\069\032\047\032"
.. tostring(#(result.Roots or {})) .. "\032\084\065\082\071\069\084\032\082\079\079\084\040\083\041"
else
rootStatus.Text = result.RootSummary
end
renderNames()
if category == "\079\114\101\115" and pendingOreTeleportName
and currentName == pendingOreTeleportName then
local requestedOreName = pendingOreTeleportName
pendingOreTeleportName = nil
local pendingEntry = getNearestEntry("\079\114\101\115", requestedOreName)
if pendingEntry then
currentEntry = pendingEntry
updateSelectedText()
detailLabel.Text = "\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\073\078\071\032\084\079\032\078\069\065\082\069\083\084\032"
.. string.upper(requestedOreName) .. " ORE\n" .. tostring(pendingEntry.Path)
teleportEntry(pendingEntry)
else
currentEntry = nil
updateSelectedText()
detailLabel.Text = "\078\079\032\076\073\086\069\032" .. string.upper(requestedOreName)
.. " ORE INSTANCE FOUND\nCHECK THE ORES/MAP ROOT OR TRY AGAIN"
end
end
if farmStates[category] and farmStates[category].Enabled then
local farmState = farmStates[category]
if farmState.Target and not entryIsAvailable(farmState.Target) then
farmState.Target = nil
end
end
end
else
if not ok then
warn("\091\080\105\108\103\114\097\109\109\101\100\115\099\114\105\112\116\093\032\067\097\116\097\108\111\103\032\115\099\097\110\032\102\097\105\108\101\100\032\102\111\114\032" .. tostring(category) .. "\058\032" .. tostring(result))
end
end
scanning[category] = nil
if rescanQueued[category] and not browserDestroyed then
rescanQueued[category] = nil
refreshCategory(category, true)
end
end)
end
teleportEntry = function(entry)
if not entry then
detailLabel.Text = "\083\069\076\069\067\084\032\065\078\032\079\066\074\069\067\084\032\070\073\082\083\084"
return false
end
if entry.StaticAprilMachine then
local character = player.Character
if not character then
detailLabel.Text = "\067\072\065\082\065\067\084\069\082\032\078\079\084\032\082\069\065\068\089"
return false
end
local exactAprilMachineCFrame = CFrame.new(
734.885559, 9.5, -310.492462,
-0.866007447, 0, 0.500031412,
0, 1, 0,
-0.500031412, 0, -0.866007447
)
local ok, err = pcall(function() character:PivotTo(exactAprilMachineCFrame) end)
detailLabel.Text = ok and "\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\069\068\032\084\079\032\065\080\082\073\076\077\065\067\072\073\078\069" or ("\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\032\070\065\073\076\069\068\058\032" .. tostring(err))
return ok
end
local targetCFrame = getEntryTransform(entry)
if not targetCFrame then
detailLabel.Text = "\084\065\082\071\069\084\032\068\073\083\065\080\080\069\065\082\069\068\032\047\032\065\085\084\079\077\065\084\073\067\065\076\076\089\032\082\069\065\067\081\085\073\082\073\078\071"
currentEntry = nil
updateSelectedText()
refreshCategory(currentCategory, true)
return false
end
local character = player.Character
local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100")
if not character or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
detailLabel.Text = "\067\072\065\082\065\067\084\069\082\032\078\079\084\032\082\069\065\068\089"
return false
end
local destination = targetCFrame * CFrame.new(0, 5, 0)
local ok, err = pcall(function() character:PivotTo(destination) end)
if ok then
detailLabel.Text = "\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\069\068\032\084\079\032" .. tostring(entry.Name) .. "\n" .. tostring(entry.Path)
else
detailLabel.Text = "\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\032\070\065\073\076\069\068\058\032" .. tostring(err)
end
return ok
end
local function stopFarm(category)
local state = farmStates[category]
if not state then return end
state.Enabled = false
state.Token += 1
state.Target = nil
if currentCategory == category then
farmButton.Text = "\065\085\084\079\032" .. getFarmNoun(category) .. "\032\070\065\082\077\032\079\070\070"
setToggleVisual(farmButton, false)
end
end
getNearestEntry = function(category, name)
if typeof(name) ~= "\115\116\114\105\110\103" or name == "" then
return nil
end
local cached = categoryCache[category]
local list = cached and cached.ByName and cached.ByName[name] or {}
local character = player.Character
local rootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
local origin = rootPart and rootPart.Position or Vector3.zero
local best = nil
local bestDistance = math.huge
for _, entry in ipairs(list) do
if entryIsAvailable(entry) then
local target = getEntryTransform(entry)
local distance = target and (target.Position - origin).Magnitude or math.huge
if distance < bestDistance then
bestDistance = distance
best = entry
end
end
end
return best
end
local function startFarm(category)
local state = farmStates[category]
if not state then return end
if not currentName or currentName == "" then
detailLabel.Text = "\083\069\076\069\067\084\032\065\032" .. getFarmNoun(category) .. "\032\078\065\077\069\032\070\073\082\083\084"
return
end
if state.Enabled then
if state.Name ~= currentName then
state.Name = currentName
state.Target = nil
state.LastTargetObject = nil
refreshCategory(category, true)
end
detailLabel.Text = "\070\079\076\076\079\087\073\078\071\032\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068\032" .. getFarmNoun(category)
.. "\058\032" .. string.upper(state.Name)
return
end
state.Enabled = true
state.Name = currentName
state.Target = nil
state.LastTargetObject = nil
state.Token += 1
local token = state.Token
farmButton.Text = "\065\085\084\079\032" .. getFarmNoun(category) .. "\032\070\065\082\077\032\079\078"
setToggleVisual(farmButton, true)
refreshCategory(category, true)
task.spawn(function()
local lastBossRefresh = 0
local lastSearchStatus = 0
while not browserDestroyed and gui.Parent and state.Enabled and state.Token == token do
local lockedName = state.Name
if not lockedName or lockedName == "" then
state.Target = nil
state.LastTargetObject = nil
break
end
if state.Target and not entryIsAvailable(state.Target) then
state.Target = nil
state.LastTargetObject = nil
refreshCategory(category, true)
end
if not state.Target then
local nextEntry = getNearestEntry(category, lockedName)
if nextEntry then
state.Target = nextEntry
state.LastTargetObject = nextEntry.Object
if currentCategory == category then
currentEntry = nextEntry
updateSelectedText()
detailLabel.Text = "\070\079\085\078\068\032\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068\032" .. getFarmNoun(category)
.. "\058\032" .. string.upper(lockedName) .. "\032\047\032\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\073\078\071"
end
teleportEntry(nextEntry)
else
if os.clock() - lastSearchStatus >= 1.5 then
lastSearchStatus = os.clock()
if currentCategory == category then
detailLabel.Text = "\087\065\073\084\073\078\071\032\070\079\082\032\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068\032" .. getFarmNoun(category)
.. "\058\032" .. string.upper(lockedName)
.. "\032\047\032\087\073\076\076\032\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\032\087\072\069\078\032\073\084\032\082\069\083\080\065\087\078\083"
end
end
local refreshInterval = category == "\079\114\101\115" and 3 or 2.5
if os.clock() - lastBossRefresh >= refreshInterval then
lastBossRefresh = os.clock()
refreshCategory(category, true)
end
end
end
task.wait(category == "\079\114\101\115" and 0.6 or 0.4)
end
end)
end
local function toggleFarm()
local category = currentCategory
local state = farmStates[category]
if not state then return end
if state.Enabled then
stopFarm(category)
else
startFarm(category)
end
end
local function openSection(category)
currentCategory = category
catalogCurrentSection = category
local savedFarm = farmStates[category]
currentName = savedFarm and savedFarm.Enabled and savedFarm.Name or nil
currentEntry = nil
namePage = 1
instancePage = 1
searchBox.Text = ""
sectionTitle.Text = string.upper(category)
detailLabel.Text = "\067\076\073\067\075\032\065\032\078\065\077\069\032\079\082\032\073\078\083\084\065\078\067\069\032\084\079\032\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084"
local isLightingSection = category == "\076\105\103\104\116\105\110\103"
lightingButton.Visible = isLightingSection
lightingDescription.Visible = isLightingSection
namesTitle.Visible = not isLightingSection
nameList.Visible = not isLightingSection
instancesTitle.Visible = not isLightingSection
instanceList.Visible = not isLightingSection
selectedLabel.Visible = not isLightingSection
detailLabel.Position = isLightingSection and UDim2.fromOffset(10, 212) or UDim2.fromOffset(10, 510)
teleportButton.Visible = false
farmButton.Visible = false
if isLightingSection then
detailLabel.Text = "\076\079\067\065\076\032\067\076\073\069\078\084\032\083\069\084\084\073\078\071\032\047\032\067\076\073\067\075\032\065\080\080\076\089\032\084\079\032\067\076\069\065\082\032\076\073\071\072\084\073\078\071\032\067\072\073\076\068\032\073\078\083\084\065\078\067\069\083\032\065\078\068\032\070\079\082\067\069\032\068\065\089\084\073\077\069\032\087\073\084\072\032\078\079\032\070\079\071"
end
rootStatus.Text = isLightingSection and "\076\079\067\065\076\032\067\076\073\069\078\084\032\076\073\071\072\084\073\078\071\032\067\079\078\084\082\079\076" or (isFarmCategory(category)
and "\065\085\084\079\032\084\065\082\071\069\084\032\068\069\084\069\067\084\073\079\078\032\047\032\078\079\032\077\065\078\085\065\076\032\083\067\065\078"
or "\065\085\084\079\032\068\073\083\067\079\086\069\082\089\032\047\032\083\069\076\069\067\084\032\065\078\032\069\078\084\082\089")
selectedLabel.Text = "\084\065\082\071\069\084\058\032\078\079\078\069"
teleportButton.Text = isFarmCategory(category) and "\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\032\043\032\070\065\082\077" or "\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\032\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068"
teleportButton.Visible = false
farmButton.Visible = isFarmCategory(category)
farmButton.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(188, 354)
if isFarmCategory(category) then
local farmState = farmStates[category]
farmButton.Text = "\065\085\084\079\032" .. getFarmNoun(category)
.. "\032\070\065\082\077\032" .. (farmState.Enabled and "\079\078" or "\079\070\070")
setToggleVisual(farmButton, farmState.Enabled)
end
if category == "\076\111\099\097\116\105\111\110\115" or category == "\083\104\111\112\115" then
detailLabel.Text = category == "\076\111\099\097\116\105\111\110\115"
and "\076\079\067\065\084\073\079\078\083\032\065\082\069\032\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\032\068\069\083\084\073\078\065\084\073\079\078\083\044\032\078\079\084\032\067\079\077\066\065\084\032\084\065\082\071\069\084\083"
or "\083\072\079\080\083\032\065\082\069\032\078\080\067\032\068\069\083\084\073\078\065\084\073\079\078\083\044\032\078\079\084\032\067\079\077\066\065\084\032\084\065\082\071\069\084\083"
elseif category == "\079\114\101\082\111\117\116\101\115" then
detailLabel.Text = "\083\069\076\069\067\084\032\065\032\082\069\071\073\079\078\032\084\079\032\086\073\069\087\032\073\084\083\032\079\082\069\032\082\079\085\084\069\032\080\079\073\078\084\083"
elseif category == "\065\114\101\097\066\111\115\115\101\115" then
detailLabel.Text = "\083\084\065\084\073\067\032\065\082\069\065\045\066\079\083\083\032\068\069\083\084\073\078\065\084\073\079\078\083\032\047\032\083\069\076\069\067\084\032\065\032\078\065\077\069\032\084\079\032\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084"
elseif category == "\076\105\103\104\116\105\110\103" then
detailLabel.Text = "\076\079\067\065\076\032\067\076\073\069\078\084\032\083\069\084\084\073\078\071\032\047\032\067\076\073\067\075\032\065\080\080\076\089\032\084\079\032\067\076\069\065\082\032\076\073\071\072\084\073\078\071\032\067\072\073\076\068\032\073\078\083\084\065\078\067\069\083\032\065\078\068\032\070\079\082\067\069\032\068\065\089\084\073\077\069\032\087\073\084\072\032\078\079\032\070\079\071"
end
left.Visible = false
right.Visible = false
panel.Visible = true
for _, child in ipairs(nav:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("\084\101\120\116\066\117\116\116\111\110") then
child.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.Element
child.TextColor3 = COLORS.Gray
end
end
local activeButton = navigationButtons[category]
if activeButton then
activeButton.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.AccentSoft
activeButton.TextColor3 = COLORS.Accent
end
local state = categoryCache[category]
if state and not isFarmCategory(category) then
rootStatus.Text = state.RootSummary
else
rootStatus.Text = isFarmCategory(category)
and "\065\085\084\079\032\084\065\082\071\069\084\032\068\069\084\069\067\084\073\079\078\032\047\032\076\079\065\068\073\078\071\032\076\073\086\069\032\073\078\083\084\065\078\067\069\083"
or "\065\085\084\079\032\068\073\083\067\079\086\069\082\089\032\047\032\076\079\065\068\073\078\071"
end
renderNames()
refreshCategory(category, true)
end
local function openAutomation()
catalogCurrentSection = "\065\117\116\111\109\097\116\105\111\110"
currentCategory = "\065\117\116\111\109\097\116\105\111\110"
panel.Visible = false
left.Visible = true
right.Visible = true
for _, child in ipairs(nav:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("\084\101\120\116\066\117\116\116\111\110") then
child.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.Element
child.TextColor3 = COLORS.Gray
end
end
local activeButton = navigationButtons.Automation
if activeButton then
activeButton.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.AccentSoft
activeButton.TextColor3 = COLORS.Accent
end
end
local visibleNavigationOrder = 0
for _, definition in ipairs(definitions) do
if not hiddenNavigationKeys[definition.Key] then
visibleNavigationOrder += 1
local button = makeButton(nav, "\083\101\099\116\105\111\110\084\097\098", definition.Label,
UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0))
button.LayoutOrder = visibleNavigationOrder
button.ZIndex = 31
button.TextSize = 9
button.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
create("\085\073\080\097\100\100\105\110\103", {PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10)}, button)
navigationButtons[definition.Key] = button
button.Activated:Connect(function()
openSection(definition.Key)
end)
end
end
local automationButton = makeButton(nav, "\065\117\116\111\109\097\116\105\111\110\084\097\098", "\065\085\084\079\077\065\084\073\079\078",
UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30), UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0))
automationButton.LayoutOrder = #definitions + 1
automationButton.ZIndex = 31
automationButton.TextSize = 9
automationButton.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
create("\085\073\080\097\100\100\105\110\103", {PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10)}, automationButton)
navigationButtons.Automation = automationButton
automationButton.Activated:Connect(openAutomation)
searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("\084\101\120\116"):Connect(function()
namePage = 1
renderNames()
end)
teleportButton.Activated:Connect(function()
if isFarmCategory(currentCategory) then
if not currentName or currentName == "" then
detailLabel.Text = "\083\069\076\069\067\084\032\065\032" .. getFarmNoun(currentCategory) .. "\032\070\073\082\083\084"
return
end
startFarm(currentCategory)
else
teleportEntry(currentEntry)
end
end)
farmButton.Activated:Connect(toggleFarm)
connectGlobal(workspace.ChildAdded, function(child)
if browserDestroyed then return end
for category in pairs(visitedSections) do
if category == "\087\111\114\107\115\112\097\099\101" then
scheduleCategoryRefresh("\087\111\114\107\115\112\097\099\101")
end
local definition = sectionByKey[category]
for _, rootName in ipairs(definition.Roots or {}) do
if child.Name == rootName then
scheduleCategoryRefresh(category)
break
end
end
for _, rootName in ipairs(definition.FallbackRoots or {}) do
if child.Name == rootName then
scheduleCategoryRefresh(category)
break
end
end
end
end)
connectGlobal(workspace.ChildRemoved, function(child)
if browserDestroyed then return end
for category in pairs(visitedSections) do
if category == "\087\111\114\107\115\112\097\099\101" then
scheduleCategoryRefresh("\087\111\114\107\115\112\097\099\101")
end
local definition = sectionByKey[category]
for _, rootName in ipairs(definition.Roots or {}) do
if child.Name == rootName then
scheduleCategoryRefresh(category)
break
end
end
for _, rootName in ipairs(definition.FallbackRoots or {}) do
if child.Name == rootName then
scheduleCategoryRefresh(category)
break
end
end
end
end)
connectGlobal(UserInputService.InputBegan, function(input, gameProcessed)
if gameProcessed or browserDestroyed then return end
if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.K then
gui.Enabled = not gui.Enabled
end
end)
gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
browserDestroyed = true
for category in pairs(farmStates) do
stopFarm(category)
end
end)
openSection("\067\104\101\115\116\115")
end
local function startLogic()
local NPC_FOLDER_NAMES = {
npc = true,
npcs = true,
nonplayercharacters = true,
}
local function isNPCFolderName(name)
return NPC_FOLDER_NAMES[string.lower(name)] == true
end
local npcFolderCache = nil
local npcFolderCheckedAt = -math.huge
local function findNPCFolder()
if npcFolderCache and npcFolderCache.Parent then
return npcFolderCache
end
if os.clock() - npcFolderCheckedAt < 8 then
return nil
end
npcFolderCheckedAt = os.clock()
npcFolderCache = nil
for _, candidateName in ipairs({"\078\080\067\115", "\078\080\067", "\078\111\110\080\108\097\121\101\114\067\104\097\114\097\099\116\101\114\115"}) do
local candidate = workspace:FindFirstChild(candidateName)
if candidate and (candidate:IsA("\070\111\108\100\101\114") or candidate:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108")) then
npcFolderCache = candidate
break
end
end
return npcFolderCache
end
local function getFolder(name)
if name == "\078\080\067\115" then
return findNPCFolder()
end
local candidates = name == "\077\111\098\115" and {"\077\111\098\115", "\069\110\101\109\105\101\115"} or {name}
for _, candidateName in ipairs(candidates) do
local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild(candidateName)
if folder and (folder:IsA("\070\111\108\100\101\114") or folder:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108")) then
return folder
end
end
return nil
end
local function isNPCModel(model)
if not model or not model:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") then
return false
end
if model:GetAttribute("\073\115\078\080\067") == true
or model:GetAttribute("\078\080\067") == true
or model:GetAttribute("\078\111\110\080\108\097\121\101\114\067\104\097\114\097\099\116\101\114") == true then
return true
end
for _, tagName in ipairs({"\078\080\067", "\078\080\067\115", "\078\111\110\080\108\097\121\101\114\067\104\097\114\097\099\116\101\114"}) do
local tagged = false
pcall(function()
tagged = CollectionService:HasTag(model, tagName)
end)
if tagged then
return true
end
end
local ancestor = model.Parent
while ancestor and ancestor ~= workspace do
if ancestor:IsA("\070\111\108\100\101\114") and isNPCFolderName(ancestor.Name) then
return true
end
ancestor = ancestor.Parent
end
return false
end
local function getTeleportPart(model)
if not model then
return nil
end
if model:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
return model
end
if not model:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") then
return nil
end
if model.PrimaryPart and model.PrimaryPart:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
return model.PrimaryPart
end
return model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116", true)
end
local function getHumanoid(model)
if not model then
return nil
end
return model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100", true)
end
local function isAlive(model, humanoid)
if not model or not model.Parent then
return false
end
if humanoid and humanoid.Parent and humanoid.Health <= 0 then
return false
end
return true
end
local function newEmptyCache()
return {
ModelsByFolder = {
Mobs = {},
NPCs = {},
},
ByName = {
Mobs = {},
NPCs = {},
},
Category = {},
Groups = {},
Signature = "",
}
end
local modelCache = newEmptyCache()
local partCache = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "\107"})
local humanoidCache = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "\107"})
local rootEntries = {}
local function getCachedPart(model)
local part = partCache[model]
if part and part ~= model and not part:IsDescendantOf(model) then
part = nil
end
if not part then
part = getTeleportPart(model)
partCache[model] = part or nil
end
return part
end
local function getCachedHumanoid(model)
local humanoid = humanoidCache[model]
if humanoid == nil or (humanoid and not humanoid.Parent) then
humanoid = getHumanoid(model) or false
humanoidCache[model] = humanoid
end
return humanoid or nil
end
local function updateWorkspaceStatus()
for name, label in pairs(folderStatuses) do
local available = getFolder(name) ~= nil
if name == "\078\080\067\115" and not available then
local npcModels = modelCache.ModelsByFolder.NPCs
available = npcModels ~= nil and #npcModels > 0
end
label.Text = name .. (available and "\058\032\065\086\065\073\076\065\066\076\069" or "\058\032\077\073\083\083\073\078\071")
label.TextColor3 = COLORS.White
end
end
local function getScanRoots()
local roots = {}
local seen = {}
local function addRoot(instance, category)
if instance and not seen[instance] then
seen[instance] = true
table.insert(roots, {
Root = instance,
Category = category,
Fast = false,
})
end
end
addRoot(workspace:FindFirstChild("\077\111\098\115"), "\077\111\098\115")
addRoot(workspace:FindFirstChild("\069\110\101\109\105\101\115"), "\077\111\098\115")
addRoot(findNPCFolder(), "\078\080\067\115")
return roots
end
local function scanRoot(root, rootCategory)
local out = {}
local visited = 0
local function walk(container)
for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
visited += 1
if visited % 300 == 0 then
task.wait()
end
if child:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") then
if not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(child) then
local shouldIndex = true
if rootCategory == "\077\111\098\115" then
shouldIndex = child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100", true) ~= nil
elseif rootCategory == "\078\080\067\115" then
shouldIndex = child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100", true) ~= nil
or child:GetAttribute("\073\115\078\080\067") == true
or child:GetAttribute("\078\080\067") == true
end
if shouldIndex then
table.insert(out, {
Object = child,
Category = rootCategory,
})
else
walk(child)
end
end
elseif child:IsA("\070\111\108\100\101\114") or child:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") then
walk(child)
end
end
end
if root:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") then
if rootCategory == "\077\111\098\115" or rootCategory == "\078\080\067\115" then
if root:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100", true) then
table.insert(out, {Object = root, Category = rootCategory})
else
walk(root)
end
end
else
walk(root)
end
return out
end
local function refreshModelCache(forceFull)
if scanBusy then
return
end
scanBusy = true
local success, err = pcall(function()
local roots = getScanRoots()
local active = {}
for _, info in ipairs(roots) do
active[info.Root] = true
if forceFull or rootEntries[info.Root] == nil then
rootEntries[info.Root] = scanRoot(info.Root, info.Category)
end
end
for root in pairs(rootEntries) do
if not active[root] then
rootEntries[root] = nil
end
end
local newCache = newEmptyCache()
local seen = {}
for _, info in ipairs(roots) do
local entries = rootEntries[info.Root]
if entries then
for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
local object = entry.Object
if object.Parent and not seen[object] then
seen[object] = true
local category = entry.Category
newCache.Category[object] = category
local list = newCache.ModelsByFolder[category]
if not list then
list = {}
newCache.ModelsByFolder[category] = list
newCache.ByName[category] = {}
end
table.insert(list, object)
local byName = newCache.ByName[category]
local group = byName[object.Name]
if not group then
group = {}
byName[object.Name] = group
end
table.insert(group, object)
end
end
end
end
for category, byName in pairs(newCache.ByName) do
for name in pairs(byName) do
table.insert(newCache.Groups, {
Folder = category,
FolderLower = string.lower(category),
Name = name,
Lower = string.lower(name),
})
end
end
table.sort(newCache.Groups, function(a, b)
if a.Lower == b.Lower then
return a.Folder < b.Folder
end
return a.Lower < b.Lower
end)
local keys = table.create(#newCache.Groups)
for index, group in ipairs(newCache.Groups) do
keys[index] = group.Folder .. "\124" .. group.Name
end
newCache.Signature = table.concat(keys, "\n")
modelCache = newCache
end)
scanBusy = false
if not success then
warn("\091\080\105\108\103\114\097\109\109\101\100\115\099\114\105\112\116\093\032\083\099\097\110\032\102\097\105\108\101\100\058\032" .. tostring(err))
end
end
local function isCachedModelUsable(model, folderName)
if not model or not model.Parent then
return false
end
if modelCache.Category[model] ~= folderName then
return false
end
if not model:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
return false
end
if not getCachedPart(model) then
return false
end
return isAlive(model, getCachedHumanoid(model))
end
local function getGroupModels(folderName, modelName)
local byName = modelCache.ByName[folderName]
local candidates = byName and byName[modelName]
if not candidates then
return {}
end
local results = {}
for _, model in ipairs(candidates) do
if isCachedModelUsable(model, folderName) then
table.insert(results, model)
end
end
return results
end
local function countAvailable(folderName, modelName)
local byName = modelCache.ByName[folderName]
local candidates = byName and byName[modelName]
if not candidates then
return 0
end
local count = 0
for _, model in ipairs(candidates) do
if model.Parent then
local humanoid = getCachedHumanoid(model)
if not humanoid or humanoid.Health > 0 then
count = count + 1
end
end
end
return count
end
local function getModelPosition(model)
local part = getCachedPart(model)
if part then
return part.Position
end
return model:GetPivot().Position
end
local function findNearestLiveModel(folderName, modelName)
local models = getGroupModels(folderName, modelName)
if #models == 0 then
return nil
end
local character = player.Character
local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
local origin = root and root.Position or Vector3.zero
local closestModel = nil
local closestDistance = math.huge
for _, model in ipairs(models) do
local distance = (getModelPosition(model) - origin).Magnitude
if distance < closestDistance then
closestDistance = distance
closestModel = model
end
end
return closestModel
end
local AUTO_FARM_METHODS = {
{Id = "\078\069\065\082\069\083\084", Label = "\078\069\065\082\069\083\084"},
{Id = "\076\079\087\069\083\084\095\072\080", Label = "\076\079\087\069\083\084\032\072\080"},
{Id = "\072\073\071\072\069\083\084\095\072\080", Label = "\072\073\071\072\069\083\084\032\072\080"},
{Id = "\070\065\082\084\072\069\083\084", Label = "\070\065\082\084\072\069\083\084"},
{Id = "\082\065\078\068\079\077", Label = "\082\065\078\068\079\077"},
{Id = "\067\089\067\076\069", Label = "\067\089\067\076\069"},
{Id = "\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068\095\078\065\077\069", Label = "\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068"},
}
local function getAutoFarmCandidates(maxDistance)
local character = player.Character
local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
local origin = root and root.Position
or (character and character:GetPivot().Position)
or Vector3.zero
local radius = maxDistance or SETTINGS.AutoFarmRadius
local candidates = {}
local selectedName = nil
if SETTINGS.AutoFarmMethod == "\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068\095\078\065\077\069" then
if selectedTarget and selectedTarget.Folder == "\077\111\098\115" then
selectedName = selectedTarget.Name
else
return candidates
end
end
for _, model in ipairs(modelCache.ModelsByFolder.Mobs or {}) do
if isCachedModelUsable(model, "\077\111\098\115")
and (not selectedName or model.Name == selectedName) then
local position = getModelPosition(model)
local distance = (position - origin).Magnitude
if distance <= radius then
local humanoid = getCachedHumanoid(model)
local healthRatio = 1
if humanoid and humanoid.MaxHealth > 0 then
healthRatio = math.clamp(humanoid.Health / humanoid.MaxHealth, 0, 1)
end
table.insert(candidates, {
Model = model,
Distance = distance,
HealthRatio = healthRatio,
Health = humanoid and humanoid.Health or math.huge,
})
end
end
end
return candidates
end
local function findAutoFarmTarget(maxDistance)
local candidates = getAutoFarmCandidates(maxDistance)
if #candidates == 0 then
return nil
end
local method = SETTINGS.AutoFarmMethod
local chosen = nil
if method == "\082\065\078\068\079\077" then
chosen = candidates[math.random(1, #candidates)]
elseif method == "\076\079\087\069\083\084\095\072\080" then
table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
if a.HealthRatio == b.HealthRatio then
return a.Distance < b.Distance
end
return a.HealthRatio < b.HealthRatio
end)
chosen = candidates[1]
elseif method == "\072\073\071\072\069\083\084\095\072\080" then
table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
if a.HealthRatio == b.HealthRatio then
return a.Distance < b.Distance
end
return a.HealthRatio > b.HealthRatio
end)
chosen = candidates[1]
elseif method == "\070\065\082\084\072\069\083\084" then
table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
return a.Distance > b.Distance
end)
chosen = candidates[1]
elseif method == "\067\089\067\076\069" then
table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
local aName = a.Model:GetFullName()
local bName = b.Model:GetFullName()
if aName == bName then
return a.Distance < b.Distance
end
return aName < bName
end)
farmCycleIndex = (farmCycleIndex % #candidates) + 1
chosen = candidates[farmCycleIndex]
else
table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
return a.Distance < b.Distance
end)
chosen = candidates[1]
end
if chosen then
farmCycleLastModel = chosen.Model
return chosen.Model
end
return nil
end
local function findNearestLiveMob(maxDistance)
local previousMethod = SETTINGS.AutoFarmMethod
SETTINGS.AutoFarmMethod = "\078\069\065\082\069\083\084"
local model = findAutoFarmTarget(maxDistance)
SETTINGS.AutoFarmMethod = previousMethod
return model
end
local function containsChestWord(value)
local lower = string.lower(tostring(value or ""))
return string.find(lower, "\099\104\101\115\116", 1, true) ~= nil
or string.find(lower, "\116\114\101\097\115\117\114\101", 1, true) ~= nil
or string.find(lower, "\108\111\111\116", 1, true) ~= nil
end
local function isChestGPrompt(prompt)
if not prompt
or not prompt:IsA("\080\114\111\120\105\109\105\116\121\080\114\111\109\112\116")
or not prompt.Parent
or not prompt.Enabled
or prompt.KeyboardKeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.G then
return false
end
local context = prompt:GetFullName() .. "\032"
.. tostring(prompt.ActionText) .. "\032"
.. tostring(prompt.ObjectText)
return containsChestWord(context)
end
local chestPromptCache = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "\107"})
local chestPromptRoots = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "\107"})
local CHEST_ROOT_NAMES = {
GoldChests = true,
Chests = true,
ChestSpawns = true,
}
local function indexChestPromptRoot(root)
if not root or not root.Parent or chestPromptRoots[root] then
return
end
chestPromptRoots[root] = true
connectGlobal(root.DescendantAdded, function(instance)
if instance:IsA("\080\114\111\120\105\109\105\116\121\080\114\111\109\112\116") then
chestPromptCache[instance] = true
end
end)
connectGlobal(root.DescendantRemoving, function(instance)
if instance:IsA("\080\114\111\120\105\109\105\116\121\080\114\111\109\112\116") then
chestPromptCache[instance] = nil
chestPromptCooldowns[instance] = nil
end
end)
local stack = {root}
local visited = 0
while #stack > 0 and not destroyed and gui.Parent do
local container = table.remove(stack)
for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
visited += 1
if child:IsA("\080\114\111\120\105\109\105\116\121\080\114\111\109\112\116") then
chestPromptCache[child] = true
end
if #child:GetChildren() > 0 then
table.insert(stack, child)
end
if visited % 300 == 0 then
task.wait()
end
end
end
end
for rootName in pairs(CHEST_ROOT_NAMES) do
local root = workspace:FindFirstChild(rootName)
if root and (root:IsA("\070\111\108\100\101\114") or root:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108")) then
task.spawn(indexChestPromptRoot, root)
end
end
connectGlobal(workspace.ChildAdded, function(child)
if CHEST_ROOT_NAMES[child.Name]
and (child:IsA("\070\111\108\100\101\114") or child:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108")) then
task.spawn(indexChestPromptRoot, child)
end
end)
connectGlobal(workspace.ChildRemoved, function(child)
if CHEST_ROOT_NAMES[child.Name] then
for prompt in pairs(chestPromptCache) do
if not prompt.Parent or prompt:IsDescendantOf(child) then
chestPromptCache[prompt] = nil
chestPromptCooldowns[prompt] = nil
end
end
end
end)
local gKeyPromptCache = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "\107"})
local function indexGKeyPrompt(instance)
if instance and instance:IsA("\080\114\111\120\105\109\105\116\121\080\114\111\109\112\116") then
gKeyPromptCache[instance] = true
end
end
for _, instance in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
indexGKeyPrompt(instance)
end
connectGlobal(workspace.DescendantAdded, indexGKeyPrompt)
connectGlobal(workspace.DescendantRemoving, function(instance)
if instance:IsA("\080\114\111\120\105\109\105\116\121\080\114\111\109\112\116") then
gKeyPromptCache[instance] = nil
chestPromptCooldowns[instance] = nil
end
end)
local function isGKeyPrompt(prompt)
return prompt ~= nil
and prompt:IsA("\080\114\111\120\105\109\105\116\121\080\114\111\109\112\116")
and prompt.Parent ~= nil
and prompt.Enabled
and prompt.KeyboardKeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.G
end
local function getPromptWorldPosition(prompt)
if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then
return nil
end
local parent = prompt.Parent
if parent:IsA("\065\116\116\097\099\104\109\101\110\116") then
return parent.WorldPosition
end
if parent:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
return parent.Position
end
local ancestor = parent
while ancestor and ancestor ~= workspace do
if ancestor:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
return ancestor.Position
elseif ancestor:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") then
local success, pivot = pcall(function()
return ancestor:GetPivot()
end)
if success then
return pivot.Position
end
end
ancestor = ancestor.Parent
end
return nil
end
local function findChestGPrompt(maxDistance, requireActivationRange)
local character = player.Character
local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
if not root then
return nil, math.huge
end
local closestPrompt = nil
local closestDistance = math.huge
for prompt in pairs(chestPromptCache) do
if isChestGPrompt(prompt)
and (chestPromptCooldowns[prompt] or 0) <= os.clock() then
local position = getPromptWorldPosition(prompt)
if position then
local distance = (position - root.Position).Magnitude
local allowedDistance = maxDistance or math.huge
if requireActivationRange then
allowedDistance = math.min(
allowedDistance,
math.max(0, prompt.MaxActivationDistance)
)
end
if distance <= allowedDistance and distance < closestDistance then
closestPrompt = prompt
closestDistance = distance
end
end
end
end
return closestPrompt, closestDistance
end
local function findAnyGKeyPrompt()
local character = player.Character
local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
if not root then
return nil, math.huge
end
local closestPrompt = nil
local closestDistance = math.huge
for prompt in pairs(gKeyPromptCache) do
if isGKeyPrompt(prompt)
and (chestPromptCooldowns[prompt] or 0) <= os.clock() then
local position = getPromptWorldPosition(prompt)
if position then
local distance = (position - root.Position).Magnitude
local activationDistance = math.max(0, prompt.MaxActivationDistance)
if distance <= activationDistance and distance < closestDistance then
closestPrompt = prompt
closestDistance = distance
end
end
end
end
return closestPrompt, closestDistance
end
local function moveCharacterNearChestPrompt(prompt)
if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then
return false
end
local character = player.Character
local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100")
local promptPosition = getPromptWorldPosition(prompt)
if not character or not root or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 or not promptPosition then
return false
end
local maxDistance = math.max(1, prompt.MaxActivationDistance)
local currentDistance = (root.Position - promptPosition).Magnitude
if currentDistance <= math.max(1, maxDistance - 0.5) then
return true
end
if not lastReturnPivot then
lastReturnPivot = character:GetPivot()
returnButton.Active = true
end
local horizontalOffset = Vector3.new(
root.Position.X - promptPosition.X,
0,
root.Position.Z - promptPosition.Z
)
if horizontalOffset.Magnitude < 0.05 then
horizontalOffset = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
else
horizontalOffset = horizontalOffset.Unit
end
local standOff = math.clamp(maxDistance * 0.45, 2, 4)
local destinationPosition = promptPosition
+ horizontalOffset * standOff
+ Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
local destination = CFrame.lookAt(destinationPosition, promptPosition)
local rootToPivot = root.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(character:GetPivot())
local success = pcall(function()
character:PivotTo(destination * rootToPivot)
root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
end)
return success
end
local function activateChestGPrompt(prompt)
if autoChestBusy or not isChestGPrompt(prompt) then
return false
end
local character = player.Character
local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
local promptPosition = getPromptWorldPosition(prompt)
if not root or not promptPosition then
return false
end
local allowedDistance = math.max(0, prompt.MaxActivationDistance)
if (root.Position - promptPosition).Magnitude > allowedDistance then
return false
end
autoChestBusy = true
local began = pcall(function()
prompt:InputHoldBegin()
end)
if began then
local holdTime = math.clamp(prompt.HoldDuration, 0, 5)
if holdTime > 0 then
task.wait(holdTime)
else
task.wait(0.08)
end
if prompt.Parent then
pcall(function()
prompt:InputHoldEnd()
end)
end
chestPromptCooldowns[prompt] = os.clock() + SETTINGS.AutoChestPromptCooldown
end
autoChestBusy = false
return began
end
local function activateAnyGKeyPrompt(prompt)
if autoChestBusy or not isGKeyPrompt(prompt) then
return false
end
local character = player.Character
local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
local position = getPromptWorldPosition(prompt)
if not root or not position then
return false
end
if (root.Position - position).Magnitude > math.max(0, prompt.MaxActivationDistance) then
return false
end
autoChestBusy = true
local began = pcall(function()
prompt:InputHoldBegin()
end)
if began then
local holdTime = math.clamp(prompt.HoldDuration, 0, 5)
task.wait(holdTime > 0 and holdTime or 0.08)
if prompt.Parent then
pcall(function()
prompt:InputHoldEnd()
end)
end
chestPromptCooldowns[prompt] = os.clock() + SETTINGS.AutoChestPromptCooldown
end
autoChestBusy = false
return began
end
local function resolveSelectedModel(target)
if not target then
return nil
end
local current = target.CurrentModel
if current and isCachedModelUsable(current, target.Folder) then
return current
end
target.CurrentModel = nil
if target.Folder ~= "\078\080\067\115" and SETTINGS.AutoNextMob then
target.CurrentModel = findNearestLiveModel(target.Folder, target.Name)
end
return target.CurrentModel
end
local function getMobForward(model)
local part = model:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116", true)
or model:FindFirstChild("\085\112\112\101\114\084\111\114\115\111", true)
or model:FindFirstChild("\084\111\114\115\111", true)
or model:FindFirstChild("\072\101\097\100", true)
or model.PrimaryPart
if not part or not part:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
return Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
end
local forward = part.CFrame.LookVector
forward = Vector3.new(forward.X, 0, forward.Z)
if forward.Magnitude < 0.01 then
return Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
end
return forward.Unit
end
local function getMobUnderCFrame(model)
if not model or not isCachedModelUsable(model, "\077\111\098\115") then
return nil
end
local centerPosition
local halfHeight
if model:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
local cf = model.CFrame
local size = model.Size
centerPosition = cf.Position
halfHeight = (
math.abs(cf.RightVector.Y) * size.X
+ math.abs(cf.UpVector.Y) * size.Y
+ math.abs(cf.LookVector.Y) * size.Z
) / 2
else
local success, boxCFrame, boxSize = pcall(function()
return model:GetBoundingBox()
end)
if not success then
return nil
end
centerPosition = boxCFrame.Position
halfHeight = boxSize.Y / 2
end
local targetPosition
local faceDirection
if farmPositionMode == "\085\080\080\069\082" then
targetPosition = Vector3.new(
centerPosition.X,
centerPosition.Y + halfHeight + SETTINGS.MobUnderDistance,
centerPosition.Z
)
faceDirection = Vector3.new(0, -1, 0)
else
targetPosition = Vector3.new(
centerPosition.X,
centerPosition.Y - halfHeight - SETTINGS.MobUnderDistance,
centerPosition.Z
)
faceDirection = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
end
local mobForward = getMobForward(model)
return CFrame.lookAt(
targetPosition,
targetPosition + faceDirection,
mobForward
)
end
local function getMobOrbitCFrame(model)
local baseCFrame = getMobUnderCFrame(model)
if not baseCFrame then
return nil
end
local centerPosition
if model:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
centerPosition = model.Position
else
local success, boxCFrame = pcall(function()
local box, _ = model:GetBoundingBox()
return box
end)
if not success or not boxCFrame then
return nil
end
centerPosition = boxCFrame.Position
end
local angle = os.clock() * math.rad(SETTINGS.MobOrbitSpeed)
local radius = SETTINGS.MobOrbitRadius
local orbitPosition = Vector3.new(
centerPosition.X + math.cos(angle) * radius,
baseCFrame.Position.Y,
centerPosition.Z + math.sin(angle) * radius
)
local inward = Vector3.new(
centerPosition.X - orbitPosition.X,
0,
centerPosition.Z - orbitPosition.Z
)
if inward.Magnitude < 0.001 then
inward = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
else
inward = inward.Unit
end
local faceDirection = farmPositionMode == "\085\080\080\069\082"
and Vector3.new(0, -1, 0)
or Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
return CFrame.lookAt(
orbitPosition,
orbitPosition + faceDirection,
inward
)
end
local function getMobFollowCFrame(model)
if orbitEnabled then
return getMobOrbitCFrame(model)
end
return getMobUnderCFrame(model)
end
local function getObjectTopCFrame(object, clearance)
if not object then
return nil
end
local centerPosition
local halfHeight
if object:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
local cf = object.CFrame
local size = object.Size
centerPosition = cf.Position
halfHeight = (
math.abs(cf.RightVector.Y) * size.X
+ math.abs(cf.UpVector.Y) * size.Y
+ math.abs(cf.LookVector.Y) * size.Z
) / 2
else
local success, boxCFrame, boxSize = pcall(function()
return object:GetBoundingBox()
end)
if not success then
return nil
end
centerPosition = boxCFrame.Position
halfHeight = boxSize.Y / 2
end
local topPosition = Vector3.new(
centerPosition.X,
centerPosition.Y + halfHeight + clearance,
centerPosition.Z
)
local character = player.Character
local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
local forward = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
if root then
local look = root.CFrame.LookVector
local flat = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)
if flat.Magnitude >= 0.01 then
forward = flat.Unit
end
end
return CFrame.lookAt(topPosition, topPosition + forward)
end
local function clearESP()
for model, highlight in pairs(espHighlights) do
if highlight then
highlight:Destroy()
end
espHighlights[model] = nil
end
end
local function createESP(model)
if espHighlights[model] then
return espHighlights[model]
end
local highlight = Instance.new("\072\105\103\104\108\105\103\104\116")
highlight.Name = "\076\111\099\097\108\077\111\098\069\083\080"
highlight.Adornee = model
highlight.FillTransparency = 1
highlight.OutlineColor = COLORS.White
highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
highlight.Parent = model
espHighlights[model] = highlight
return highlight
end
local function updateESP()
if destroyed or not espEnabled then
clearESP()
return
end
local character = player.Character
local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
if not root then
clearESP()
return
end
local candidates = {}
local mobModels = modelCache.ModelsByFolder.Mobs or {}
for _, model in ipairs(mobModels) do
if isCachedModelUsable(model, "\077\111\098\115") then
local distance = (getModelPosition(model) - root.Position).Magnitude
if distance <= SETTINGS.ESPRadius then
table.insert(candidates, {
Model = model,
Distance = distance,
})
end
end
end
table.sort(candidates, function(a, b)
return a.Distance < b.Distance
end)
local wanted = {}
local amount = math.min(#candidates, SETTINGS.MaxESPHighlights)
for index = 1, amount do
local model = candidates[index].Model
wanted[model] = true
createESP(model)
end
for model, highlight in pairs(espHighlights) do
if not wanted[model] then
highlight:Destroy()
espHighlights[model] = nil
end
end
end
local function getEquippedTool()
local character = player.Character
if not character then
return nil
end
for _, object in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
if object:IsA("\084\111\111\108") and object.Enabled then
return object
end
end
return nil
end
local function deactivateEquippedTool()
local tool = getEquippedTool()
if tool then
pcall(function()
tool:Deactivate()
end)
end
end

return

local function applyMobUnderDistance()

if not value or value ~= value or value == math.huge or value == -math.huge then
mobDistanceBox.Text = tostring(SETTINGS.MobUnderDistance)
feedbackLabel.Text = "\073\078\086\065\076\073\068\032\077\079\066\032\068\073\083\084\065\078\067\069"
return
end
value = math.clamp(value, 0, 100)
SETTINGS.MobUnderDistance = value
mobDistanceBox.Text = tostring(value)
mobDistanceDescription.Text = "\071\065\080\058\032" .. tostring(value) .. "\032\083\084\085\068\083"
feedbackLabel.Text = "\077\079\066\032\071\065\080\032\085\080\068\065\084\069\068\058\032" .. tostring(value) .. "\032\083\084\085\068\083"
backFollowDescription.Text = backFollowEnabled
and ("\083\084\065\089\032" .. tostring(value) .. "\032\083\084\085\068\083\032" .. (farmPositionMode == "\085\080\080\069\082" and "\065\066\079\086\069" or "\085\078\068\069\082"))
or "\085\078\068\069\082\032\070\079\076\076\079\087\032\068\073\083\065\066\076\069\068"
end
applyMobDistanceButton.Activated:Connect(applyMobUnderDistance)
mobDistanceBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
if enterPressed then
applyMobUnderDistance()
end
end)

local function setCameraNoclipEnabled(enabled)
enabled = enabled == true
local success, err = pcall(function()
player.DevCameraOcclusionMode = enabled
and Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Invisicam
or (originalCameraOcclusionMode or Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Zoom)
end)
if not success then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\067\065\077\069\082\065\032\078\079\067\076\073\080\032\085\078\065\086\065\073\076\065\066\076\069\058\032" .. tostring(err)
return

SETTINGS.CameraNoclipEnabled = enabled

and "\067\065\077\069\082\065\032\078\079\067\076\073\080\032\069\078\065\066\076\069\068"
or "\067\065\077\069\082\065\032\078\079\067\076\073\080\032\068\073\083\065\066\076\069\068"
