setCameraNoclipEnabled(not cameraNoclipEnabled)
end)
local noclipConnection = nil
local noclipOriginal = {}
local function stopNoclip()
if noclipConnection then
noclipConnection:Disconnect()
noclipConnection = nil
end
for part, original in pairs(noclipOriginal) do
if part.Parent then
part.CanCollide = original
end
end
noclipOriginal = {}
end
local function startNoclip(character)
stopNoclip()
for _, object in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
if object:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then
noclipOriginal[object] = object.CanCollide
end
end
noclipConnection = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
for part in pairs(noclipOriginal) do
if part.Parent then
part.CanCollide = false
end
end
end)
end
local function zeroCharacterVelocity(character)
local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
if root then
root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
end
end
local function captureCharacterState(character, humanoid, root)
local animateScript = character:FindFirstChild("\065\110\105\109\097\116\101")
local saved = {
Character = character,
Humanoid = humanoid,
Root = root,
Anchored = root.Anchored,
PlatformStand = humanoid.PlatformStand,
AutoRotate = humanoid.AutoRotate,
AnimateScript = animateScript,
AnimateDisabled = nil,
RootToPivot = root.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(character:GetPivot()),
AnimationSpeeds = {},
}
if animateScript and animateScript:IsA("\066\097\115\101\083\099\114\105\112\116") then
saved.AnimateDisabled = animateScript.Disabled
animateScript.Disabled = true
end
for _, track in ipairs(humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
local success, speed = pcall(function()
return track.Speed
end)
if success then
table.insert(saved.AnimationSpeeds, {
Track = track,
Speed = speed,
})
pcall(function()
track:AdjustSpeed(0)
end)
end
end
humanoid.AutoRotate = false
humanoid.PlatformStand = true
humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
startNoclip(character)
return saved
end
local function restoreCharacterState(saved, standUp)
if not saved then
return
end
stopNoclip()
local root = saved.Root
local humanoid = saved.Humanoid
local character = saved.Character
if standUp and character and character.Parent and root and root.Parent then
local forward = root.CFrame.LookVector
forward = Vector3.new(forward.X, 0, forward.Z)
if forward.Magnitude < 0.01 then
forward = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
else
forward = forward.Unit
end
local uprightRoot = CFrame.lookAt(
root.Position,
root.Position + forward
)
character:PivotTo(uprightRoot * saved.RootToPivot)
end
if root and root.Parent then
root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
root.Anchored = saved.Anchored
end
if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then
humanoid.AutoRotate = saved.AutoRotate
humanoid.PlatformStand = saved.PlatformStand
local animateScript = saved.AnimateScript
if animateScript and animateScript.Parent and saved.AnimateDisabled ~= nil then
animateScript.Disabled = saved.AnimateDisabled
end
for _, info in ipairs(saved.AnimationSpeeds) do
if info.Track and info.Track.IsPlaying then
pcall(function()
info.Track:AdjustSpeed(info.Speed)
end)
end
end
if humanoid.Health > 0 then
humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
end
end
if activeSavedState == saved then
activeSavedState = nil
end
end
stopMount = function()
local state = mountState
if not state then
return
end
mountState = nil
if mountConnection then
mountConnection:Disconnect()
mountConnection = nil
end
restoreCharacterState(state.SavedState, true)
end
local function startMobMount(model, savedState, autoFarmMode)
if mountState then
stopMount()
end
local character = savedState.Character
local humanoid = savedState.Humanoid
local root = savedState.Root
if not character or not character.Parent
or not humanoid or not humanoid.Parent
or not root or not root.Parent then
restoreCharacterState(savedState, true)
return false
end
local targetFolder = selectedTarget and selectedTarget.Folder or "\077\111\098\115"
local targetName = selectedTarget and selectedTarget.Name or (model and model.Name or "")
local usableModel = model and isCachedModelUsable(model, "\077\111\098\115") and model or nil
local initialTargetCFrame = usableModel and getMobFollowCFrame(usableModel) or nil
local initialHoldCFrame = initialTargetCFrame
and initialTargetCFrame * savedState.RootToPivot
or character:GetPivot()
mountState = {
Model = usableModel,
Folder = targetFolder,
Name = targetName,
AutoFarm = autoFarmMode == true,
Character = character,
Root = root,
Humanoid = humanoid,
RootToPivot = savedState.RootToPivot,
SavedState = savedState,
HoldCFrame = initialHoldCFrame,
NextSearchAt = 0,
WasSearching = false,
}
if selectedTarget and selectedTarget.Folder == targetFolder
and selectedTarget.Name == targetName then
selectedTarget.CurrentModel = usableModel
end
mountConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
local state = mountState
if not state or destroyed then
return
end
if not state.Character.Parent or not state.Root.Parent or not state.Humanoid.Parent then
stopMount()
return
end
if not state.Model or not isCachedModelUsable(state.Model, "\077\111\098\115") then
if state.Model then
state.Model = nil
end
if state.AutoFarm and autoFarmEnabled then
if os.clock() >= state.NextSearchAt then
state.NextSearchAt = os.clock() + SETTINGS.MobSearchInterval
local replacement = findAutoFarmTarget(SETTINGS.AutoFarmRadius)
if replacement then
state.Model = replacement
state.Name = replacement.Name
farmCurrentTarget = replacement
state.WasSearching = false
feedbackLabel.Text = "\065\085\084\079\070\065\082\077\058\032\070\079\076\076\079\087\073\078\071\032" .. string.upper(replacement.Name)
updateSelectedStatus()
end
end
else
local target = selectedTarget
if target and target.Folder == state.Folder and target.Name == state.Name then
if target.CurrentModel and not isCachedModelUsable(target.CurrentModel, "\077\111\098\115") then
target.CurrentModel = nil
end
if os.clock() >= state.NextSearchAt then
state.NextSearchAt = os.clock() + SETTINGS.MobSearchInterval
local replacement = resolveSelectedModel(target)
if replacement and isCachedModelUsable(replacement, "\077\111\098\115") then
state.Model = replacement
state.WasSearching = false
feedbackLabel.Text = "\070\079\085\078\068\032\078\069\088\084\032" .. string.upper(state.Name) .. "\032\045\032\070\079\076\076\079\087\073\078\071"
updateSelectedStatus()
end
end
end
end
end
if state.Model and isCachedModelUsable(state.Model, "\077\111\098\115") then
local targetCFrame = getMobFollowCFrame(state.Model)
if targetCFrame then
state.HoldCFrame = targetCFrame * state.RootToPivot
state.Character:PivotTo(state.HoldCFrame)
zeroCharacterVelocity(state.Character)
if state.WasSearching then
state.WasSearching = false
feedbackLabel.Text = state.AutoFarm
and ("\065\085\084\079\070\065\082\077\058\032\070\079\076\076\079\087\073\078\071\032" .. string.upper(state.Name))
or ("\070\079\076\076\079\087\073\078\071\032\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068\032" .. string.upper(state.Name))
end
return
end
end
if not state.WasSearching then
state.WasSearching = true
feedbackLabel.Text = state.AutoFarm
and "\065\085\084\079\070\065\082\077\058\032\083\069\065\082\067\072\073\078\071\032\070\079\082\032\078\069\065\082\066\089\032\077\079\066\083\032\045\032\083\084\065\089\073\078\071\032\080\082\079\078\069"
or ("\083\069\065\082\067\072\073\078\071\032\070\079\082\032" .. string.upper(state.Name) .. "\032\045\032\083\084\065\089\073\078\071\032\080\082\079\078\069")
updateSelectedStatus()
end
if state.HoldCFrame then
state.Character:PivotTo(state.HoldCFrame)
zeroCharacterVelocity(state.Character)
end
end)
return true
end
local function playCharacterTween(
character,
rootDestination,
rootToPivot,
duration,
style,
targetModel,
targetCategory
)
if SETTINGS.TravelMode == "\073\078\083\084\065\078\084" then
if destroyed or not gui.Parent or not character.Parent then
error("\077\079\086\069\077\069\078\084\032\073\078\084\069\082\082\085\080\084\069\068")
end
if targetModel and not isCachedModelUsable(targetModel, targetCategory or "\077\111\098\115") then
error("\084\065\082\071\069\084\032\082\069\077\079\086\069\068\032\066\069\070\079\082\069\032\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084")
end
character:PivotTo(rootDestination * rootToPivot)
zeroCharacterVelocity(character)
return
end
local driver = Instance.new("\067\070\114\097\109\101\086\097\108\117\101")
driver.Value = character:GetPivot()
activeDriver = driver
local pivotConnection = driver:GetPropertyChangedSignal("\086\097\108\117\101"):Connect(function()
if character.Parent then
character:PivotTo(driver.Value)
zeroCharacterVelocity(character)
end
end)
local tween = TweenService:Create(
driver,
TweenInfo.new(
duration,
style or Enum.EasingStyle.Linear,
Enum.EasingDirection.InOut
),
{
Value = rootDestination * rootToPivot
}
)
activeTween = tween
local completed = false
local playbackState = nil
local completionConnection = tween.Completed:Connect(function(state)
completed = true
playbackState = state
end)
tween:Play()
while not completed do
task.wait(0.05)
if targetModel and not isCachedModelUsable(targetModel, targetCategory or "\077\111\098\115") then
tween:Cancel()
break
end
if destroyed or not gui.Parent or not character.Parent then
tween:Cancel()
break
end
end
completionConnection:Disconnect()
pivotConnection:Disconnect()
if activeTween == tween then
activeTween = nil
end
if activeDriver == driver then
activeDriver = nil
end
driver:Destroy()
if playbackState ~= Enum.PlaybackState.Completed then
error("\077\079\086\069\077\069\078\084\032\073\078\084\069\082\082\085\080\084\069\068\032\079\082\032\084\065\082\071\069\084\032\082\069\077\079\086\069\068")
end
end
local function chaseMobUnder(character, rootToPivot, model, startRootCFrame)
if SETTINGS.TravelMode == "\073\078\083\084\065\078\084" then
if not isCachedModelUsable(model, "\077\111\098\115") then
error("\077\079\066\032\068\073\069\068\032\066\069\070\079\082\069\032\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084")
end
local targetRoot = getMobUnderCFrame(model)
if not targetRoot then
error("\067\065\078\078\079\084\032\070\073\078\068\032\077\079\066\032\080\079\083\073\084\073\079\078")
end
character:PivotTo(targetRoot * rootToPivot)
zeroCharacterVelocity(character)
return
end
local rootCFrame = startRootCFrame
local startedAt = os.clock()
while true do
local dt = RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
if destroyed or not gui.Parent or not character.Parent then
error("\077\079\086\069\077\069\078\084\032\073\078\084\069\082\082\085\080\084\069\068\032\079\082\032\084\065\082\071\069\084\032\082\069\077\079\086\069\068")
end
if not isCachedModelUsable(model, "\077\111\098\115") then
error("\077\079\066\032\068\073\069\068\032\068\085\082\073\078\071\032\084\082\065\086\069\076")
end
local targetRoot = getMobUnderCFrame(model)
if not targetRoot then
error("\067\065\078\078\079\084\032\070\073\078\068\032\077\079\066\032\080\079\083\073\084\073\079\078")
end
local offset = targetRoot.Position - rootCFrame.Position
local distance = offset.Magnitude
local step = SETTINGS.TeleportSpeed * dt
if distance <= step + 0.05 then
character:PivotTo(targetRoot * rootToPivot)
zeroCharacterVelocity(character)
return
end
if os.clock() - startedAt > SETTINGS.MaxDuration then
error("\067\079\085\076\068\032\078\079\084\032\067\065\084\067\072\032\077\079\066\032\045\032\084\082\089\032\073\078\083\084\065\078\084\032\077\079\068\069")
end
local newPosition = rootCFrame.Position + offset.Unit * step
local newRotation = rootCFrame.Rotation:Lerp(
targetRoot.Rotation,
math.min(1, dt * 8)
)
rootCFrame = CFrame.new(newPosition) * newRotation
character:PivotTo(rootCFrame * rootToPivot)
zeroCharacterVelocity(character)
end
end
updateSelectedStatus = function()
if autoFarmEnabled then
if farmCurrentTarget and isCachedModelUsable(farmCurrentTarget, "\077\111\098\115") then
modelStatus.Text = "\065\085\084\079\070\065\082\077\073\078\071\058\032" .. string.upper(farmCurrentTarget.Name)
else
modelStatus.Text = "\065\085\084\079\070\065\082\077\058\032\083\069\065\082\067\072\073\078\071\032\078\069\065\082\066\089\032\077\079\066\083"
end
teleportButton.Active = not teleporting
teleportButton.Text = teleporting and "\077\079\086\073\078\071\046\046\046" or "\065\085\084\079\070\065\082\077\058\032\079\078"
return
end
if not selectedTarget then
selectedLabel.Text = "\078\079\078\069"
modelStatus.Text = "\083\084\065\084\085\083\058\032\078\079\084\032\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068"
teleportButton.Active = teleportModeEnabled
teleportButton.Text = teleportModeEnabled and "\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\058\032\079\078" or "\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\032\084\079\032\077\079\068\069\076"
return
end
local target = selectedTarget
local model = resolveSelectedModel(target)
local count = countAvailable(target.Folder, target.Name)
selectedLabel.Text = "\091" .. target.Folder .. "\093\032"
.. target.Name
.. "\032\124\032"
.. count
.. "\032\065\086\065\073\076\065\066\076\069"
if not model then
if target.Folder == "\077\111\098\115" then
modelStatus.Text = "\078\079\032\076\073\086\073\078\071\032\077\079\066\032\070\079\085\078\068"
elseif target.Folder == "\078\080\067\115" then
modelStatus.Text = "\067\085\082\082\069\078\084\032\078\080\067\032\085\078\065\086\065\073\076\065\066\076\069"
else
modelStatus.Text = "\078\079\032\076\073\086\073\078\071\032\084\065\082\071\069\084\032\070\079\085\078\068"
end
local canSearchSelectedMob = target.Folder == "\077\111\098\115"
and mobTeleportEnabled
and backFollowEnabled
teleportButton.Active = teleportModeEnabled or canSearchSelectedMob
if teleportModeEnabled then
teleportButton.Text = "\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\058\032\079\078"
elseif canSearchSelectedMob then
teleportButton.Text = "\083\069\065\082\067\072\032\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068\032\077\079\066"
else
teleportButton.Text = "\078\079\032\065\086\065\073\076\065\066\076\069\032\084\065\082\071\069\084"
end
return
end
if teleporting then
modelStatus.Text = "\084\065\082\071\069\084\058\032" .. model.Name .. "\032\047\032\077\079\086\073\078\071"
teleportButton.Active = false
teleportButton.Text = "\077\079\086\073\078\071\046\046\046"
return
end
if teleportModeEnabled then
if mountState and mountState.Model == model then
modelStatus.Text = "\070\079\076\076\079\087\073\078\071\032\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068\032\077\079\066\032\047\032\067\076\073\067\075\032\084\079\032\083\084\079\080"
else
modelStatus.Text = "\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\032\077\079\068\069\032\065\067\084\073\086\069\032\047\032\067\076\073\067\075\032\084\079\032\083\084\079\080"
end
teleportButton.Active = true
teleportButton.Text = "\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\058\032\079\078"
return
end
if mountState and mountState.Model == model then
modelStatus.Text = farmPositionMode == "\085\080\080\069\082"
and "\076\089\073\078\071\032\070\065\067\069\045\068\079\087\078\032\065\066\079\086\069\032\084\072\069\032\077\079\066"
or "\076\089\073\078\071\032\070\065\067\069\045\085\080\032\085\078\068\069\082\032\084\072\069\032\077\079\066"
teleportButton.Active = false
teleportButton.Text = "\077\079\085\078\084\069\068"
return
end
if target.Folder == "\077\111\098\115" and not mobTeleportEnabled then
modelStatus.Text = "\065\086\065\073\076\065\066\076\069\032\047\032\077\079\066\032\084\080\032\079\070\070"
teleportButton.Active = false
teleportButton.Text = "\077\079\066\032\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\032\079\070\070"
return
end
if target.Folder == "\077\111\098\115" then
modelStatus.Text = "\065\085\084\079\032\078\069\088\084\032\083\065\077\069\045\078\065\077\069\032\077\079\066\032\069\078\065\066\076\069\068"
elseif target.Folder == "\078\080\067\115" then
modelStatus.Text = "\078\080\067\032\084\065\082\071\069\084\032\065\086\065\073\076\065\066\076\069"
else
modelStatus.Text = "\084\065\082\071\069\084\032\065\086\065\073\076\065\066\076\069\032\047\032\065\085\084\079\032\078\069\088\084\032\083\065\077\069\045\078\065\077\069"
end
teleportButton.Active = true
teleportButton.Text = "\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\032\084\079\032\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068\032\077\079\068\069\076"
end
local function clearDropdown()
table.clear(dropdownRows)
for _, child in ipairs(dropdownList:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("\071\117\105\079\098\106\101\099\116") then
child:Destroy()
end
end
end
local function updateDropdownCounts()
for _, entry in ipairs(dropdownRows) do
local group = entry.Group
local count = countAvailable(group.Folder, group.Name)
if entry.Button.Parent then
entry.Button.Text = "\091" .. group.Folder .. "\093\032"
.. group.Name
.. "\032\124\032"
.. count
.. "\032\065\086\065\073\076\065\066\076\069"
end
end
end
local function createDropdownRow(group, index)
local count = countAvailable(group.Folder, group.Name)
local option = create("\084\101\120\116\066\117\116\116\111\110", {
Name = "\077\111\100\101\108\071\114\111\117\112",
Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 29),
BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.Element,
BorderSizePixel = 0,
Text = "\091" .. group.Folder .. "\093\032" .. group.Name .. "\032\124\032" .. count .. "\032\065\086\065\073\076\065\066\076\069",
TextColor3 = COLORS.White,
TextSize = 9,
Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
LayoutOrder = index,
ZIndex = 21,
}, dropdownList)
addCorner(option, 7)
addStroke(option, 0.55, 1)
create("\085\073\080\097\100\100\105\110\103", {
PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8),
PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8),
}, option)
table.insert(dropdownRows, {
Button = option,
Group = group,
})
option.Activated:Connect(function()
if teleporting then
return
end
local sameGroup = selectedTarget
and selectedTarget.Folder == group.Folder
and selectedTarget.Name == group.Name
if not sameGroup and not autoFarmEnabled then
mobSearchToken = mobSearchToken + 1
teleportModeEnabled = false
if mountState then
stopMount()
end
end
selectedTarget = {
Folder = group.Folder,
Name = group.Name,
CurrentModel = findNearestLiveModel(group.Folder, group.Name),
}
dropdownButton.Text = "\091" .. group.Folder .. "\093\032" .. group.Name .. "\032\063"
dropdownList.Visible = false
feedbackLabel.Text = "\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068\032\071\082\079\085\080\058\032" .. group.Name
updateSelectedStatus()
if updateAutoFarmMethodUI then
updateAutoFarmMethodUI()
end
if autoFarmEnabled and SETTINGS.AutoFarmMethod == "\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068\095\078\065\077\069" then
farmCurrentTarget = nil
if mountState and mountState.AutoFarm then
mountState.Model = nil
mountState.NextSearchAt = 0
end
end
end)
end
local function searchModels()
clearDropdown()
local tokens = {}
for token in string.gmatch(string.lower(searchBox.Text), "\037\083\043") do
table.insert(tokens, token)
end
local matches = {}
for _, group in ipairs(modelCache.Groups) do
local matched = true
for _, token in ipairs(tokens) do
if not string.find(group.Lower, token, 1, true)
and not string.find(group.FolderLower, token, 1, true) then
matched = false
break
end
end
if matched then
table.insert(matches, group)
end
end
local shown = math.min(#matches, SETTINGS.MaxDropdownRows)
if shown < #matches then
countLabel.Text = "\076\073\086\069\032\068\069\084\069\067\084\073\079\078\032\079\078\032\124\032" .. #matches .. "\032\071\082\079\085\080\083\032\047\032\083\072\079\087\073\078\071\032" .. shown
else
countLabel.Text = "\076\073\086\069\032\068\069\084\069\067\084\073\079\078\032\079\078\032\124\032\070\079\085\078\068\032" .. #matches .. "\032\071\082\079\085\080\083"
end
if #matches == 0 then
create("\084\101\120\116\076\097\098\101\108", {
Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 29),
BackgroundTransparency = 1,
Text = "\078\079\032\077\079\068\069\076\083\032\070\079\085\078\068",
TextColor3 = COLORS.White,
TextSize = 9,
Font = Enum.Font.Gotham,
ZIndex = 21,
}, dropdownList)
else
for index = 1, shown do
createDropdownRow(matches[index], index)
end
end
updateSelectedStatus()
end
local modelRootWatchers = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "\107"})
local modelRootRefreshQueued = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "\107"})
local function refreshAfterWorkspaceChange()
if destroyed or not gui.Parent then
return
end
updateWorkspaceStatus()
updateSelectedStatus()
if modelCache.Signature ~= lastSignature then
lastSignature = modelCache.Signature
searchModels()
else
updateDropdownCounts()
end
end
local function queueModelRootRefresh(root)
if not root or modelRootRefreshQueued[root] or destroyed then
return
end
modelRootRefreshQueued[root] = true
task.delay(0.18, function()
if destroyed or not gui.Parent then
modelRootRefreshQueued[root] = nil
return
end
modelRootRefreshQueued[root] = nil
if root.Parent == workspace then
rootEntries[root] = nil
end
refreshModelCache(false)
refreshAfterWorkspaceChange()
end)
end
local function watchModelRoot(root)
if not root or modelRootWatchers[root] then
return
end
modelRootWatchers[root] = true
connectGlobal(root.DescendantAdded, function(instance)
if instance:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") or instance:IsA("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100") then
queueModelRootRefresh(root)
end
end)
connectGlobal(root.DescendantRemoving, function(instance)
if instance:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") or instance:IsA("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100") then
queueModelRootRefresh(root)
end
end)
end
for _, info in ipairs(getScanRoots()) do
watchModelRoot(info.Root)
end
local MODEL_ROOT_NAMES = {
Mobs = true,
Enemies = true,
NPCs = true,
NPC = true,
NonPlayerCharacters = true,
}
connectGlobal(workspace.ChildAdded, function(child)
if not MODEL_ROOT_NAMES[child.Name] then
return
end
if child.Name == "\078\080\067\115" or child.Name == "\078\080\067" or child.Name == "\078\111\110\080\108\097\121\101\114\067\104\097\114\097\099\116\101\114\115" then
npcFolderCache = child
end
watchModelRoot(child)
task.defer(function()
refreshModelCache(true)
refreshAfterWorkspaceChange()
end)
end)
connectGlobal(workspace.ChildRemoved, function(child)
if not MODEL_ROOT_NAMES[child.Name] then
return
end
rootEntries[child] = nil
if npcFolderCache == child then
npcFolderCache = nil
npcFolderCheckedAt = -math.huge
end
refreshModelCache(false)
refreshAfterWorkspaceChange()
end)
local function smoothTeleport(target, autoFarmMode)
if teleporting or not target then
return
end
if mountState then
stopMount()
end
local isNPC = target.Folder == "\078\080\067\115"
local isMob = target.Folder == "\077\111\098\115"
if isMob and not mobTeleportEnabled then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\077\079\066\032\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\032\073\083\032\079\070\070"
return
end
local model = resolveSelectedModel(target)
if not model then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\078\079\032\076\073\086\073\078\071\032\084\065\082\071\069\084\032\070\079\085\078\068"
updateSelectedStatus()
return
end
local character = player.Character
if not character then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\067\072\065\082\065\067\084\069\082\032\078\079\084\032\076\079\065\068\069\068"
return
end
local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100")
local root = character:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
if not humanoid or not root or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\067\072\065\082\065\067\084\069\082\032\078\079\084\032\082\069\065\068\089"
return
end
local destination
if isNPC then
destination = SETTINGS.NPCWorldPivot
elseif isMob then
destination = getMobUnderCFrame(model)
if not destination then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\067\065\078\078\079\084\032\070\073\078\068\032\077\079\066\032\080\079\083\073\084\073\079\078"
return
end
else
destination = getObjectTopCFrame(model, SETTINGS.OtherClearance)
if not destination then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\067\065\078\078\079\084\032\070\073\078\068\032\084\065\082\071\069\084\032\080\079\083\073\084\073\079\078"
return
end
end
local startingPosition
if isNPC then
startingPosition = destination
* CFrame.new(0, -SETTINGS.NPCBelowDistance, 0)
else
startingPosition = destination
end
if not lastReturnPivot then
lastReturnPivot = character:GetPivot()
end
returnButton.Active = true
teleportModeEnabled = true
local distance = (startingPosition.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
local travelDuration = math.clamp(
distance / SETTINGS.TeleportSpeed,
SETTINGS.MinDuration,
SETTINGS.MaxDuration
)
teleporting = true
updateSelectedStatus()
local savedState = captureCharacterState(character, humanoid, root)
activeSavedState = savedState
local rootToPivot = savedState.RootToPivot
local success, err = pcall(function()
humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
if isMob then
local mobForward = getMobForward(model)
local proneStart = CFrame.lookAt(
root.Position,
root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
mobForward
)
feedbackLabel.Text = "\077\079\086\073\078\071\032\070\065\067\069\045\085\080\032\085\078\068\069\082\032\084\072\069\032\077\079\066\046\046\046"
playCharacterTween(
character,
proneStart,
rootToPivot,
SETTINGS.LayDuration,
Enum.EasingStyle.Sine
)
feedbackLabel.Text = "\077\079\086\073\078\071\032\085\078\068\069\082\032\084\072\069\032\077\079\066\046\046\046"
chaseMobUnder(character, rootToPivot, model, proneStart)
elseif isNPC then
local npcStart = destination
* CFrame.new(0, -SETTINGS.NPCBelowDistance, 0)
* CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0)
feedbackLabel.Text = "\065\080\080\082\079\065\067\072\073\078\071\032\078\080\067\046\046\046"
playCharacterTween(
character,
npcStart,
rootToPivot,
travelDuration,
Enum.EasingStyle.Linear
)
feedbackLabel.Text = "\083\076\079\087\076\089\032\065\083\067\069\078\068\073\078\071\046\046\046"
playCharacterTween(
character,
destination * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0),
rootToPivot,
SETTINGS.NPCRiseDuration,
Enum.EasingStyle.Sine
)
feedbackLabel.Text = "\083\084\065\078\068\073\078\071\032\085\080\046\046\046"
playCharacterTween(
character,
destination,
rootToPivot,
SETTINGS.StandDuration,
Enum.EasingStyle.Sine
)
else
feedbackLabel.Text = "\077\079\086\073\078\071\032\084\079\032" .. string.upper(model.Name) .. "\046\046\046"
playCharacterTween(
character,
destination,
rootToPivot,
travelDuration,
Enum.EasingStyle.Sine,
model,
target.Folder
)
if not isCachedModelUsable(model, target.Folder) then
error("\084\065\082\071\069\084\032\082\069\077\079\086\069\068\032\068\085\082\073\078\071\032\084\082\065\086\069\076")
end
end
end)
if activeTween then
activeTween:Cancel()
activeTween = nil
end
if activeDriver then
activeDriver:Destroy()
activeDriver = nil
end
teleporting = false
if not success and not (isMob and backFollowEnabled and teleportModeEnabled) then
teleportModeEnabled = false
end
local shouldMount = isMob
and backFollowEnabled
and teleportModeEnabled
if shouldMount then
startMobMount(model, savedState, autoFarmMode)
if autoFarmMode then
farmCurrentTarget = model
end
else
restoreCharacterState(savedState, true)
end
updateSelectedStatus()
if success then
if isNPC then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\065\082\082\073\086\069\068\032\065\084\032\078\080\067\032\087\079\082\076\068\032\080\073\086\079\084"
elseif shouldMount then
feedbackLabel.Text = autoFarmMode
and ("\065\085\084\079\070\065\082\077\058\032\070\079\076\076\079\087\073\078\071\032" .. string.upper(model.Name))
or (farmPositionMode == "\085\080\080\069\082" and "\076\089\073\078\071\032\070\065\067\069\045\068\079\087\078\032\065\066\079\086\069\032\084\072\069\032\077\079\066" or "\076\089\073\078\071\032\070\065\067\069\045\085\080\032\085\078\068\069\082\032\084\072\069\032\077\079\066")
else
feedbackLabel.Text = "\065\082\082\073\086\069\068\032\065\066\079\086\069\032" .. target.Name
end
elseif isMob and not isCachedModelUsable(model, "\077\111\098\115") then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\077\079\066\032\068\073\069\068\032\045\032\078\069\088\084\032\083\065\077\069\045\078\065\077\069\032\084\065\082\071\069\084\032\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068"
elseif not isNPC and not isMob and not isCachedModelUsable(model, target.Folder) then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\084\065\082\071\069\084\032\082\069\077\079\086\069\068\032\045\032\078\069\088\084\032\083\065\077\069\045\078\065\077\069\032\084\065\082\071\069\084\032\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068"
else
feedbackLabel.Text = "\070\065\073\076\069\068\058\032" .. tostring(err)
end
end
local function getAutoFarmMethodLabel()
for _, method in ipairs(AUTO_FARM_METHODS) do
if method.Id == SETTINGS.AutoFarmMethod then
return method.Label
end
end
return "\078\069\065\082\069\083\084"
end
updateAutoFarmMethodUI = function()
autoFarmMethodButton.Text = getAutoFarmMethodLabel()
if SETTINGS.AutoFarmMethod == "\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068\095\078\065\077\069" then
autoFarmMethodDescription.Text = selectedTarget and selectedTarget.Folder == "\077\111\098\115"
and ("\079\078\076\089\058\032" .. string.upper(selectedTarget.Name))
or "\083\069\076\069\067\084\032\065\032\077\079\066\032\077\079\068\069\076\032\070\073\082\083\084"
else
autoFarmMethodDescription.Text = "\067\076\073\067\075\032\084\079\032\067\072\065\078\071\069\032\084\065\082\071\069\084\032\080\082\073\079\082\073\084\089"
end
end
autoFarmMethodButton.Activated:Connect(function()
local currentIndex = 1
for index, method in ipairs(AUTO_FARM_METHODS) do
if method.Id == SETTINGS.AutoFarmMethod then
currentIndex = index
break
end
end
local nextIndex = (currentIndex % #AUTO_FARM_METHODS) + 1
SETTINGS.AutoFarmMethod = AUTO_FARM_METHODS[nextIndex].Id
farmCycleLastModel = nil
farmCycleIndex = 0
updateAutoFarmMethodUI()
updateAutoFarmToggle()
if autoFarmEnabled then
farmCurrentTarget = nil
if mountState and mountState.AutoFarm then
mountState.Model = nil
mountState.NextSearchAt = 0
end
feedbackLabel.Text = "\065\085\084\079\070\065\082\077\032\077\069\084\072\079\068\058\032" .. getAutoFarmMethodLabel()
end
end)
local function startAutoFarmSearch()
autoFarmToken = autoFarmToken + 1
local myToken = autoFarmToken
task.spawn(function()
while not destroyed
and gui.Parent
and myToken == autoFarmToken
and autoFarmEnabled do
if not teleporting and not mountState then
local model = findAutoFarmTarget(SETTINGS.AutoFarmRadius)
if model then
farmCurrentTarget = model
local target = {
Folder = "\077\111\098\115",
Name = model.Name,
CurrentModel = model,
}
feedbackLabel.Text = "\065\085\084\079\070\065\082\077\058\032\065\080\080\082\079\065\067\072\073\078\071\032" .. string.upper(model.Name)
smoothTeleport(target, true)
else
farmCurrentTarget = nil
feedbackLabel.Text = "\065\085\084\079\070\065\082\077\058\032\083\069\065\082\067\072\073\078\071\032\087\073\084\072\073\078\032" .. tostring(SETTINGS.AutoFarmRadius) .. "\032\083\084\085\068\083"
updateSelectedStatus()
end
end
task.wait(SETTINGS.AutoFarmSearchInterval)
end
end)
end
updateAutoFarmToggle = function()
autoFarmButton.Text = autoFarmEnabled and "\079\078" or "\079\070\070"
setToggleVisual(autoFarmButton, autoFarmEnabled)
autoFarmDescription.Text = autoFarmEnabled
and (getAutoFarmMethodLabel() .. "\032\047\032" .. tostring(SETTINGS.AutoFarmRadius) .. "\032\083\084\085\068\032\082\065\068\073\085\083")
or (getAutoFarmMethodLabel() .. "\032\047\032\065\076\076\032\077\079\066\032\078\065\077\069\083")
end
local function setAutoFarmEnabled(enabled)
if teleporting then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\087\065\073\084\032\085\078\084\073\076\032\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\032\070\073\078\073\083\072\069\083"
return
end
enabled = enabled == true
if autoFarmEnabled == enabled then
return
end
if enabled then
local character = player.Character
if not character or not character.Parent then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\067\072\065\082\065\067\084\069\082\032\078\079\084\032\076\079\065\068\069\068"
return
end
local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100")
if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\067\072\065\082\065\067\084\069\082\032\078\079\084\032\082\069\065\068\089"
return
end
if not lastReturnPivot then
lastReturnPivot = character:GetPivot()
end
returnButton.Active = true
mobSearchToken = mobSearchToken + 1
if mountState then
stopMount()
end
mobTeleportEnabled = true
SETTINGS.MobTeleportEnabled = true
backFollowEnabled = true
SETTINGS.MobBackFollowEnabled = true
updateMobToggle()
updateBackFollowToggle()
if not autoClickEnabled then
setAutoClickEnabled(true)
end
autoFarmEnabled = true
teleportModeEnabled = true
feedbackLabel.Text = "\065\085\084\079\070\065\082\077\032\079\078\032\045\032\084\065\082\071\069\084\073\078\071\032\065\076\076\032\078\069\065\082\066\089\032\077\079\066\032\078\065\077\069\083"
updateAutoFarmToggle()
updateSelectedStatus()
startAutoFarmSearch()
else
autoFarmEnabled = false
autoFarmToken = autoFarmToken + 1
farmCurrentTarget = nil
teleportModeEnabled = false
if mountState then
stopMount()
end
feedbackLabel.Text = "\065\085\084\079\070\065\082\077\032\079\070\070\032\045\032\083\084\079\080\080\069\068\032\070\079\076\076\079\087\073\078\071"
updateAutoFarmToggle()
updateSelectedStatus()
end
end

end)

or "\070\073\078\068\032\043\032\065\080\080\082\079\065\067\072\032\071\045\075\069\089\032\067\072\069\083\084\032\080\082\079\077\080\084\083"

or "\065\078\089\032\069\078\065\066\076\069\068\032\071\045\075\069\089\032\080\082\079\088\073\077\073\084\089\032\080\082\079\077\080\084"
end
local function startAutoChestWorker()
autoChestToken = autoChestToken + 1
local myToken = autoChestToken
task.spawn(function()
while not destroyed
and gui.Parent
and myToken == autoChestToken
and (autoChestEnabled or autoGPromptEnabled) do
if not teleporting then
local prompt = nil
local isChestPrompt = false
if autoChestEnabled then
prompt = findChestGPrompt(SETTINGS.AutoChestRadius, false)
isChestPrompt = prompt ~= nil
end
if not prompt and autoGPromptEnabled then
prompt = findAnyGKeyPrompt()
end
if prompt then
local character = player.Character
local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116")
local promptPosition = getPromptWorldPosition(prompt)
local distance = root and promptPosition
and (root.Position - promptPosition).Magnitude
or math.huge
if isChestPrompt and autoChestEnabled
and distance > math.max(1, prompt.MaxActivationDistance - 0.5) then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\065\085\084\079\032\067\072\069\083\084\058\032\065\080\080\082\079\065\067\072\073\078\071\032"
.. string.upper(prompt.ObjectText ~= "" and prompt.ObjectText or "\067\072\069\083\084")
moveCharacterNearChestPrompt(prompt)
task.wait(0.2)
end
if prompt.Parent then
local activated
if isChestPrompt then
activated = activateChestGPrompt(prompt)
else
activated = activateAnyGKeyPrompt(prompt)
end
if activated then
feedbackLabel.Text = isChestPrompt
and "\065\085\084\079\032\067\072\069\083\084\058\032\071\032\080\082\079\077\080\084\032\065\067\084\073\086\065\084\069\068"
or "\065\085\084\079\032\071\032\080\082\079\077\080\084\058\032\065\067\084\073\086\065\084\069\068\032" .. tostring(prompt.ObjectText)
end
end
elseif autoChestEnabled then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\065\085\084\079\032\067\072\069\083\084\058\032\083\069\065\082\067\072\073\078\071\032\070\079\082\032\071\045\075\069\089\032\067\072\069\083\084\083"
elseif autoGPromptEnabled then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\065\085\084\079\032\071\032\080\082\079\077\080\084\058\032\087\065\073\084\073\078\071\032\070\079\082\032\065\032\078\069\065\082\066\089\032\071\032\080\082\079\077\080\084"
end
end
task.wait(SETTINGS.AutoChestSearchInterval)
end
end)
end
local function setAutoChestEnabled(enabled)
enabled = enabled == true
if autoChestEnabled == enabled then
return
end
if enabled then
if teleporting then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\087\065\073\084\032\085\078\084\073\076\032\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\032\070\073\078\073\083\072\069\083"
return
end
if autoFarmEnabled then
setAutoFarmEnabled(false)
end
teleportModeEnabled = false
mobSearchToken = mobSearchToken + 1
if mountState then
stopMount()
end
local character = player.Character
if not character or not character.Parent then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\067\072\065\082\065\067\084\069\082\032\078\079\084\032\076\079\065\068\069\068"
return
end
if not lastReturnPivot then
lastReturnPivot = character:GetPivot()
returnButton.Active = true
end
end
autoChestEnabled = enabled
SETTINGS.AutoChestEnabled = autoChestEnabled
updateAutoChestToggle()
autoChestToken = autoChestToken + 1
if autoChestEnabled or autoGPromptEnabled then
startAutoChestWorker()
else
feedbackLabel.Text = "\065\085\084\079\032\067\072\069\083\084\032\068\073\083\065\066\076\069\068"
end
end
local function setAutoGPromptEnabled(enabled)
autoGPromptEnabled = enabled == true
SETTINGS.AutoGPromptEnabled = autoGPromptEnabled
updateAutoGPromptToggle()
autoChestToken = autoChestToken + 1
if autoChestEnabled or autoGPromptEnabled then
startAutoChestWorker()
else
feedbackLabel.Text = "\065\085\084\079\032\071\032\080\082\079\077\080\084\032\068\073\083\065\066\076\069\068"
end
end
autoChestButton.Activated:Connect(function()
setAutoChestEnabled(not autoChestEnabled)
end)
autoGPromptButton.Activated:Connect(function()
setAutoGPromptEnabled(not autoGPromptEnabled)
end)
local function startSelectedMobSearch()
mobSearchToken = mobSearchToken + 1
local myToken = mobSearchToken
task.spawn(function()
while not destroyed
and gui.Parent
and myToken == mobSearchToken
and teleportModeEnabled do
local target = selectedTarget
if not target or target.Folder ~= "\077\111\098\115" then
break
end
if mobTeleportEnabled and backFollowEnabled and not teleporting and not mountState then
local model = resolveSelectedModel(target)
if model then
smoothTeleport(target)
else
feedbackLabel.Text = "\083\069\065\082\067\072\073\078\071\032\070\079\082\032\083\069\076\069\067\084\069\068\032\077\079\068\069\076\058\032" .. string.upper(target.Name)
updateSelectedStatus()
end
elseif not mobTeleportEnabled then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\077\079\066\032\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\032\068\073\083\065\066\076\069\068\032\045\032\083\069\065\082\067\072\032\080\065\085\083\069\068"
elseif not backFollowEnabled then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\069\078\065\066\076\069\032\077\079\066\032\080\079\083\073\084\073\079\078\032\070\079\076\076\079\087\032\084\079\032\083\084\065\089\032\080\082\079\078\069"
end
task.wait(SETTINGS.MobSearchInterval)
end
end)
end

if teleportModeEnabled then
if autoFarmEnabled then

feedbackLabel.Text = "\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\032\077\079\068\069\032\068\073\083\065\066\076\069\068\032\045\032\083\084\079\079\068\032\085\080"

if not backFollowEnabled then

updateSelectedStatus()
return
end
if not lastReturnPivot then
local character = player.Character
if character and character.Parent then
lastReturnPivot = character:GetPivot()
returnButton.Active = true
end
end
teleportModeEnabled = true
feedbackLabel.Text = "\077\079\066\032\077\079\068\069\032\079\078\032\045\032\083\069\065\082\067\072\073\078\071\032\070\079\082\032" .. string.upper(selectedTarget.Name)
updateSelectedStatus()
startSelectedMobSearch()
return
end
smoothTeleport(selectedTarget)
end)

if teleporting then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\087\065\073\084\032\085\078\084\073\076\032\084\069\076\069\080\079\082\084\032\070\073\078\073\083\072\069\083"
return
end
if not lastReturnPivot then

local character = player.Character

end
teleportModeEnabled = false
mobSearchToken = mobSearchToken + 1

local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100")

humanoid.PlatformStand = false

humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)

end)

end)
local function cleanup()
if destroyed then
return
end
destroyed = true
dragging = false
dragInput = nil
autoClickEnabled = false
SETTINGS.AutoClickEnabled = false
teleportModeEnabled = false
mobSearchToken = mobSearchToken + 1
autoFarmEnabled = false
autoFarmToken = autoFarmToken + 1
autoChestEnabled = false
autoGPromptEnabled = false
SETTINGS.AutoChestEnabled = false
SETTINGS.AutoGPromptEnabled = false
autoChestToken = autoChestToken + 1
autoChestBusy = false
farmCurrentTarget = nil
lastReturnPivot = nil
deactivateEquippedTool()
if activeTween then
activeTween:Cancel()
activeTween = nil
end
if activeDriver then
activeDriver:Destroy()
activeDriver = nil
end
stopMount()
if activeSavedState then
restoreCharacterState(activeSavedState, true)
end
clearESP()
stopNoclip()
pcall(function()
player.DevCameraOcclusionMode = originalCameraOcclusionMode
or Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Zoom
end)
cameraNoclipEnabled = false
SETTINGS.CameraNoclipEnabled = false
for _, connection in ipairs(globalConnections) do
connection:Disconnect()
end
table.clear(globalConnections)
end
destroyButton.Activated:Connect(function()
cleanup()
gui:Destroy()
end)
gui.Destroying:Connect(cleanup)
connectGlobal(player.CharacterAdded, function()
teleportModeEnabled = false
mobSearchToken = mobSearchToken + 1
autoFarmEnabled = false
autoFarmToken = autoFarmToken + 1
autoChestEnabled = false
autoGPromptEnabled = false
SETTINGS.AutoChestEnabled = false
SETTINGS.AutoGPromptEnabled = false
autoChestToken = autoChestToken + 1
farmCurrentTarget = nil
updateAutoFarmToggle()
updateAutoChestToggle()
updateAutoGPromptToggle()
lastReturnPivot = nil
returnButton.Active = false
if mountState then
stopMount()
end
if autoClickEnabled then
feedbackLabel.Text = "\067\072\065\082\065\067\084\069\082\032\082\069\083\080\065\087\078\069\068\032\045\032\065\085\084\079\032\067\076\073\067\075\032\082\069\065\068\089"
end
end)
updateMobToggle()
updateESPToggle()
updateAutoClickToggle()
updateBackFollowToggle()
updateFarmPositionMode()
mobDistanceDescription.Text = "\071\065\080\058\032" .. tostring(SETTINGS.MobUnderDistance) .. "\032\083\084\085\068\083"
updateOrbitToggle()
updateCameraNoclipToggle()
updateAutoFarmMethodUI()
updateAutoFarmToggle()
updateAutoChestToggle()
updateAutoGPromptToggle()
main.Visible = true
gui.Enabled = true
updateSelectedStatus()
task.defer(function()
if destroyed or not gui.Parent then
return
end
local ok, err = pcall(updateWorkspaceStatus)
if not ok then
warn("\091\080\105\108\103\114\097\109\109\101\100\115\099\114\105\112\116\093\032\087\111\114\107\115\112\097\099\101\032\115\116\097\116\117\115\032\114\101\102\114\101\115\104\032\102\097\105\108\101\100\058\032" .. tostring(err))
feedbackLabel.Text = "\071\085\073\032\082\069\065\068\089\032\047\032\087\079\082\075\083\080\065\067\069\032\083\067\065\078\032\087\065\082\078\073\078\071"
end
end)
task.spawn(function()
local lastFullScan = -math.huge
while gui.Parent and not destroyed do
local fullScan = os.clock() - lastFullScan >= math.max(SETTINGS.FullScanRate, 15)
if fullScan then
lastFullScan = os.clock()
end
local scanOk, scanErr = pcall(refreshModelCache, fullScan)
if not scanOk then
warn("\091\080\105\108\103\114\097\109\109\101\100\115\099\114\105\112\116\093\032\077\111\100\101\108\032\115\099\097\110\032\102\097\105\108\101\100\058\032" .. tostring(scanErr))
feedbackLabel.Text = "\077\079\068\069\076\032\083\067\065\078\032\069\082\082\079\082\032\045\032\071\085\073\032\073\083\032\083\084\073\076\076\032\082\085\078\078\073\078\071"
end
if destroyed or not gui.Parent then
break
end
pcall(updateWorkspaceStatus)
pcall(updateSelectedStatus)
if modelCache.Signature ~= lastSignature then
lastSignature = modelCache.Signature
searchModels()
else
updateDropdownCounts()
end
task.wait(SETTINGS.CacheRefreshRate)
end
end)
task.spawn(function()
while gui.Parent and not destroyed do
updateESP()
task.wait(SETTINGS.ESPRefreshRate)
end
end)
local autoClickWorkerRunning = true
task.spawn(function()
while autoClickWorkerRunning and gui.Parent and not destroyed do
if autoClickEnabled and not teleporting then
local character = player.Character
local humanoid = character
and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100")
local tool = getEquippedTool()
if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and tool
and tool.Parent == character and tool.Enabled then
pcall(function()
if autoClickEnabled and tool.Parent == character then
tool:Activate()
end
end)
local pulseStarted = os.clock()
while autoClickEnabled
and os.clock() - pulseStarted < SETTINGS.AutoClickPulseDuration do
task.wait(0.01)
end
pcall(function()
tool:Deactivate()
end)
end
end
task.wait(SETTINGS.AutoClickInterval)
end
end)
end

local function RenderMyScripts()
for _, Child in ipairs(ScriptsItemsHolder:GetChildren()) do
if not Child:IsA("\085\073\076\105\115\116\076\097\121\111\117\116") then
Child:Destroy()
end
end
local Categories = {}
for _, Entry in ipairs(MyScriptEntries) do
Categories[#Categories + 1] = Entry.Category or "\079\084\072\069\082"
end
ScriptsFilter:SetCategories(Categories)
local VisibleCount = 0
for _, Entry in ipairs(MyScriptEntries) do
if ScriptsFilter:Matches(Entry) then
VisibleCount += 1
CreateCatalogCard(
ScriptsItemsHolder,
Entry.Name,
Entry.Description,
Entry.Category,
function()
assert(type(Entry.Run) == "\102\117\110\099\116\105\111\110", "\069\109\098\101\100\100\101\100\032\115\099\114\105\112\116\032\104\097\115\032\110\111\032\082\117\110\032\102\117\110\099\116\105\111\110")
Entry.Run()
end,
VisibleCount
)
end
end
if VisibleCount == 0 then
local Empty = CreateLabel(
ScriptsItemsHolder,
"\078\111\077\121\083\099\114\105\112\116\082\101\115\117\108\116\115",
"\078\111\032\115\099\114\105\112\116\115\032\109\097\116\099\104\032\121\111\117\114\032\115\101\097\114\099\104\032\111\114\032\099\097\116\101\103\111\114\121\046",
UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0),
UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 42),
"\077\117\116\101\100",
Enum.Font.Gotham,
11
)
Empty.LayoutOrder = 1
end
end
ScriptsFilter.OnChanged = RenderMyScripts
RenderMyScripts()

local function RefreshThemeLabels()
ThemeValue.Text = CurrentThemeName
GlowButton.Text = GlowEnabled and "\071\076\079\087\058\032\079\078" or "\071\076\079\087\058\032\079\070\070"
GlassButton.Text = GlassEnabled and "\071\076\065\083\083\058\032\079\078" or "\071\076\065\083\083\058\032\079\070\070"
for ThemeName, Data in pairs(ThemeSwatches) do
local Selected = ThemeName == CurrentThemeName
Data.Button.Text = (Selected and "\063\032\032" or "") .. ThemeName
Data.Stroke.Color = Selected and Theme.Text or THEMES[ThemeName].Accent
Data.Stroke.Thickness = Selected and 2 or 1
Data.Stroke.Transparency = Selected and 0 or 0.38
Data.Button.BackgroundTransparency = Selected and 0 or 0.12
end
end
local function RefreshNavigationTheme()
for Index, Button in ipairs(NavigationButtons) do
local Selected = Navigation[Index] and Navigation[Index].Name == ActivePageName
CreateTween(Button, 0.18, {
ImageColor3 = Selected and Theme.Accent or Theme.Muted,
}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
end
CreateTween(Selection, 0.18, {
BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Hover,
}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
local SelectionStroke = Selection:FindFirstChildOfClass("\085\073\083\116\114\111\107\101")
if SelectionStroke then
CreateTween(SelectionStroke, 0.18, {
Color = Theme.Accent,
}, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
end
end
local function SetTheme(ThemeName)
if not THEMES[ThemeName] then return end
CurrentThemeName = ThemeName
ApplyTheme()
RefreshNavigationTheme()
for Index, Toggle in pairs(ToggleButtons) do
if Toggle and Toggle.Parent then
Toggle.ImageColor3 = Theme.Accent
end
local Status = ToggleStatusLabels[Index]
if Status then
Status.TextColor3 = FeatureStates[Index] and Theme.Accent or Theme.Muted
end
end
for _, Data in pairs(ESPObjects) do
if Data.Highlight then
Data.Highlight.FillColor = Theme.Accent
Data.Highlight.OutlineColor = Theme.Accent
end
if Data.Billboard then
local NameLabel = Data.Billboard:FindFirstChild("\080\108\097\121\101\114\078\097\109\101")
if NameLabel then
NameLabel.TextColor3 = Theme.Accent
end
end
end
Main.BackgroundTransparency = GlassEnabled and 0.12 or 0
Sidebar.BackgroundTransparency = GlassEnabled and 0.08 or 0
RefreshThemeLabels()
if ActivePageName == "\072\117\098" and RenderHub then
RenderHub()
end
Notify("\084\104\101\109\101\032\099\104\097\110\103\101\100", ThemeName .. "\032\116\104\101\109\101\032\097\112\112\108\105\101\100")
end
for ThemeName, Data in pairs(ThemeSwatches) do
Data.Button.Activated:Connect(function()
SetTheme(ThemeName)
end)
end
RefreshThemeLabels()
RefreshNavigationTheme()
GlowButton.Activated:Connect(function()
SetGlow(not GlowEnabled)
RefreshThemeLabels()
end)
GlassButton.Activated:Connect(function()
GlassEnabled = not GlassEnabled
Main.BackgroundTransparency = GlassEnabled and 0.12 or 0
Sidebar.BackgroundTransparency = GlassEnabled and 0.08 or 0
RefreshThemeLabels()
end)
local InfoScroll = CreateScrollPage(InfoPage, "\073\110\102\111\114\109\097\116\105\111\110\083\099\114\111\108\108")

local function RefreshGameInformation()
if GameName and GameName.Parent then
GameName.Text = game.Name
end
if GameDetails and GameDetails.Parent then
GameDetails.Text = "\080\108\097\099\101\032\073\068\058\032" .. tostring(game.PlaceId)
.. "\032\032\032\124\032\032\032\080\108\097\121\101\114\115\058\032" .. tostring(#Players:GetPlayers())
.. "\032\032\032\124\032\032\032\067\114\101\097\116\111\114\058\032" .. tostring(game.CreatorId)
end
if ServerDetails and ServerDetails.Parent then
ServerDetails.Text = "\074\111\098\032\073\068\058\032"
.. (game.JobId ~= "" and string.sub(game.JobId, 1, 18) .. "\046\046\046" or "\083\116\117\100\105\111\032\047\032\108\111\099\097\108\032\115\101\115\115\105\111\110")
end
end
PageRefreshers.Misc = function()
for Index, Button in pairs(ToggleButtons) do
if Button and Button.Parent then
Button:SetAttribute("\069\110\097\098\108\101\100", FeatureStates[Index] == true)
Button.Image = FeatureStates[Index] and CONFIG.ON_IMAGE or CONFIG.OFF_IMAGE
end
local Status = ToggleStatusLabels[Index]
if Status then
Status.Text = FeatureStates[Index] and "\079\078" or "\079\070\070"
Status.TextColor3 = FeatureStates[Index] and Theme.Accent or Theme.Muted
end
local ValueBox = FeatureValueBoxes[Index]
local ValueSpec = FeatureValueSpecs[Index]
if ValueBox and ValueSpec and not ValueBox:IsFocused() then
ValueBox.Text = FormatFeatureValue(ValueSpec.Get())
end
end
end
PageRefreshers.Hub = function()
if RenderHub then RenderHub() end
end
PageRefreshers.Scripts = function()
if RenderMyScripts then
RenderMyScripts()
end
end
PageRefreshers.Settings = function()
RefreshThemeLabels()
UpdateScaleLabel()
end
PageRefreshers.Info = RefreshGameInformation

while ScreenGui.Parent do
task.wait(1.5)
if not IsMinimized and ActivePageName == "\073\110\102\111" then
RefreshGameInformation()
elseif not IsMinimized and ActivePageName == "\077\105\115\099" then
PageRefreshers.Misc()
elseif not IsMinimized and ActivePageName == "\083\101\116\116\105\110\103\115" then
RefreshThemeLabels()
end
end
end)

local function UpdateIntroScale()
local Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
if not Camera then return end
local Viewport = Camera.ViewportSize
local ScaleX = (Viewport.X - 24) / 680
local ScaleY = (Viewport.Y - 24) / 455
IntroScale.Scale = math.clamp(math.min(ScaleX, ScaleY, 1), 0.45, 1)
end
UpdateIntroScale()
TrackConnection(Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("\067\117\114\114\101\110\116\067\097\109\101\114\097"):Connect(UpdateIntroScale))
if Workspace.CurrentCamera then
TrackConnection(Workspace.CurrentCamera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("\086\105\101\119\112\111\114\116\083\105\122\101"):Connect(UpdateIntroScale))
end

task.spawn(function()
task.wait(0.12)
if not LoadingOverlay.Parent then return end
ProgressTween:Play()
ProgressTween.Completed:Wait()
task.wait(0.2)
if LoadingOverlay.Parent then
CreateTween(IntroGroup, 0.28, {
GroupTransparency = 1,
Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.47),
})
CreateTween(LoadingOverlay, 0.28, {
BackgroundTransparency = 1,
})
task.wait(0.3)
end
if ProgressChangedConnection then
ProgressChangedConnection:Disconnect()
end
ProgressValue:Destroy()
if LoadingOverlay.Parent then
LoadingOverlay:Destroy()
end
if Root and Root.Parent then
Root.Visible = true
local TargetScale = RootScale.Scale
RootScale.Scale = TargetScale * 0.965
Root.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.515)
CreateTween(RootScale, 0.32, { Scale = TargetScale }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
CreateTween(Root, 0.32, { Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
end
end)
task.delay(5, function()
if LoadingOverlay and LoadingOverlay.Parent then
LoadingOverlay:Destroy()
end
if Root and Root.Parent then
Root.Visible = true
end
end)
ScreenGui:SetAttribute("\076\111\097\100\101\100", true)
ScreenGui:SetAttribute("\086\101\114\115\105\111\110", CONFIG.VERSION)
